/src/controllers/healthCheckController.ts
import type { RequestHandler } from 'express';
import type { Container } from '../container.js';
import { asyncHandler } from '../http/errors.js';
import { k8sStatus } from '../utils/k8s-errors.js';

function failureDetails(error: unknown) {
  const cause = error instanceof Error && error.cause ? error.cause : error;
  const httpStatus = k8sStatus(cause);
  const message = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : '';
  const reason =
    httpStatus === 401
      ? 'authentication_failed'
      : httpStatus === 403
        ? 'permission_denied'
        : /certificate|ssl|tls/i.test(message)
          ? 'certificate_error'
          : /timeout|timed out|abort/i.test(message)
            ? 'timeout'
            : /ENOTFOUND|ECONNREFUSED|EHOSTUNREACH|fetch failed|connect/i.test(message)
              ? 'connection_failed'
              : 'dependency_check_failed';
  return { reason, ...(httpStatus === undefined ? {} : { httpStatus }) };
}

export function healthCheckController(container: Container) {
  return {
    health: (_req, res) => {
      res.send('OK');
    },
    ready: asyncHandler(async (_req, res) => {
      const checks: Record<string, string> = {};
      const failed = (dependency: string) => (error: unknown) => {
        checks[dependency] = 'unavailable';
        container.logger.error('readiness.check_failed', {
          dependency,
          requestId: res.locals.requestId as string | undefined,
          ...failureDetails(error),
        });
      };
      await Promise.all([
        container.kube.ping().then(() => (checks.kubernetes = 'ok'), failed('kubernetes')),
        container.metrics.ping().then(() => (checks.postgres = 'ok'), failed('postgres')),
        // only when build/deploy projects live on a different cluster
        ...(container.deployKube !== container.kube
          ? [container.deployKube.ping().then(() => (checks.deployCluster = 'ok'), failed('deployCluster'))]
          : []),
      ]);
      const ready = Object.values(checks).every((v) => v === 'ok');
      res.status(ready ? 200 : 503).json({ status: ready ? 'ready' : 'not-ready', checks });
    }),
  } satisfies Record<string, RequestHandler>;
}

/src/controllers/logController.ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { queryLogs } from '../config/daily-logs.js';
import { formatPretty } from '../utils/log-format.js';
import { asyncHandler } from '../http/errors.js';
import { parse } from '../http/schemas.js';

const query = z.strictObject({
  days: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(31).default(1),
  limit: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(1000).default(200),
  level: z.enum(['debug', 'info', 'warn', 'error']).optional(),
  search: z.string().max(200).optional(),
  requestId: z.string().max(64).optional(),
  soeid: z.string().max(64).optional(),
  format: z.enum(['json', 'text']).default('json'),
});
export function logController() {
  return {
    list: asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const options = parse(query, req.query);
      const result = await queryLogs(options);
      if (options.format === 'text') {
        res.type('text').send(
          [
            `Application logs | ${result.from} to ${result.to} | ${result.entries.length} entries | truncated: ${result.truncated}`,
            ...result.entries.map(({ time, level, event, timestamp: _timestamp, ...fields }) =>
              formatPretty(
                {
                  time: new Date(String(time)),
                  kind: level === 'error' || level === 'warn' || level === 'debug' ? level : 'info',
                  event: String(event),
                  fields,
                },
                false,
              ),
            ),
          ].join('\n'),
        );
      } else res.json(result);
    }),
  };
}

/src/controllers/patController.ts
import type { RequestHandler } from 'express';
import { param } from '../http/helpers.js';
import type { Container } from '../container.js';
import { getSoeid } from '../http/auth.js';
import { asyncHandler } from '../http/errors.js';
import { csiidQuerySchema, parse, patBodySchema } from '../http/schemas.js';
import { parseCsiid, parseTokenName } from '../utils/names.js';

export function patController(container: Container) {
  return {
    list: asyncHandler(async (_req, res) => {
      res.json(await container.pats.list(getSoeid(res)));
    }),
    view: asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const { csiid } = parse(csiidQuerySchema, req.query);
      res.json(
        await container.pats.view(getSoeid(res), parseCsiid(csiid), parseTokenName(param(req, 'tokenName'))),
      );
    }),
    create: asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const body = parse(patBodySchema, req.body);
      const created = await container.pats.create(
        getSoeid(res),
        parseCsiid(body.csiid),
        parseTokenName(param(req, 'tokenName')),
        body.token,
      );
      res.status(201).json(created);
    }),
    update: asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const body = parse(patBodySchema, req.body);
      res.json(
        await container.pats.update(
          getSoeid(res),
          parseCsiid(body.csiid),
          parseTokenName(param(req, 'tokenName')),
          body.token,
        ),
      );
    }),
    remove: asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const { csiid } = parse(csiidQuerySchema, req.query);
      const tokenName = parseTokenName(param(req, 'tokenName'));
      const removed = await container.pats.delete(getSoeid(res), parseCsiid(csiid), tokenName);
      res.json({
        message: removed ? `PAT '${tokenName}' deleted.` : `PAT '${tokenName}' not found; nothing to delete.`,
      });
    }),
  } satisfies Record<string, RequestHandler>;
}

/src/controllers/supportController.ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { Container } from '../container.js';
import { asyncHandler } from '../http/errors.js';

const resource = z
  .object({
    kind: z.enum(['Role', 'RoleBinding', 'ResourceQuota', 'LimitRange', 'ClusterRole', 'ClusterRoleBinding']),

      .object({

          .string()

/src/controllers/workspaceController.ts
import type { RequestHandler } from 'express';
import { optionalCsiid, param } from '../http/helpers.js';
import type { Container } from '../container.js';
import { getSoeid } from '../http/auth.js';
import { asyncHandler } from '../http/errors.js';
import { createWorkspaceSchema, parse } from '../http/schemas.js';
import { parseCsiid } from '../utils/names.js';

export function workspaceController(container: Container) {
  return {
    list: asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const csiid = optionalCsiid(req);
      const workspaces = await container.workspaces.list(getSoeid(res));
      res.json(csiid ? workspaces.filter((workspace) => workspace.csiid === csiid) : workspaces);
    }),
    get: asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      res.json(await container.workspaces.get(getSoeid(res), param(req, 'id')));
    }),
    create: asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const body = parse(createWorkspaceSchema, req.body);
      const workspace = await container.workspaces.create({
        soeid: getSoeid(res),
        csiid: parseCsiid(body.csiid),
        devworkspaceValue: body.devworkspaceValue,
        editor: body.editor,
        stackVersion: body.stackVersion,
        repositoryUrl: body.repositoryUrl,
        repositoryBranch: body.repositoryBranch,
      });
      res.status(200).json(workspace);
    }),
    remove: asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const id = param(req, 'id');
      await container.workspaces.delete(getSoeid(res), id);
      res.json({ message: `Workspace with id ${id} is terminating.` });
    }),
    start: asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const id = param(req, 'id');
      await container.workspaces.start(getSoeid(res), id);
      res.json({ message: `Workspace with id ${id} is starting.` });
    }),
    stop: asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const id = param(req, 'id');
      await container.workspaces.stop(getSoeid(res), id);
      res.json({ message: `Workspace with id ${id} is stopping.` });
    }),
  } satisfies Record<string, RequestHandler>;
}

/src/factories/devfile-updater.ts
import { load } from 'js-yaml';
import type { Devfile, EditorKey } from '../types.js';
import { BadRequestError } from '../utils/errors.js';

interface EditorProfile {
  serverComponentName: string;
  serverPath: string;
  partOf: string;
  targetPort: number;
}

/** JetBrains editors need their server wired into the stack's `tools` container; VS Code needs nothing. */
const PROFILES: Record<EditorKey, EditorProfile | null> = {
  vscode: null,
  intellij: {
    serverComponentName: 'che-idea-server',
    serverPath: '/che-idea-server',
    partOf: 'che-idea-server.eclipse.org',
    targetPort: 2222,
  },
  goland: {
    serverComponentName: 'che-goland-server',
    serverPath: '/che-goland-server',
    partOf: 'che-goland-server.eclipse.org',
    targetPort: 3400,
  },
};

export const DEFAULT_HOME_VOLUME_SIZE = '10Gi';

export function parseDevfile(yamlText: string): Devfile {
  let parsed: unknown;
  try {
    parsed = load(yamlText);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`Devfile is not valid YAML: ${(error as Error).message}`, 'INVALID_DEVFILE');
  }
  if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Devfile must be a YAML mapping', 'INVALID_DEVFILE');
  }
  const devfile = parsed as Devfile;
  if (!Array.isArray(devfile.components)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Devfile has no components', 'INVALID_DEVFILE');
  }
  return devfile;
}

/** Returns a modified deep copy; the input is never mutated. */
export function applyEditorProfile(
  devfile: Devfile,
  editor: EditorKey,
  homeVolumeSize = DEFAULT_HOME_VOLUME_SIZE,
): Devfile {
  const updated = structuredClone(devfile);
  const profile = PROFILES[editor];
  if (!profile) return updated;

  delete updated.commands;
  delete updated.events;

  const components = updated.components ?? [];
  const tools = components.find((c) => c.name === 'tools');
  if (tools?.container) {
    if (tools.container.volumeMounts) {
      tools.container.volumeMounts = tools.container.volumeMounts.filter((vm) => vm.name !== 'venv');
      const editorMount = tools.container.volumeMounts.find((vm) => vm.name === 'checode');
      if (editorMount) {
        editorMount.name = profile.serverComponentName;
        editorMount.path = profile.serverPath;
      }
    }
    delete tools.container.env;
    tools.container.endpoints = [
      {
        attributes: {
          cookiesAuthEnabled: true,
          discoverable: false,
          type: 'main',
          urlRewriteSupported: true,
        },
        exposure: 'public',
        name: profile.serverComponentName,
        protocol: 'https',
        secure: true,
        targetPort: profile.targetPort,
      },
    ];
    tools.attributes = { ...tools.attributes, 'app.kubernetes.io/part-of': profile.partOf };
  }

  updated.components = components.filter((c) => !(c.name === 'venv' && c.volume !== undefined));

  const editorVolume = updated.components.find((c) => c.name === 'checode' && c.volume !== undefined);
  if (editorVolume) editorVolume.name = profile.serverComponentName;

  const home = updated.components.find((c) => c.name === 'home' && c.volume !== undefined);
  if (home?.volume) home.volume.size = homeVolumeSize;

  return updated;
}

/src/factories/log-factory.ts
import type { MetricsRecord } from '../types.js';

export type MetricsInput = Pick<MetricsRecord, 'soeid' | 'eventtype' | 'resourcetype'> &
  Partial<MetricsRecord>;

/** Default values for a metrics row. */
export function buildLogRecord(data: MetricsInput, now: Date = new Date()): MetricsRecord {
  return {
    timestamp: data.timestamp ?? now,
    csiid: data.csiid ?? '',
    soeid: data.soeid,
    eventtype: data.eventtype,
    resourcename: data.resourcename ?? '',
    resourcetype: data.resourcetype,
    status: data.status ?? { failed: false, error: '', message: '' },
    useraction: data.useraction ?? 'user',
    limitcpu: data.limitcpu ?? 0,
    limitmemory: data.limitmemory ?? 0,
    requestcpu: data.requestcpu ?? 0,
    requestmemory: data.requestmemory ?? 0,
    duration: data.duration ?? '0 seconds',
    storage: data.storage ?? 0,
    workspaceversion: data.workspaceversion ?? '',
    editorname: data.editorname ?? '',
  };
}

/src/factories/workspace-factory.ts
import type { Devfile, DevWorkspaceResource, EditorKey } from '../types.js';

export const WORKSPACE_LABELS = {
  csiid: 'devspaces.citi.com/csiid',
  stack: 'devspaces.citi.com/stack',
  managedBy: 'app.kubernetes.io/managed-by',
} as const;

export interface BuildWorkspaceArgs {
  name: string;
  namespace: string;
  csiid: string;
  stack: string;
  stackVersion: string;
  devfile: Devfile;
  editor: EditorKey;
  /** Full editor id as the Dev Spaces dashboard knows it, e.g. `che-incubator/che-code/latest`. */
  editorId: string;
  devSpacesServiceFqdn: string;
  repositoryUrl?: string;
  repositoryBranch?: string;
}

const EDITOR_DISPLAY: Record<EditorKey, string> = {
  vscode: 'Visual Studio Code',
  intellij: 'IntelliJ IDEA',
  goland: 'GoLand',
};

function repoName(url: string): string {
  const last = url
    .replace(/\.git$/, '')
    .split('/')
    .filter(Boolean)
    .pop();
  return (last ?? 'project').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, '-');
}

export function buildDevWorkspaceObject(a: BuildWorkspaceArgs): DevWorkspaceResource {
  const projects = [...((a.devfile.projects as unknown[] | undefined) ?? [])];
  if (a.repositoryUrl) {
    projects.push({
      name: repoName(a.repositoryUrl),
      git: {
        remotes: { origin: a.repositoryUrl },
        ...(a.repositoryBranch ? { checkoutFrom: { revision: a.repositoryBranch } } : {}),
      },
    });
  }

  const template: NonNullable<NonNullable<DevWorkspaceResource['spec']>['template']> = {
    attributes: {
      'controller.devfile.io/devworkspace-config': {
        name: 'devworkspace-config',
        namespace: 'openshift-devspaces',
      },
      'controller.devfile.io/scc': 'container-build',
      'controller.devfile.io/storage-type': 'per-workspace',
    },
    components: a.devfile.components ?? [],
  };
  if (projects.length > 0) template.projects = projects;
  if (Array.isArray(a.devfile.commands)) template.commands = a.devfile.commands;
  if (a.devfile.events) template.events = a.devfile.events;

  return {
    apiVersion: 'workspace.devfile.io/v1alpha2',
    kind: 'DevWorkspace',
    metadata: {
      name: a.name,
      namespace: a.namespace,
      annotations: {
        dw: a.name,
        workspaceVersion: a.stackVersion,
        workspaceDisplayName: a.devfile.metadata?.displayName ?? a.devfile.metadata?.name ?? a.stack,
        workspaceDescription: a.devfile.metadata?.description ?? '',
        editorDisplayName: EDITOR_DISPLAY[a.editor],
      },
      labels: {
        [WORKSPACE_LABELS.csiid]: a.csiid,
        [WORKSPACE_LABELS.stack]: a.stack,
        [WORKSPACE_LABELS.managedBy]: 'workspace-mgr',
      },
    },
    spec: {
      started: true,
      routingClass: 'che',
      template,
      contributions: [
        {
          name: 'ide',
          uri: `${a.devSpacesServiceFqdn}/dashboard/api/editors/devfile?che-editor=${a.editorId}`,
        },
      ],
    },
  };
}

/src/http/auth.ts
import { createHash, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
import type { NextFunction, Request, RequestHandler, Response } from 'express';
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import type { AppConfig } from '../config/env.js';
import type { Principal } from '../types.js';
import { ForbiddenError, UnauthorizedError } from '../utils/errors.js';
import { parseSoeid } from '../utils/names.js';

const sha256 = (value: string): Buffer => createHash('sha256').update(value).digest();

/** Constant-time string comparison (hashing equalises lengths). */
export function safeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  return timingSafeEqual(sha256(a), sha256(b));
}

export function checkAdminCredentials(auth: AppConfig['auth'], username: string, password: string): boolean {
  // Evaluate both so timing does not reveal which one was wrong.
  const userOk = safeEqual(username, auth.adminUsername);
  const passOk = safeEqual(password, auth.adminPassword);
  return userOk && passOk;
}

export function signToken(auth: AppConfig['auth'], principal: Principal): string {
  return jwt.sign({ role: principal.role }, auth.secretKey, {
    algorithm: 'HS256',
    subject: principal.soeid,
    expiresIn: auth.expiresIn as jwt.SignOptions['expiresIn'],
  });
}

export function getPrincipal(res: Response): Principal {
  const principal = res.locals.principal as Principal | undefined;
  if (!principal) throw new UnauthorizedError();
  return principal;
}

/** `Authorization: Bearer <jwt>` → `res.locals.principal`. */
export function authenticate(auth: AppConfig['auth']): RequestHandler {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    const match = /^Bearer (\S+)$/.exec(req.headers.authorization ?? '');
    if (!match?.[1]) return next(new UnauthorizedError('A bearer token is required for authentication'));
    try {
      const decoded = jwt.verify(match[1], auth.secretKey, { algorithms: ['HS256'] });
      if (typeof decoded === 'string' || typeof decoded.sub !== 'string') throw new Error('malformed claims');
      const role = decoded.role === 'admin' ? 'admin' : 'user';
      res.locals.principal = { soeid: decoded.sub, role } satisfies Principal;
      return next();
    } catch {
      return next(new UnauthorizedError('Invalid token'));
    }
  };
}

/** Validates `:soeid` and makes sure the caller may act for that user (admins may act for anyone). */
export function authorizeSoeid(): RequestHandler {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    try {
      const soeid = parseSoeid(req.params.soeid ?? '');
      const principal = getPrincipal(res);
      if (principal.role !== 'admin' && principal.soeid.toLowerCase() !== soeid) {
        throw new ForbiddenError('You may only access your own resources');
      }
      res.locals.soeid = soeid;
      next();
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  };
}

export const getSoeid = (res: Response): string => res.locals.soeid as string;

/src/http/errors.ts
import type { ErrorRequestHandler, NextFunction, Request, RequestHandler, Response } from 'express';
import { AppError, errorMessage } from '../utils/errors.js';
import type { Logger } from '../utils/logger.js';

export interface ErrorBody {
  error: string;
  message: string;
  code: string;
}

export const errorBody = (message: string, code: string): ErrorBody => ({ error: message, message, code });

/** Express 4 does not catch rejected promises; this forwards them to the error handler. */
export const asyncHandler =
  (fn: (req: Request, res: Response) => Promise<void>): RequestHandler =>
  (req, res, next: NextFunction) => {
    fn(req, res).catch(next);
  };

export const notFoundHandler: RequestHandler = (req, res) => {
  res.status(404).json(errorBody(`Route not found: ${req.method} ${req.path}`, 'ROUTE_NOT_FOUND'));
};

export function createErrorHandler(logger: Logger): ErrorRequestHandler {
  return (err: unknown, req, res, next) => {
    if (res.headersSent) return next(err);
    const requestId = res.locals.requestId as string | undefined;

    if (err instanceof AppError) {
      if (err.status >= 500) logger.error('request.failed', { requestId, code: err.code, error: err });
      res.status(err.status).json(errorBody(err.message, err.code));
      return;
    }
    // body-parser errors carry a status and a type
    const parserError = err as { status?: number; type?: string };
    if (parserError.type === 'entity.parse.failed') {
      res.status(400).json(errorBody('Request body is not valid JSON', 'INVALID_JSON'));
      return;
    }
    if (parserError.type === 'entity.too.large') {
      res.status(413).json(errorBody('Request body is too large', 'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE'));
      return;
    }
    // Never leak internals for unexpected errors.
    logger.error('request.crashed', {
      requestId,
      path: req.path,
      error: err instanceof Error ? err : errorMessage(err),
    });
    res.status(500).json(errorBody('Internal server error', 'INTERNAL_ERROR'));
  };
}

/src/http/helpers.ts
import type { Request, RequestHandler } from 'express';
import { getPrincipal } from '../http/auth.js';
import { ForbiddenError } from '../utils/errors.js';
import { parse } from '../http/schemas.js';
import { csiidQuerySchema } from '../http/schemas.js';
import { parseCsiid } from '../utils/names.js';

export const param = (req: Request, name: string): string => req.params[name] ?? '';

export const optionalCsiid = (req: Request): string | undefined =>
  req.query.csiid === undefined ? undefined : parseCsiid(parse(csiidQuerySchema, req.query).csiid);

export const requireAdmin: RequestHandler = (_req, res, next) => {
  try {
    if (getPrincipal(res).role !== 'admin') throw new ForbiddenError('Administrator access required');
    next();
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
};

/src/http/schemas.ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { BadRequestError } from '../utils/errors.js';

const identifier = /^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$/;

const csiid = z
  .union([z.string(), z.number()])
  .transform((v) => String(v))
  .pipe(z.string().regex(/^\d{1,10}$/, 'csiid must be numeric'));

export const loginSchema = z.strictObject({ username: z.string().min(1), password: z.string().min(1) });

export const createWorkspaceSchema = z.strictObject({
  devworkspaceValue: z.string().min(1).max(100).regex(identifier, 'invalid stack name'),
  csiid,
  editor: z.enum(['vscode', 'intellij', 'goland']).default('vscode'),
  stackVersion: z.string().max(50).regex(identifier, 'invalid stack version').optional(),
  repositoryUrl: z
    .url({ protocol: /^https$/ })
    .max(500)
    .optional(),
  repositoryBranch: z
    .string()
    .max(200)
    .regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9._/-]+$/, 'invalid branch name')
    .optional(),
});

export const csiidQuerySchema = z.object({ csiid });
export const csiidBodySchema = z.strictObject({ csiid });
export const patBodySchema = z.strictObject({ csiid, token: z.string().min(1).max(200) });
export const gitConfigBodySchema = z.strictObject({ csiid, content: z.string().min(1) });

export function parse<T extends z.ZodType>(schema: T, data: unknown): z.output<T> {
  const result = schema.safeParse(data ?? {});
  if (!result.success) {
    const message = result.error.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join('.') || 'body'}: ${i.message}`).join('; ');
    throw new BadRequestError(message, 'VALIDATION_ERROR');
  }
  return result.data;
}

/src/index.ts
import type { Server } from 'node:http';
import { loadConfig, startupSummary } from './config/env.js';
import { buildContainer, createProductionDependencies } from './container.js';
import { createApp } from './app.js';
import { createLogger } from './utils/logger.js';
import { errorMessage } from './utils/errors.js';
import { flushDailyLogs } from './config/daily-logs.js';

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  // Optional CLI argument: node dist/index.js <kubeconfig>
  const config = loadConfig(
    process.argv[2] ? { ...process.env, KUBECONFIG_PATH: process.argv[2] } : process.env,
  );
  const logger = createLogger(
    config.logLevel,
    undefined,
    {},
    { format: config.logFormat, color: config.logColor, dailyFiles: true },
  );
  if (!config.cluster.tlsRejectUnauthorized) {
    logger.warn('cluster.tls_verification_disabled', {
      reason: 'NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED=0',
      risk: 'the clusters, the devfile registry and other HTTPS peers are not verified',
    });
  }
  const dependencies = createProductionDependencies(config, logger);
  const container = buildContainer(config, logger, dependencies, (next) => {
    // Runtime settings do not change PostgreSQL; retain its existing pool.
    const adapters = createProductionDependencies(
      { ...next, postgres: { ...next.postgres, enabled: false } },
      logger,
    );
    return { ...adapters, metrics: dependencies.metrics };
  });
  const server: Server = createApp(container).listen(config.port, () =>
    logger.success('server.listening', startupSummary(config)),
  );

  let shuttingDown = false;
  const shutdown = (signal: string): void => {
    if (shuttingDown) return;
    shuttingDown = true;
    logger.info('server.shutdown', { signal });
    server.close(() => {
      container.metrics.close().finally(async () => {
        await flushDailyLogs();
        process.exit(0);
      });
    });
    setTimeout(() => process.exit(1), 10_000).unref();
  };
  process.on('SIGTERM', () => shutdown('SIGTERM'));
  process.on('SIGINT', () => shutdown('SIGINT'));
}

main().catch((error: unknown) => {
  process.stderr.write(`Fatal: ${errorMessage(error)}\n`);
  process.exit(1);
});

/src/infra/db/MetricsRepository.ts
import pg from 'pg';
import type { AppConfig } from '../../config/env.js';
import type { MetricsRecord } from '../../types.js';
import { errorMessage } from '../../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../../utils/logger.js';
import type { MetricsStore } from '../ports.js';
import { buildSslOptions } from './pg-ssl.js';

/** The subset of `pg.Pool` the repository needs (also satisfied by pg-mem's pool in tests). */
export interface Queryable {
  query(text: string, values?: unknown[]): Promise<unknown>;
  end(): Promise<void>;
}

const TABLE_RE = /^("[\w-]+"|[A-Za-z_]\w*)(\.("[\w-]+"|[A-Za-z_]\w*))?$/;

export function assertSafeTableName(table: string): string {
  if (!TABLE_RE.test(table)) throw new Error(`Unsafe metrics table name: ${table}`);
  return table;
}

/** `metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"` → `{ schema: 'metrics', name: 'devspaces-metrics-dev' }`. */
export function parseTableName(table: string): { schema?: string; name: string } {
  const unq = (s: string): string => s.replace(/^"|"$/g, '');
  const parts = assertSafeTableName(table).match(/"[\w-]+"|[A-Za-z_]\w*/g) ?? [];
  const name = unq(parts[parts.length - 1] ?? '');
  return parts.length > 1 ? { schema: unq(parts[0] ?? ''), name } : { name };
}

export function createPgPool(config: AppConfig['postgres'], logger: Logger = silentLogger): pg.Pool {
  const pool = new pg.Pool({
    host: config.host,
    port: config.port,
    database: config.database,
    user: config.user,
    password: config.password,
    ssl: buildSslOptions(config),
    max: 5,
    connectionTimeoutMillis: 3000,
    idleTimeoutMillis: 30_000,
    statement_timeout: 5000,
  });
  // An idle client dropped by the server emits 'error'; unhandled, it would crash the process.
  pool.on('error', (error) => logger.error('postgres.pool_error', { error: errorMessage(error) }));
  return pool;
}

/** Database timestamps use UTC wall-clock text: `2026-01-02 03:04:05.678`. */
export const formatUtc = (date: Date): string => date.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').replace('Z', '');

/** Every value the service can store, keyed by column name. */
export function candidateColumns(r: MetricsRecord): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    timestamp: formatUtc(r.timestamp),
    csiid: r.csiid,
    soeid: r.soeid,
    eventtype: r.eventtype,
    resourcename: r.resourcename,
    resourcetype: r.resourcetype,
    status: JSON.stringify(r.status),
    limitcpu: r.limitcpu,
    limitmemory: r.limitmemory,
    requestcpu: r.requestcpu,
    requestmemory: r.requestmemory,
    duration: r.duration,
    storage: r.storage,
    useraction: r.useraction,
    workspaceversion: r.workspaceversion,
    editorname: r.editorname,
    errordetail: r.status.failed ? r.status.error : null,
  };
}

/**
 * Writes metrics rows into an EXISTING table. It never issues DDL (no CREATE/ALTER/DROP): it reads the table's
 * column list once and inserts only the columns that exist, so a table that predates some of our fields still works.
 */
export class MetricsRepository implements MetricsStore {
  private readonly table: string;
  private columns?: Promise<Set<string> | undefined>;
  private warnedMissing = false;

  constructor(
    private readonly db: Queryable,
    table = 'metrics."devspaces-metrics"',
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
  ) {
    this.table = assertSafeTableName(table);
  }

  private knownColumns(): Promise<Set<string> | undefined> {
    this.columns ??= this.discoverColumns();
    return this.columns;
  }

  /**
   * Read-only column discovery, two independent ways: the catalog (works with INSERT-only privileges),
   * then an empty SELECT whose result metadata lists the columns (works when the catalog is hidden).
   */
  private async discoverColumns(): Promise<Set<string> | undefined> {
    const errors: string[] = [];

    try {
      const { schema, name } = parseTableName(this.table);
      const result = (await this.db.query(
        schema
          ? 'SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = $1 AND table_name = $2'
          : 'SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = current_schema() AND table_name = $1',
        schema ? [schema, name] : [name],
      )) as { rows?: { column_name: string }[] };
      const names = (result.rows ?? []).map((row) => row.column_name.toLowerCase());
      if (names.length > 0) return new Set(names);
    } catch (error) {
      errors.push(`information_schema: ${errorMessage(error)}`);
    }

    try {
      const result = (await this.db.query(`SELECT * FROM ${this.table} LIMIT 0`)) as {
        fields?: { name: string }[];
      };
      const names = (result.fields ?? []).map((field) => field.name.toLowerCase());
      if (names.length > 0) return new Set(names);
    } catch (error) {
      errors.push(`select: ${errorMessage(error)}`);
      // Do not cache a failure: try again on the next insert.
      this.columns = undefined;
    }

    this.logger.warn('metrics.columns_unknown', {
      table: this.table,
      reason: 'could not read the table columns; inserting all fields',
      errors: errors.join(' | '),
    });
    return undefined;
  }

  async insert(record: MetricsRecord): Promise<void> {
    const all = candidateColumns(record);
    const known = await this.knownColumns();
    const names = known ? Object.keys(all).filter((column) => known.has(column)) : Object.keys(all);

    if (known && !this.warnedMissing) {
      const missing = Object.keys(all).filter((column) => !known.has(column));
      if (missing.length > 0) {
        this.warnedMissing = true;
        this.logger.warn('metrics.columns_missing', { table: this.table, missing: missing.join(',') });
      }
    }
    if (names.length === 0) throw new Error(`Table ${this.table} has none of the expected metrics columns`);

    const columns = names.map((column) => `"${column}"`).join(', ');
    const placeholders = names.map((_, i) => `$${i + 1}`).join(', ');
    await this.db.query(
      `INSERT INTO ${this.table} (${columns}) VALUES (${placeholders})`,
      names.map((column) => all[column]),
    );
  }

  async ping(): Promise<void> {
    await this.db.query('SELECT 1');
  }

  close(): Promise<void> {
    return this.db.end();
  }
}

/** Used when POSTGRES_ENABLED=false (local runs); drops records. */
export class NoopMetricsStore implements MetricsStore {
  async insert(): Promise<void> {}
  async ping(): Promise<void> {}
  async close(): Promise<void> {}
}

/src/infra/db/pg-ssl.ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import type { ConnectionOptions } from 'node:tls';
import type { AppConfig } from '../../config/env.js';

type PgConfig = AppConfig['postgres'];

/** CA bundle names by environment. */
const CERT_FILES: Record<string, string> = {
  development: 'dev.pem',
  uat: 'uat.pem',
  production: 'prod.pem',
};

/** Default certificate directory in the container. */
const CONTAINER_CERT_DIR = '/app/backend/cert';

/**
 * Which CA bundle to trust: an explicit POSTGRES_CA_FILE, else `<certDir>/<dev|uat|prod>.pem` chosen by NODE_ENV
 * (unknown environments fall back to dev.pem). `certDir` defaults to `./certs` in development and
 * `/app/backend/cert` otherwise.
 */
export function resolveCaPath(
  config: Pick<PgConfig, 'env' | 'caFile' | 'certDir'>,
  cwd = process.cwd(),
): string {
  if (config.caFile) return config.caFile;
  const file = CERT_FILES[config.env] ?? 'dev.pem';
  const dir =
    config.certDir ?? (config.env === 'development' ? path.resolve(cwd, 'certs') : CONTAINER_CERT_DIR);
  return path.join(dir, file);
}

/**
 * TLS options for the existing Postgres server. The server certificate is always verified against the CA;
 * only in `development` is the hostname check skipped (the dev instance is reached through a different name).
 */
export function buildSslOptions(
  config: Pick<PgConfig, 'ssl' | 'env' | 'caFile' | 'certDir'>,
  readFile: (file: string) => string = (file) => readFileSync(file, 'utf8'),
): false | ConnectionOptions {
  if (!config.ssl) return false;
  const caPath = resolveCaPath(config);
  let ca: string;
  try {
    ca = readFile(caPath);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error(
      `Cannot read the Postgres CA certificate at ${caPath} (NODE_ENV=${config.env}): ${(error as Error).message}. ` +
        'Put the CA there, or set POSTGRES_CA_FILE / POSTGRES_CERT_DIR.',
      { cause: error },
    );
  }
  return config.env === 'development'
    ? { rejectUnauthorized: true, checkServerIdentity: () => undefined, ca }
    : { rejectUnauthorized: true, ca };
}

/src/infra/devreg/DevfileRegistryClient.ts
import axios, { type AxiosInstance } from 'axios';
import type { CatalogEntry } from '../../types.js';
import { UpstreamError, WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError } from '../../utils/errors.js';
import { describeError } from '../../utils/k8s-errors.js';
import type { DevfileRegistry } from '../ports.js';

export function createRegistryHttp(baseURL: string): AxiosInstance {
  return axios.create({ baseURL, timeout: 10_000, headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } });
}

export class DevfileRegistryClient implements DevfileRegistry {
  constructor(private readonly http: AxiosInstance) {}

  async getCatalog(): Promise<CatalogEntry[]> {
    try {
      const res = await this.http.get<unknown>('v2index/all');
      if (!Array.isArray(res.data)) throw new Error('registry index is not a list');
      return res.data as CatalogEntry[];
    } catch (error) {
      throw new UpstreamError(describeError('Devfile registry catalog', error), { cause: error });
    }
  }

  async getDevfile(stack: string, version?: string): Promise<{ yaml: string; version: string }> {
    const catalog = await this.getCatalog();
    const entry = catalog.find((e) => e.name === stack);
    if (!entry) throw new WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError(`No such workspace config: ${stack}`);

    const versions = entry.versions ?? [];
    const chosen = version
      ? versions.find((v) => v.version === version)
      : (versions.find((v) => v.default) ?? versions[0]);
    if (!chosen) {
      throw new WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError(
        version ? `No version '${version}' for workspace config: ${stack}` : `No versions for ${stack}`,
      );
    }

    try {
      const res = await this.http.get<string>(
        `devfiles/${encodeURIComponent(stack)}/${encodeURIComponent(chosen.version)}`,
        { responseType: 'text', transformResponse: (d: unknown) => d, headers: { Accept: 'text/plain' } },
      );
      return { yaml: String(res.data), version: chosen.version };
    } catch (error) {
      throw new UpstreamError(describeError(`Devfile '${stack}'`, error), { cause: error });
    }
  }
}

/src/infra/email/EmailNotifier.ts
import type { AppConfig } from '../../config/env.js';
import { errorMessage } from '../../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../../utils/logger.js';
import type {
  NamespaceCreatedEmail,
  NamespaceExistsEmail,
  NamespacesDeletedEmail,
  Notifier,
  UserDirectory,
} from '../ports.js';
import {
  clusterLinks,
  deletedNamespacesEmailBody,
  existingNamespaceEmailBody,
  newNamespaceEmailBody,
} from './email-templates.js';

export type FetchFn = (url: string, init: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;

export class EmailNotifier implements Notifier {
  constructor(
    private readonly config: AppConfig['notification'],
    private readonly clusterApiUrl: string,
    private readonly directory: UserDirectory,
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
    private readonly fetchFn: FetchFn = (url, init) => fetch(url, init),
  ) {}

  async namespaceCreated(e: NamespaceCreatedEmail): Promise<void> {
    await this.send(e.soeid, 'Success! Your DevSpaces Deployment Project is Ready!', (firstName, recipient) =>
      newNamespaceEmailBody({
        firstName,
        soeid: e.soeid,
        namespace: e.namespace,
        cluster: clusterLinks(this.clusterApiUrl),
        recipient,
      }),
    );
  }

  async namespaceAlreadyExists(e: NamespaceExistsEmail): Promise<void> {
    await this.send(
      e.soeid,
      'Important: Your DevSpaces Deployment Project Already Exists',
      (firstName, recipient) =>
        existingNamespaceEmailBody({
          firstName,
          soeid: e.soeid,
          newNamespace: e.newNamespace,
          existingNamespace: e.existingNamespace,
          cluster: clusterLinks(this.clusterApiUrl),
          recipient,
        }),
    );
  }

  async namespacesDeleted(e: NamespacesDeletedEmail): Promise<void> {
    if (e.namespaces.length === 0) return;
    await this.send(
      e.soeid,
      'Confirmation: DevSpaces Deployment Project(s) Deleted',
      (firstName, recipient) =>
        deletedNamespacesEmailBody({ firstName, soeid: e.soeid, namespaces: e.namespaces, recipient }),
    );
  }

  private async send(
    soeid: string,
    subject: string,
    body: (firstName: string | undefined, recipient: string) => string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const { url, username, password, sender, cc, emailDomain } = this.config;
    if (!url) {
      this.logger.debug('email.skipped', { reason: 'NOTIFICATION_MAIL_URL not set', subject });
      return;
    }
    const recipient = `${soeid}@${emailDomain}`;
    try {
      const firstName = await this.directory.firstName(soeid);
      const response = await this.fetchFn(`${url.replace(/\/$/, '')}/send-mail/v2`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          Authorization: `Basic ${Buffer.from(`${username}:${password}`).toString('base64')}`,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          sender,
          receiver: recipient,
          receiverName: soeid.toUpperCase(),
          cc: cc ?? '',
          bcc: '',
          subject,
          emailMessage: body(firstName, recipient),
          isHtml: true,
        }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000),
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`mail service answered ${response.status}`);
      this.logger.success('email.sent', { soeid, subject });
    } catch (error) {
      this.logger.error('email.failed', { soeid, subject, error: errorMessage(error) });
    }
  }
}

export class NoopNotifier implements Notifier {
  async namespaceCreated(): Promise<void> {}
  async namespaceAlreadyExists(): Promise<void> {}
  async namespacesDeleted(): Promise<void> {}
}

/src/infra/email/GraphqlUserDirectory.ts
import { errorMessage } from '../../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../../utils/logger.js';
import type { UserDirectory } from '../ports.js';
import type { FetchFn } from './EmailNotifier.js';

const QUERY = 'query($id: String!) { users(filter: { soeId: { equals: $id } }) { firstName } }';

export class GraphqlUserDirectory implements UserDirectory {
  constructor(
    private readonly url: string,
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
    private readonly fetchFn: FetchFn = (url, init) => fetch(url, init),
  ) {}

  async firstName(soeid: string): Promise<string | undefined> {
    try {
      const response = await this.fetchFn(this.url, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ query: QUERY, variables: { id: soeid.toUpperCase() } }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5_000),
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`user directory answered ${response.status}`);
      const json = (await response.json()) as { data?: { users?: { firstName?: string }[] } };
      return json.data?.users?.[0]?.firstName || undefined;
    } catch (error) {
      this.logger.warn('userdirectory.failed', { soeid, error: errorMessage(error) });
      return undefined;
    }
  }
}

export class NullUserDirectory implements UserDirectory {
  async firstName(): Promise<string | undefined> {
    return undefined;
  }
}

/src/infra/email/email-templates.ts
export const escapeHtml = (value: string): string =>
  value
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');

export interface ClusterLinks {
  apiUrl: string;
  consoleUrl: string;
  tokenUrl: string;
}

/** `https://api.<domain>:6443` → console and OAuth token URLs on `apps.<domain>`. */
export function clusterLinks(apiUrl: string): ClusterLinks {
  try {
    const url = new URL(apiUrl);
    const apps = url.hostname.replace(/^api\./, 'apps.');
    return {
      apiUrl,
      consoleUrl: `https://console-openshift-console.${apps}/`,
      tokenUrl: `https://oauth-openshift.${apps}/oauth/token/request`,
    };
  } catch {
    return { apiUrl, consoleUrl: '', tokenUrl: '' };
  }
}

const STYLE = `body{margin:0;padding:0;background:#f4f6f9;font-family:'Segoe UI',Arial,sans-serif;color:#323130}
.wrapper{max-width:700px;margin:20px auto;background:#fff;border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden}
.header{background:#0f4c81;color:#fff;padding:24px;text-align:center}.header h1{margin:0;font-size:26px}
.content{padding:28px}code,pre{font-family:Consolas,'Courier New',monospace;background:#1e1e1e;color:#fff;padding:4px 8px;border-radius:4px}
.footer{background:#0f4c81;color:#fff;padding:16px;text-align:center;font-size:.9em}a{color:#0078d4}`;

function layout(title: string, heading: string, body: string, recipient: string): string {
  return `<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0"><title>${escapeHtml(title)}</title><style>${STYLE}</style></head><body><div class="wrapper"><div class="header"><h1>${escapeHtml(heading)}</h1></div><div class="content">${body}</div><div class="footer"><p>&copy; ${new Date().getFullYear()} DevSpaces Team.</p><p>This email was sent to ${escapeHtml(recipient)}.</p><p>Confidentiality Notice: this email and any attachments are confidential and intended solely for the addressee.</p></div></div></body></html>`;
}

const greeting = (firstName: string | undefined, soeid: string): string =>
  escapeHtml(firstName ?? `User ${soeid.toUpperCase()}`);

const links = (c: ClusterLinks): string =>
  `<ul><li>Cluster API endpoint: <a href="${escapeHtml(c.apiUrl)}">${escapeHtml(c.apiUrl)}</a></li>` +
  (c.consoleUrl
    ? `<li>OpenShift console: <a href="${escapeHtml(c.consoleUrl)}">${escapeHtml(c.consoleUrl)}</a></li>`
    : '') +
  (c.tokenUrl
    ? `<li>Generate a login token: <a href="${escapeHtml(c.tokenUrl)}">${escapeHtml(c.tokenUrl)}</a></li>`
    : '') +
  `</ul>`;

export function newNamespaceEmailBody(args: {
  firstName?: string;
  soeid: string;
  namespace: string;
  cluster: ClusterLinks;
  recipient: string;
}): string {
  return layout(
    'DevSpaces Deployment Project Ready',
    'DevSpaces Deployment Project Ready',
    `<p>Dear ${greeting(args.firstName, args.soeid)},</p>
<p>Your OpenShift deployment project <code>${escapeHtml(args.namespace)}</code> has been created and is ready for use.</p>
<h2>Quick start</h2>
<ol><li>Generate a login token and run the <code>oc login</code> command it gives you.</li>
<li>Select your project: <pre>oc project ${escapeHtml(args.namespace)}</pre></li>
<li>Build and deploy your application inside this project.</li></ol>
${links(args.cluster)}
<p>Newly provisioned resources may take several minutes to become fully available.</p>`,
    args.recipient,
  );
}

export function existingNamespaceEmailBody(args: {
  firstName?: string;
  soeid: string;
  newNamespace: string;
  existingNamespace: string;
  cluster: ClusterLinks;
  recipient: string;
}): string {
  return layout(
    'Important: Your DevSpaces Deployment Project Already Exists',
    'DevSpaces Notification',
    `<p>Dear ${greeting(args.firstName, args.soeid)},</p>
<p>You recently asked for a new deployment project (<code>${escapeHtml(args.newNamespace)}</code>), but you already have one,
so no new project was created.</p>
<h2>Connect to your existing deployment project</h2>
<pre>oc project ${escapeHtml(args.existingNamespace)}</pre>
${links(args.cluster)}`,
    args.recipient,
  );
}

export function deletedNamespacesEmailBody(args: {
  firstName?: string;
  soeid: string;
  namespaces: string[];
  recipient: string;
}): string {
  const items = args.namespaces.map((ns) => `<li><code>${escapeHtml(ns)}</code></li>`).join('');
  return layout(
    'Confirmation: DevSpaces Deployment Project(s) Deleted',
    'DevSpaces Notification',
    `<p>Dear ${greeting(args.firstName, args.soeid)},</p>
<p>The following DevSpaces deployment project(s) associated with your user id have been deleted:</p>
<ul>${items}</ul>
<p>Thank you for managing your resources.</p>`,
    args.recipient,
  );
}

/src/infra/kube/KubeClient.ts
import {
  CoreV1Api,
  KubeConfig,
  KubernetesObjectApi,
  PatchStrategy,
  type KubernetesObject,
} from '@kubernetes/client-node';
import type { DevWorkspaceResource, KubeObject } from '../../types.js';
import { isK8sStatus, toAppError } from '../../utils/k8s-errors.js';
import type { KubeGateway, NamespacedKind } from '../ports.js';

const API_VERSION: Record<NamespacedKind, string> = {
  Secret: 'v1',
  ConfigMap: 'v1',
  ResourceQuota: 'v1',
  LimitRange: 'v1',
  Role: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1',
  RoleBinding: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1',
  NetworkPolicy: 'networking.k8s.io/v1',
  DevWorkspace: 'workspace.devfile.io/v1alpha2',
  DevWorkspaceTemplate: 'workspace.devfile.io/v1alpha2',
};

const asK8s = (o: unknown): KubernetesObject => o as KubernetesObject;
const asKube = <T>(o: unknown): T => o as T;

export function loadKubeConfig(kubeconfigPath?: string): KubeConfig {
  const kc = new KubeConfig();
  if (kubeconfigPath) kc.loadFromFile(kubeconfigPath);
  else kc.loadFromDefault();
  return kc;
}

export interface ClusterConnection {
  apiUrl: string;
  /** Bearer token (e.g. from devSpacesClusterToken001d). Wins over a kubeconfig. */
  token?: string;
  kubeconfigPath?: string;
  /** Do not verify the cluster's certificate (NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED=0). */
  skipTlsVerify?: boolean;
}

/** Token + API URL when a token is given, else the kubeconfig file, else the pod's service account / default config. */
export function createKubeConfig(connection: ClusterConnection): KubeConfig {
  if (!connection.token || !connection.apiUrl) return loadKubeConfig(connection.kubeconfigPath);
  const kc = new KubeConfig();
  kc.loadFromOptions({
    clusters: [
      { name: 'cluster', server: connection.apiUrl, skipTLSVerify: connection.skipTlsVerify === true },
    ],
    users: [{ name: 'service-account', token: connection.token }],
    contexts: [{ name: 'default', cluster: 'cluster', user: 'service-account' }],
    currentContext: 'default',
  });
  return kc;
}

/** Kubernetes/OpenShift access through the pod's service account (or a kubeconfig for local runs). */
export class KubeClient implements KubeGateway {
  async applyCluster(kind: 'ClusterRole' | 'ClusterRoleBinding', body: KubeObject): Promise<KubeObject> {
    const object = asK8s({
      ...body,
      apiVersion: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1',
      kind,
      metadata: { ...body.metadata, namespace: undefined },
    });
    return this.call(`Apply ${kind}`, async () => {
      try {
        const current = await this.objects.read({
          apiVersion: object.apiVersion,
          kind,
          metadata: { name: body.metadata?.name ?? '' },
        });
        object.metadata = { ...object.metadata, resourceVersion: current.metadata?.resourceVersion };
        return asKube<KubeObject>(await this.objects.replace(object));
      } catch (error) {
        if (!isK8sStatus(error, 404)) throw error;
        return asKube<KubeObject>(await this.objects.create(object));
      }
    });
  }
  private readonly core: CoreV1Api;
  private readonly objects: KubernetesObjectApi;

  constructor(kubeConfig: KubeConfig) {
    this.core = kubeConfig.makeApiClient(CoreV1Api);
    this.objects = KubernetesObjectApi.makeApiClient(kubeConfig);
  }

  private async call<T>(context: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    try {
      return await fn();
    } catch (error) {
      throw toAppError(context, error);
    }
  }

  private async orNull<T>(context: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T | null> {
    try {
      return await fn();
    } catch (error) {
      if (isK8sStatus(error, 404)) return null;
      throw toAppError(context, error);
    }
  }

  private header(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string) {
    return { apiVersion: API_VERSION[kind], kind, metadata: { name, namespace } };
  }

  private body<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, body: T): KubernetesObject {
    return asK8s({
      ...body,
      apiVersion: API_VERSION[kind],
      kind,
      metadata: { ...body.metadata, namespace },
    });
  }

  async listNamespaces(labelSelector?: string): Promise<KubeObject[]> {
    const res = await this.call('List namespaces', () => this.core.listNamespace({ labelSelector }));
    return asKube<KubeObject[]>(res.items);
  }

  getNamespace(name: string): Promise<KubeObject | null> {
    return this.orNull(`Read namespace '${name}'`, async () =>
      asKube<KubeObject>(await this.core.readNamespace({ name })),
    );
  }

  async createNamespace(body: KubeObject): Promise<KubeObject> {
    const created = await this.call(`Create namespace '${body.metadata?.name}'`, () =>
      this.core.createNamespace({ body: asKube(body) }),
    );
    return asKube<KubeObject>(created);
  }

  async deleteNamespace(name: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const result = await this.orNull(`Delete namespace '${name}'`, () => this.core.deleteNamespace({ name }));
    return result !== null;
  }

  async createProject(body: KubeObject): Promise<KubeObject> {
    const name = body.metadata?.name ?? '';
    const request = asK8s({ ...body, apiVersion: 'project.openshift.io/v1', kind: 'ProjectRequest' });
    const created = await this.call(`Create project '${name}'`, () => this.objects.create(request));
    const result = asKube<KubeObject>(created);
    return result.metadata?.name ? result : { ...result, metadata: { ...result.metadata, name } };
  }

  get<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string): Promise<T | null> {
    return this.orNull(`Read ${kind} '${name}'`, async () =>
      asKube<T>(await this.objects.read(this.header(kind, namespace, name))),
    );
  }

  async list<T extends KubeObject>(
    kind: NamespacedKind,
    namespace: string,
    labelSelector?: string,
  ): Promise<T[]> {
    const res = await this.call(`List ${kind} in '${namespace}'`, () =>
      this.objects.list(
        API_VERSION[kind],
        kind,
        namespace,
        undefined,
        undefined,
        undefined,
        undefined,
        labelSelector,
      ),
    );
    return asKube<T[]>(res.items);
  }

  async create<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, body: T): Promise<T> {
    const created = await this.call(`Create ${kind} '${body.metadata?.name}'`, () =>
      this.objects.create(this.body(kind, namespace, body)),
    );
    return asKube<T>(created);
  }

  async apply<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, body: T): Promise<T> {
    const name = body.metadata?.name ?? '';
    const context = `Apply ${kind} '${name}'`;
    const replaceExisting = async (): Promise<T | null> => {
      const existing = await this.get<T>(kind, namespace, name);
      if (!existing) return null;
      const spec = this.body(kind, namespace, {
        ...body,
        metadata: { ...body.metadata, resourceVersion: existing.metadata?.resourceVersion },
      });
      return asKube<T>(await this.call(context, () => this.objects.replace(spec)));
    };

    const replaced = await replaceExisting();
    if (replaced) return replaced;
    try {
      return asKube<T>(await this.objects.create(this.body(kind, namespace, body)));
    } catch (error) {
      if (isK8sStatus(error, 409)) {
        // Lost a creation race with another request; replace what the winner created.
        const retried = await replaceExisting();
        if (retried) return retried;
      }
      throw toAppError(context, error);
    }
  }

  async delete(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const result = await this.orNull(`Delete ${kind} '${name}'`, () =>
      this.objects.delete(asK8s(this.header(kind, namespace, name))),
    );
    return result !== null;
  }

  async setWorkspaceStarted(
    namespace: string,
    name: string,
    started: boolean,
  ): Promise<DevWorkspaceResource> {
    const patch = asK8s({ ...this.header('DevWorkspace', namespace, name), spec: { started } });
    const patched = await this.call(`${started ? 'Start' : 'Stop'} DevWorkspace '${name}'`, () =>
      this.objects.patch(patch, undefined, undefined, undefined, undefined, PatchStrategy.MergePatch),
    );
    return asKube<DevWorkspaceResource>(patched);
  }

  async ping(): Promise<void> {
    await this.call('Kubernetes API ping', () => this.core.getAPIResources());
  }
}

/src/infra/ports.ts
import type { CatalogEntry, DevWorkspaceResource, KubeObject, MetricsRecord } from '../types.js';

export type NamespacedKind =
  | 'Secret'
  | 'ConfigMap'
  | 'ResourceQuota'
  | 'LimitRange'
  | 'Role'
  | 'RoleBinding'
  | 'NetworkPolicy'
  | 'DevWorkspace'
  | 'DevWorkspaceTemplate';

/** Everything the services need from Kubernetes/OpenShift. Implemented by KubeClient and by the in-memory fake. */
export interface KubeGateway {
  applyCluster?(kind: 'ClusterRole' | 'ClusterRoleBinding', body: KubeObject): Promise<KubeObject>;
  listNamespaces(labelSelector?: string): Promise<KubeObject[]>;
  getNamespace(name: string): Promise<KubeObject | null>;
  createNamespace(body: KubeObject): Promise<KubeObject>;
  deleteNamespace(name: string): Promise<boolean>;
  /** OpenShift ProjectRequest; the resulting namespace is returned. */
  createProject(body: KubeObject): Promise<KubeObject>;

  get<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string): Promise<T | null>;
  list<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, labelSelector?: string): Promise<T[]>;
  create<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, body: T): Promise<T>;
  /** Create the object, or replace it when it already exists. */
  apply<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, body: T): Promise<T>;
  /** Returns false when the object was already gone. */
  delete(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string): Promise<boolean>;

  setWorkspaceStarted(namespace: string, name: string, started: boolean): Promise<DevWorkspaceResource>;
  ping(): Promise<void>;
}

export interface MetricsStore {
  insert(record: MetricsRecord): Promise<void>;
  ping(): Promise<void>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

export interface DevfileRegistry {
  getCatalog(): Promise<CatalogEntry[]>;
  /** Raw devfile YAML for a stack. Throws WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError for unknown stack/version. */
  getDevfile(stack: string, version?: string): Promise<{ yaml: string; version: string }>;
}

export interface NamespaceCreatedEmail {
  soeid: string;
  namespace: string;
}
export interface NamespaceExistsEmail {
  soeid: string;
  newNamespace: string;
  existingNamespace: string;
}
export interface NamespacesDeletedEmail {
  soeid: string;
  namespaces: string[];
}

/** Best-effort user notifications; implementations must never throw. */
export interface Notifier {
  namespaceCreated(event: NamespaceCreatedEmail): Promise<void>;
  namespaceAlreadyExists(event: NamespaceExistsEmail): Promise<void>;
  namespacesDeleted(event: NamespacesDeletedEmail): Promise<void>;
}

/** Looks up a person's first name for greetings; resolves undefined when unknown or unavailable. */
export interface UserDirectory {
  firstName(soeid: string): Promise<string | undefined>;
}

export interface Clock {
  now(): Date;
  sleep(ms: number): Promise<void>;
}

export const systemClock: Clock = {
  now: () => new Date(),
  sleep: (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)),
};

/src/routers/index.ts
import { Router } from 'express';
import { requireAdmin } from '../http/helpers.js';
import type { Container } from '../container.js';
import { authenticate, authorizeSoeid } from '../http/auth.js';
import authRouter from './authRouter.js';
import catalogRouter from './catalogRouter.js';
import configurationRouter from './configurationRouter.js';
import deploymentNamespaceRouter from './deploymentNamespaceRouter.js';
import gitConfigRouter from './gitConfigRouter.js';
import healthCheckRouter from './healthCheckRouter.js';
import logRouter from './logRouter.js';
import namespaceRouter from './namespaceRouter.js';
import patRouter from './patRouter.js';
import projectRouter from './projectRouter.js';
import stackRouter from './stackRouter.js';
import supportRouter from './supportRouter.js';
import workspaceRouter from './workspaceRouter.js';

export default function baseRouter(container: Container) {
  const api = Router();
  api.use(healthCheckRouter(container));
  api.use(authRouter(container));
  api.use('/support', authenticate(container.config.auth), requireAdmin, supportRouter(container));
  api.use(
    '/configuration',
    authenticate(container.config.auth),
    requireAdmin,
    configurationRouter(container),
  );
  api.use('/logs', authenticate(container.config.auth), requireAdmin, logRouter());
  api.use('/catalog', authenticate(container.config.auth));
  api.use(catalogRouter(container));
  const user = Router({ mergeParams: true });
  user.use(stackRouter(container));
  user.use(projectRouter(container));
  user.use(namespaceRouter(container));
  user.use(workspaceRouter(container));
  user.use(patRouter(container));
  user.use(gitConfigRouter(container));
  user.use(deploymentNamespaceRouter(container));
  api.use('/:soeid', authenticate(container.config.auth), authorizeSoeid(), user);
  return api;
}
