/src/services/DeploymentNamespaceService.ts
import {
  ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME,
  BUILDER_ROLE_CONFIG,
  DEPLOYER_ROLE_CONFIG,
  DEPLOYMENT_LIMIT_RANGE_DEFAULT_CONFIG,
  DEPLOYMENT_RESOURCE_QUOTA_COMPUTE_CONFIG,
  DEPLOYMENT_RESOURCE_QUOTA_STORAGE_CONFIG,
  LABEL_COMPONENT,
  LABEL_CSIID,
  LABEL_DEPLOYMENT_NAMESPACE,
} from '../constants/kubernetes-configs.js';
import type { KubeObject } from '../types.js';
import type { KubeGateway, Notifier } from '../infra/ports.js';
import { errorMessage } from '../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../utils/logger.js';
import { csiidFromDeploymentNamespace, deploymentNamespace } from '../utils/names.js';
import type { MetricsRecorder } from './MetricsRecorder.js';

export interface DeploymentEnsureResult {
  exists: boolean;
  name: string;
}

export interface DeploymentDeleteResult {
  exists: boolean;
  deleted: string[];
  failed: string[];
  message: string;
}

export class DeploymentNamespaceService {
  constructor(
    private readonly kube: KubeGateway,
    private readonly recorder: MetricsRecorder,
    private readonly notifier: Notifier,
    private readonly requester: string,
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
  ) {}

  async list(soeid: string): Promise<string[]> {
    const pattern = new RegExp(`^${soeid}-devspaces-deploy-\\d+$`);
    const all = await this.kube.listNamespaces();
    return all.map((ns) => ns.metadata?.name ?? '').filter((name) => pattern.test(name));
  }

  /** Returns the existing deployment project for this CSI id, or creates and provisions a new one. */
  async ensure(soeid: string, csiid: string): Promise<DeploymentEnsureResult> {
    const desired = deploymentNamespace(soeid, csiid);
    if (await this.kube.getNamespace(desired)) {
      await this.notifier.namespaceAlreadyExists({
        soeid,
        newNamespace: desired,
        existingNamespace: desired,
      });
      return { exists: true, name: desired };
    }

    const actual = await this.recorder.track(
      { soeid, csiid, eventtype: 'create', resourcetype: 'deployment_namespace', resourcename: desired },
      () => this.createAndProvision(soeid, csiid, desired),
    );
    await this.notifier.namespaceCreated({ soeid, namespace: actual });
    return { exists: false, name: actual };
  }

  private async createAndProvision(soeid: string, csiid: string, desired: string): Promise<string> {
    const project = await this.kube.createProject({
      metadata: {
        name: desired,
        labels: {
          [LABEL_COMPONENT]: LABEL_DEPLOYMENT_NAMESPACE,
          'app.kubernetes.io/managed-by': 'devspaces-support-team',
          'devspaces.citi.com/purpose': 'deployment-resource-management',
          [LABEL_CSIID]: csiid,
        },
        annotations: {
          [ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME]: soeid,
          'devspaces.citi.com/creator': 'workspace-manager-automation',
          'openshift.io/requester': this.requester,
          'openshift.io/display-name': `DevSpaces Deployment Project for User ${soeid} [CSIID: ${csiid}]`,
          'openshift.io/description': `Temporary deployment testing project for user ${soeid} [CSIID: ${csiid}], removable by DevSpaces support`,
        },
      },
    });
    const name = project.metadata?.name ?? desired;

    await this.reconcile(soeid, name);
    this.logger.success('deployment_namespace.created', { soeid, namespace: name });
    return name;
  }

  private async grantAccess(soeid: string, namespace: string): Promise<void> {
    for (const role of [BUILDER_ROLE_CONFIG, DEPLOYER_ROLE_CONFIG]) {
      const roleName = role.metadata?.name ?? '';
      await this.kube.apply('Role', namespace, role);
      await this.kube.apply('RoleBinding', namespace, this.binding(soeid, namespace, roleName));
    }
  }

  async reconcile(soeid: string, namespace: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.kube.apply('ResourceQuota', namespace, DEPLOYMENT_RESOURCE_QUOTA_COMPUTE_CONFIG);
    await this.kube.apply('LimitRange', namespace, DEPLOYMENT_LIMIT_RANGE_DEFAULT_CONFIG);
    await this.kube.apply('ResourceQuota', namespace, DEPLOYMENT_RESOURCE_QUOTA_STORAGE_CONFIG);
    await this.grantAccess(soeid, namespace);
  }

  private binding(soeid: string, namespace: string, roleName: string): KubeObject {
    return {
      apiVersion: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1',
      kind: 'RoleBinding',
      metadata: { name: `${roleName}-${soeid}-binding` },
      subjects: [
        { kind: 'User', name: soeid, apiGroup: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io' },
        { kind: 'ServiceAccount', name: 'default', namespace },
      ],
      roleRef: { apiGroup: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io', kind: 'Role', name: roleName },
    };
  }

  async delete(soeid: string): Promise<DeploymentDeleteResult> {
    const names = await this.list(soeid);
    if (names.length === 0) {
      return { exists: false, deleted: [], failed: [], message: 'No deployment namespaces found for user.' };
    }

    const deleted: string[] = [];
    const failed: string[] = [];
    for (const name of names) {
      try {
        await this.recorder.track(
          {
            soeid,
            csiid: csiidFromDeploymentNamespace(name) ?? '',
            eventtype: 'delete',
            resourcetype: 'deployment_namespace',
            resourcename: name,
          },
          () => this.kube.deleteNamespace(name),
        );
        deleted.push(name);
      } catch (error) {
        this.logger.error('deployment_namespace.delete_failed', {
          soeid,
          namespace: name,
          error: errorMessage(error),
        });
        failed.push(name);
      }
    }

    if (deleted.length > 0) await this.notifier.namespacesDeleted({ soeid, namespaces: deleted });

    const parts: string[] = [];
    if (deleted.length > 0)
      parts.push(`Deleted ${deleted.length} deployment namespace(s): ${deleted.join(', ')}`);
    if (failed.length > 0) parts.push(`Failed to delete ${failed.length}: ${failed.join(', ')}`);
    return { exists: deleted.length > 0, deleted, failed, message: parts.join('. ') };
  }
}

/src/services/GitConfigService.ts
import type { KubeObject } from '../types.js';
import type { KubeGateway } from '../infra/ports.js';
import { BadRequestError, NotFoundError } from '../utils/errors.js';
import type { MetricsRecorder } from './MetricsRecorder.js';
import type { NamespaceService } from './NamespaceService.js';

export const GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP = 'workspace-userdata-gitconfig-configmap';
const MAX_CONTENT_BYTES = 64 * 1024;

export interface GitConfigFile {
  name: string;
  namespace: string;
  content: string;
}

export class GitConfigService {
  constructor(
    private readonly kube: KubeGateway,
    private readonly namespaces: NamespaceService,
    private readonly recorder: MetricsRecorder,
  ) {}

  async view(soeid: string, csiid: string): Promise<GitConfigFile> {
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    const configMap = await this.kube.get<KubeObject>('ConfigMap', namespace, GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP);
    if (!configMap) throw new NotFoundError('Git config not found', 'GITCONFIG_NOT_FOUND');
    return {
      name: GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP,
      namespace,
      content: (configMap.data as Record<string, string> | undefined)?.['.gitconfig'] ?? '',
    };
  }

  /** Create or replace the user's `~/.gitconfig`. */
  async upsert(soeid: string, csiid: string, content: string): Promise<GitConfigFile> {
    if (content.trim().length === 0) throw new BadRequestError('Git config content must not be empty');
    if (Buffer.byteLength(content) > MAX_CONTENT_BYTES) {
      throw new BadRequestError('Git config content is too large (max 64 KiB)');
    }
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    const existed = (await this.kube.get('ConfigMap', namespace, GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP)) !== null;

    await this.recorder.track(
      {
        soeid,
        csiid,
        eventtype: existed ? 'update' : 'create',
        resourcetype: 'gitconfig',
        resourcename: GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP,
      },
      () =>
        this.kube.apply('ConfigMap', namespace, {
          apiVersion: 'v1',
          kind: 'ConfigMap',
          metadata: {
            name: GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP,
            labels: {
              'controller.devfile.io/mount-to-devworkspace': 'true',
              'controller.devfile.io/watch-configmap': 'true',
            },
            annotations: {
              'controller.devfile.io/mount-as': 'subpath',
              'controller.devfile.io/mount-path': '/home/user',
            },
          },
          data: { '.gitconfig': content },
        }),
    );
    return { name: GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP, namespace, content };
  }

  /** Returns false when there was nothing to delete. */
  async delete(soeid: string, csiid: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    return this.recorder.track(
      { soeid, csiid, eventtype: 'delete', resourcetype: 'gitconfig', resourcename: GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP },
      () => this.kube.delete('ConfigMap', namespace, GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP),
    );
  }
}

/src/services/MetricsRecorder.ts
import { buildLogRecord, type MetricsInput } from '../factories/log-factory.js';
import type { MetricsRecord } from '../types.js';
import { systemClock, type Clock, type MetricsStore } from '../infra/ports.js';
import { errorMessage } from '../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../utils/logger.js';

export type MetricBase = MetricsInput;

/**
 * Writes one metrics row per user action. Recording is best-effort: a slow or broken database is logged
 * and never fails the user's action.
 */
export class MetricsRecorder {
  constructor(
    private readonly store: MetricsStore,
    private readonly clock: Clock = systemClock,
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
    private readonly timeoutMs = 2000,
  ) {}

  async record(input: MetricsInput): Promise<void> {
    const record = buildLogRecord(input, this.clock.now());
    let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
    try {
      await Promise.race([
        this.store.insert(record),
        new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
          timer = setTimeout(
            () => reject(new Error(`metrics insert timed out after ${this.timeoutMs}ms`)),
            this.timeoutMs,
          );
        }),
      ]);
    } catch (error) {
      this.logger.error('metrics.insert_failed', {
        soeid: record.soeid,
        eventtype: record.eventtype,
        resourcetype: record.resourcetype,
        error: errorMessage(error),
      });
    } finally {
      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }

  /** Run `fn`, record success (with optional extra fields from the result) or failure, and pass the outcome through. */
  async track<T>(
    base: MetricBase,
    fn: () => Promise<T>,
    onSuccess?: (result: T) => Partial<MetricsRecord>,
  ): Promise<T> {
    let result: T;
    try {
      result = await fn();
    } catch (error) {
      await this.record({ ...base, status: { failed: true, error: errorMessage(error), message: '' } });
      throw error;
    }
    await this.record({ ...base, ...(onSuccess?.(result) ?? {}) });
    return result;
  }
}

/src/services/NamespaceService.ts
import {
  ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME,
  LABEL_COMPONENT,
  LABEL_CSIID,
  LABEL_WORKSPACES_NAMESPACE,
  NETWORK_POLICIES_DEFAULT_CONFIG,
  ORION_LIMIT_RANGE_CONFIG,
  ORION_RESOURCE_QUOTA_CONFIG,
  USER_ROLE_CONFIG,
  WORKSPACES_NAMESPACE_SELECTOR,
} from '../constants/kubernetes-configs.js';
import type { KubeObject, Project } from '../types.js';
import type { KubeGateway } from '../infra/ports.js';
import { ConflictError, NotFoundError } from '../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../utils/logger.js';
import { csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace, workspaceNamespace } from '../utils/names.js';
import type { MetricsRecorder } from './MetricsRecorder.js';

export interface NamespaceServiceOptions {
  workspaceProxyUrl?: string;
  emailDomain: string;
}

export interface EnsuredNamespace {
  name: string;
  created: boolean;
}

export class NamespaceService {
  constructor(
    private readonly kube: KubeGateway,
    private readonly recorder: MetricsRecorder,
    private readonly options: NamespaceServiceOptions,
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
  ) {}

  /** All workspace namespaces owned by the user (by label and Che username annotation). */
  async listForUser(soeid: string): Promise<KubeObject[]> {
    const all = await this.kube.listNamespaces(WORKSPACES_NAMESPACE_SELECTOR);
    return all.filter((ns) => ns.metadata?.annotations?.[ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME] === soeid);
  }

  async listProjects(soeid: string): Promise<Project[]> {
    const namespaces = await this.listForUser(soeid);
    return namespaces.map((ns) => {
      const name = ns.metadata?.name ?? '';
      return {
        name,
        soeid,
        csiid: ns.metadata?.labels?.[LABEL_CSIID] ?? csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace(name) ?? '',
        createdAt: ns.metadata?.creationTimestamp
          ? new Date(ns.metadata.creationTimestamp).toISOString()
          : '',
      };
    });
  }

  /** The namespace must already exist (PATs, git config); never creates one. */
  async require(soeid: string, csiid: string): Promise<string> {
    const name = workspaceNamespace(soeid, csiid);
    const existing = await this.kube.getNamespace(name);
    if (!existing) throw new NotFoundError(`Project '${name}' not found`, 'PROJECT_NOT_FOUND');
    this.assertOwner(existing, soeid);
    return name;
  }

  /** Find the user's namespace for this CSI id, creating and provisioning it when it does not exist. */
  async ensure(soeid: string, csiid: string): Promise<EnsuredNamespace> {
    const name = workspaceNamespace(soeid, csiid);
    const existing = await this.kube.getNamespace(name);
    if (existing) {
      this.assertOwner(existing, soeid);
      return { name, created: false };
    }

    this.logger.info('namespace.creating', { soeid, namespace: name });
    const created = await this.recorder.track(
      { soeid, csiid, eventtype: 'create', resourcetype: 'project', resourcename: name },
      () => this.createAndProvision(soeid, csiid, name),
    );
    return { name, created };
  }

  private async createAndProvision(soeid: string, csiid: string, name: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      await this.kube.createNamespace({
        apiVersion: 'v1',
        kind: 'Namespace',
        metadata: {
          name,
          labels: {
            [LABEL_COMPONENT]: LABEL_WORKSPACES_NAMESPACE,
            'app.kubernetes.io/part-of': 'che.eclipse.org',
            'app.kubernetes.io/managed-by': 'workspace-mgr',
            [LABEL_CSIID]: csiid,
          },
          annotations: { [ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME]: soeid },
        },
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof ConflictError) {
        // Another request created it between our read and write; use theirs.
        const raced = await this.kube.getNamespace(name);
        if (raced) {
          this.assertOwner(raced, soeid);
          return false;
        }
      }
      throw error;
    }

    try {
      await this.reconcile(soeid, name);
    } catch (error) {
      this.logger.error('namespace.provision_failed', { soeid, namespace: name });
      await this.kube.deleteNamespace(name).catch(() => false);
      throw error;
    }
    return true;
  }

  async reconcile(soeid: string, namespace: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.kube.apply('Role', namespace, USER_ROLE_CONFIG);
    await this.kube.apply('RoleBinding', namespace, {
      apiVersion: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1',
      kind: 'RoleBinding',
      metadata: {
        name: `devspaces-project-access-${soeid}`,
        labels: { [LABEL_COMPONENT]: 'devspaces-workspace-management' },
      },
      subjects: [{ kind: 'User', name: soeid, apiGroup: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io' }],
      roleRef: {
        kind: 'Role',
        name: USER_ROLE_CONFIG.metadata?.name,
        apiGroup: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io',
      },
    });
    await this.kube.apply('ResourceQuota', namespace, ORION_RESOURCE_QUOTA_CONFIG);
    await this.kube.apply('LimitRange', namespace, ORION_LIMIT_RANGE_CONFIG);
    for (const policy of NETWORK_POLICIES_DEFAULT_CONFIG) {
      await this.kube.apply('NetworkPolicy', namespace, policy);
    }
    await this.applyProxyConfig(soeid, namespace);
  }

  private async applyProxyConfig(soeid: string, namespace: string): Promise<void> {
    const proxy = this.options.workspaceProxyUrl;
    if (!proxy) return;
    const noProxy = 'localhost,127.0.0.1,*.svc,*.svc.cluster.local';

    await this.kube.apply('ConfigMap', namespace, {
      apiVersion: 'v1',
      kind: 'ConfigMap',
      metadata: {
        name: 'proxy-env-vars-ws-mgr',
        labels: {
          'controller.devfile.io/mount-to-devworkspace': 'true',
          'controller.devfile.io/watch-configmap': 'true',
        },
        annotations: { 'controller.devfile.io/mount-as': 'env' },
      },
      data: {
        HTTP_PROXY: proxy,
        HTTPS_PROXY: proxy,
        http_proxy: proxy,
        https_proxy: proxy,
        NO_PROXY: noProxy,
        no_proxy: noProxy,
      },
    });

    await this.kube.apply('ConfigMap', namespace, {
      apiVersion: 'v1',
      kind: 'ConfigMap',
      metadata: {
        name: 'workspace-userdata-gitconfig-configmap-ws-mgr',
        labels: {
          'controller.devfile.io/mount-to-devworkspace': 'true',
          'controller.devfile.io/watch-configmap': 'true',
        },
        annotations: {
          'controller.devfile.io/mount-as': 'subpath',
          'controller.devfile.io/mount-path': '/etc/',
        },
      },
      data: {
        gitconfig: `[user]\n  name = ${soeid}\n  email = ${soeid}@${this.options.emailDomain}\n[http]\n  sslVerify = false\n[proxy]\n  http = ${proxy}\n  https = ${proxy}\n`,
      },
    });
  }

  private assertOwner(namespace: KubeObject, soeid: string): void {
    const owner = namespace.metadata?.annotations?.[ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME];
    if (owner !== soeid) {
      throw new ConflictError(
        `Namespace '${namespace.metadata?.name}' exists but belongs to another user`,
        'NAMESPACE_OWNERSHIP_MISMATCH',
      );
    }
  }
}

/src/services/PatService.ts
import type { AppConfig } from '../config/env.js';
import type { KubeObject } from '../types.js';
import type { KubeGateway } from '../infra/ports.js';
import { BadRequestError, ConflictError, NotFoundError } from '../utils/errors.js';
import type { MetricsRecorder } from './MetricsRecorder.js';
import type { NamespaceService } from './NamespaceService.js';

export const PAT_SECRET_PREFIX = 'git-credentials-secret-';
const CREDENTIALS_KEY = 'credentials';
const GIT_CREDENTIAL_SELECTOR = 'controller.devfile.io/git-credential=true';
const GITHUB_PAT_RE = /^ghp_[A-Za-z0-9]{36}$/;

export interface PatSummary {
  namespace: string;
  name: string;
  createdOn: string;
  lastTransitionTime: string;
}

export interface PatDetails extends PatSummary {
  token: string;
  scmUrl: string;
}

/** `ghp_ab…xyz`; the full value is never returned or logged. */
export function maskToken(token: string): string {
  return token.length > 5 ? `${token.slice(0, 2)}…${token.slice(-3)}` : '***';
}

export function assertValidGithubToken(token: string): void {
  if (!GITHUB_PAT_RE.test(token)) {
    throw new BadRequestError(
      "Invalid GitHub PAT format: token must start with 'ghp_' and be 40 characters long",
      'INVALID_PAT',
    );
  }
}

const iso = (value: Date | string | undefined): string => (value ? new Date(value).toISOString() : '');

export class PatService {
  constructor(
    private readonly kube: KubeGateway,
    private readonly namespaces: NamespaceService,
    private readonly recorder: MetricsRecorder,
    private readonly github: AppConfig['github'],
  ) {}

  async list(soeid: string): Promise<PatSummary[]> {
    const namespaces = await this.namespaces.listForUser(soeid);
    const result: PatSummary[] = [];
    for (const ns of namespaces) {
      const namespace = ns.metadata?.name ?? '';
      const secrets = await this.kube.list<KubeObject>('Secret', namespace, GIT_CREDENTIAL_SELECTOR);
      for (const secret of secrets) {
        const name = secret.metadata?.name ?? '';
        if (name.startsWith(PAT_SECRET_PREFIX)) result.push(this.summary(namespace, secret));
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  async view(soeid: string, csiid: string, tokenName: string): Promise<PatDetails> {
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    const secret = await this.kube.get<KubeObject>('Secret', namespace, this.secretName(tokenName));
    if (!secret) throw new NotFoundError(`PAT '${tokenName}' not found`, 'PAT_NOT_FOUND');
    const encoded = (secret.data as Record<string, string> | undefined)?.[CREDENTIALS_KEY] ?? '';
    const decoded = Buffer.from(encoded, 'base64').toString('utf8');
    const token = /:([^:@]+)@[^@]+$/.exec(decoded)?.[1] ?? '';
    return {
      ...this.summary(namespace, secret),
      token: token ? maskToken(token) : '[unrecognized format]',
      scmUrl: secret.metadata?.annotations?.['che.eclipse.org/scm-url'] ?? this.github.url,
    };
  }

  async create(soeid: string, csiid: string, tokenName: string, token: string): Promise<PatSummary> {
    assertValidGithubToken(token);
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    const name = this.secretName(tokenName);
    if (await this.kube.get('Secret', namespace, name)) {
      throw new ConflictError(`PAT '${tokenName}' already exists`, 'PAT_EXISTS');
    }
    const created = await this.recorder.track(
      { soeid, csiid, eventtype: 'create', resourcetype: 'pat', resourcename: name },
      () => this.kube.create<KubeObject>('Secret', namespace, this.secretBody(soeid, namespace, name, token)),
    );
    return this.summary(namespace, created);
  }

  async update(soeid: string, csiid: string, tokenName: string, token: string): Promise<PatSummary> {
    assertValidGithubToken(token);
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    const name = this.secretName(tokenName);
    if (!(await this.kube.get('Secret', namespace, name))) {
      throw new NotFoundError(`PAT '${tokenName}' not found`, 'PAT_NOT_FOUND');
    }
    const updated = await this.recorder.track(
      { soeid, csiid, eventtype: 'update', resourcetype: 'pat', resourcename: name },
      () => this.kube.apply<KubeObject>('Secret', namespace, this.secretBody(soeid, namespace, name, token)),
    );
    return this.summary(namespace, updated);
  }

  /** Deleting a token that is already gone is a success (returns false). */
  async delete(soeid: string, csiid: string, tokenName: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    const name = this.secretName(tokenName);
    return this.recorder.track(
      { soeid, csiid, eventtype: 'delete', resourcetype: 'pat', resourcename: name },
      () => this.kube.delete('Secret', namespace, name),
    );
  }

  private secretName(tokenName: string): string {
    return `${PAT_SECRET_PREFIX}${tokenName}`;
  }

  private summary(namespace: string, secret: KubeObject): PatSummary {
    const name = secret.metadata?.name ?? '';
    const createdOn = iso(secret.metadata?.creationTimestamp);
    return {
      namespace,
      name: name.startsWith(PAT_SECRET_PREFIX) ? name.slice(PAT_SECRET_PREFIX.length) : name,
      createdOn,
      lastTransitionTime: secret.metadata?.annotations?.lastTransitionTime ?? createdOn,
    };
  }

  private secretBody(soeid: string, namespace: string, name: string, token: string): KubeObject {
    const scm = new URL(this.github.url);
    const user = encodeURIComponent(`${soeid}${this.github.userSuffix}`);
    const credentials = `${scm.protocol}//${user}:${token}@${scm.host}`;
    return {
      apiVersion: 'v1',
      kind: 'Secret',
      metadata: {
        name,
        namespace,
        labels: {
          'app.kubernetes.io/component': 'workspace-secret',
          'app.kubernetes.io/part-of': 'che.eclipse.org',
          'controller.devfile.io/git-credential': 'true',
          'controller.devfile.io/watch-secret': 'true',
        },
        annotations: {
          'che.eclipse.org/automount-workspace-secret': 'true',
          'che.eclipse.org/git-credential': 'true',
          'che.eclipse.org/mount-as': 'file',
          'che.eclipse.org/mount-path': '/.git-credentials',
          'che.eclipse.org/scm-url': this.github.url,
          'controller.devfile.io/mount-path': '/.git-credentials',
          lastTransitionTime: new Date().toISOString(),
        },
      },
      type: 'Opaque',
      data: { [CREDENTIALS_KEY]: Buffer.from(credentials).toString('base64') },
    };
  }
}

/src/services/SupportService.ts
import { ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME } from '../constants/kubernetes-configs.js';
import type { KubeObject } from '../types.js';
import type { KubeGateway } from '../infra/ports.js';
import type { NamespaceService } from './NamespaceService.js';
import type { DeploymentNamespaceService } from './DeploymentNamespaceService.js';
import type { Logger } from '../utils/logger.js';
import { ConflictError, NotFoundError } from '../utils/errors.js';
import { deploymentNamespace, parseCsiid, parseSoeid } from '../utils/names.js';

export interface SupportInput {
  soeid: string;
  csiid: string;
  target: 'workspace' | 'deployment';
  resources?: (KubeObject & {
    kind: 'Role' | 'RoleBinding' | 'ResourceQuota' | 'LimitRange' | 'ClusterRole' | 'ClusterRoleBinding';
  })[];
}
export class SupportService {
  constructor(
    private readonly dependencies: {
      kube: KubeGateway;
      deployKube: KubeGateway;
      namespaces: NamespaceService;
      deployments: DeploymentNamespaceService;
      logger: Logger;
    },
  ) {}
  async apply(body: SupportInput) {
    const soeid = parseSoeid(body.soeid);
    const csiid = parseCsiid(body.csiid);
    const kube = body.target === 'workspace' ? this.dependencies.kube : this.dependencies.deployKube;
    const namespace =
      body.target === 'workspace'
        ? await this.dependencies.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid)
        : deploymentNamespace(soeid, csiid);
    const existing = await kube.getNamespace(namespace);
    if (!existing) throw new NotFoundError('Project not found');
    if (existing.metadata?.annotations?.[ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME] !== soeid)
      throw new ConflictError('Project belongs to another user');
    if (!body.resources) {
      if (body.target === 'workspace') await this.dependencies.namespaces.reconcile(soeid, namespace);
      else await this.dependencies.deployments.reconcile(soeid, namespace);
    } else {
      for (const resource of body.resources) {
        const object = resource as KubeObject;
        if (resource.kind === 'ClusterRole' || resource.kind === 'ClusterRoleBinding') {
          if (!kube.applyCluster) throw new ConflictError('Cluster resource updates unavailable');
          await kube.applyCluster(resource.kind, object);
        } else await kube.apply(resource.kind, namespace, object);
      }
    }
    this.dependencies.logger.info('support.resources_applied', { soeid, namespace, target: body.target });
    return { namespace, target: body.target, message: 'Resources reconciled successfully.' };
  }
}

/src/services/WorkspaceService.ts
import type { AppConfig } from '../config/env.js';
import type { CreateWorkspaceInput, DevWorkspaceResource, EditorKey, Workspace } from '../types.js';
import { applyEditorProfile, parseDevfile } from '../factories/devfile-updater.js';
import { buildDevWorkspaceObject, WORKSPACE_LABELS } from '../factories/workspace-factory.js';
import { systemClock, type Clock, type DevfileRegistry, type KubeGateway } from '../infra/ports.js';
import {
  NotFoundError,
  WorkspaceCountExceededError,
  WorkspaceRunCountExceededError,
} from '../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../utils/logger.js';
import { cleanWorkspaceName, csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace } from '../utils/names.js';
import { summarizeComponents } from '../utils/resources.js';
import { durationSince } from '../utils/timestamp.js';
import type { MetricsRecorder } from './MetricsRecorder.js';
import type { NamespaceService } from './NamespaceService.js';

export interface WorkspaceServiceOptions {
  limits: AppConfig['limits'];
  editors: AppConfig['devreg']['editors'];
  devSpacesServiceFqdn: string;
}

const ACTIVE_PHASES = new Set(['Running', 'Starting', 'Stopping']);

/** Running, starting, stopping — or not yet reporting a phase — all still hold cluster resources. */
export const isActive = (dw: DevWorkspaceResource): boolean =>
  !dw.status?.phase || ACTIVE_PHASES.has(dw.status.phase);

export function toWorkspace(dw: DevWorkspaceResource): Workspace {
  const meta = dw.metadata ?? {};
  const annotations = meta.annotations ?? {};
  const namespace = meta.namespace ?? '';
  const resources = summarizeComponents(dw.spec?.template?.components);
  return {
    id: meta.name ?? '',
    name: meta.name ?? '',
    displayName: annotations.workspaceDisplayName ?? meta.name ?? '',
    description: annotations.workspaceDescription ?? '',
    namespace,
    csiid: meta.labels?.[WORKSPACE_LABELS.csiid] ?? csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace(namespace) ?? '',
    phase: dw.status?.phase ?? 'Starting',
    mainUrl: dw.status?.mainUrl ?? '',
    editor: annotations.editorDisplayName ?? '',
    version: annotations.workspaceVersion ?? '',
    stack: meta.labels?.[WORKSPACE_LABELS.stack] ?? '',
    created: meta.creationTimestamp ? new Date(meta.creationTimestamp).toISOString() : '',
    limitcpu: resources.limitcpu,
    limitmemory: resources.limitmemory,
    storage: resources.storage,
  };
}

/** When the workspace last became ready; falls back to creation time. */
function runningSince(dw: DevWorkspaceResource): Date | string | undefined {
  const ready = dw.status?.conditions?.find(
    (c) => (c.type === 'Started' || c.type === 'Ready') && c.status === 'True',
  );
  return ready?.lastTransitionTime ?? dw.metadata?.creationTimestamp;
}

export class WorkspaceService {
  constructor(
    private readonly kube: KubeGateway,
    private readonly namespaces: NamespaceService,
    private readonly registry: DevfileRegistry,
    private readonly recorder: MetricsRecorder,
    private readonly options: WorkspaceServiceOptions,
    private readonly clock: Clock = systemClock,
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
  ) {}

  private async listResources(soeid: string): Promise<DevWorkspaceResource[]> {
    const namespaces = await this.namespaces.listForUser(soeid);
    const lists = await Promise.all(
      namespaces.map((ns) => this.kube.list<DevWorkspaceResource>('DevWorkspace', ns.metadata?.name ?? '')),
    );
    return lists.flat();
  }

  async list(soeid: string): Promise<Workspace[]> {
    return (await this.listResources(soeid)).map(toWorkspace);
  }

  private async find(soeid: string, id: string): Promise<DevWorkspaceResource> {
    const found = (await this.listResources(soeid)).find((dw) => dw.metadata?.name === id);
    if (!found) throw new NotFoundError(`Workspace '${id}' not found`, 'WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND');
    return found;
  }

  async get(soeid: string, id: string): Promise<Workspace> {
    return toWorkspace(await this.find(soeid, id));
  }

  private assertCanRunAnother(all: DevWorkspaceResource[]): void {
    const active = all.filter(isActive).length;
    if (active >= this.options.limits.maxRunning) {
      throw new WorkspaceRunCountExceededError(
        `You already have ${active} running workspace(s); the limit is ${this.options.limits.maxRunning}. Stop one first.`,
      );
    }
  }

  private assertCanCreateAnother(all: DevWorkspaceResource[]): void {
    if (all.length >= this.options.limits.maxTotal) {
      throw new WorkspaceCountExceededError(
        `You already have ${all.length} workspace(s); the limit is ${this.options.limits.maxTotal}. Delete one first.`,
      );
    }
    this.assertCanRunAnother(all);
  }

  async create(input: CreateWorkspaceInput): Promise<Workspace> {
    const { soeid, csiid } = input;
    this.assertCanCreateAnother(await this.listResources(soeid));

    const { name: namespace } = await this.namespaces.ensure(soeid, csiid);
    const { yaml, version } = await this.registry.getDevfile(input.devworkspaceValue, input.stackVersion);
    const devfile = applyEditorProfile(parseDevfile(yaml), input.editor);
    const body = buildDevWorkspaceObject({
      name: cleanWorkspaceName(input.devworkspaceValue),
      namespace,
      csiid,
      stack: input.devworkspaceValue,
      stackVersion: version,
      devfile,
      editor: input.editor,
      editorId: this.editorId(input.editor),
      devSpacesServiceFqdn: this.options.devSpacesServiceFqdn,
      repositoryUrl: input.repositoryUrl,
      repositoryBranch: input.repositoryBranch,
    });

    const created = await this.recorder.track(
      {
        soeid,
        csiid,
        eventtype: 'create',
        resourcetype: 'workspace',
        resourcename: body.metadata?.name ?? '',
        workspaceversion: version,
        editorname: input.editor,
        ...summarizeComponents(devfile.components),
      },
      () => this.kube.create<DevWorkspaceResource>('DevWorkspace', namespace, body),
    );
    this.logger.success('workspace.created', { soeid, namespace, workspace: created.metadata?.name });
    return toWorkspace(created);
  }

  async start(soeid: string, id: string): Promise<Workspace> {
    const dw = await this.find(soeid, id);
    if (dw.status?.phase === 'Running' || dw.status?.phase === 'Starting') return toWorkspace(dw);
    this.assertCanRunAnother(await this.listResources(soeid));
    return this.setStarted(soeid, dw, true);
  }

  async stop(soeid: string, id: string): Promise<Workspace> {
    const dw = await this.find(soeid, id);
    return this.setStarted(soeid, dw, false);
  }

  private async setStarted(soeid: string, dw: DevWorkspaceResource, started: boolean): Promise<Workspace> {
    const namespace = dw.metadata?.namespace ?? '';
    const name = dw.metadata?.name ?? '';
    const resources = summarizeComponents(dw.spec?.template?.components);
    const patched = await this.recorder.track(
      {
        soeid,
        csiid: toWorkspace(dw).csiid,
        eventtype: started ? 'start' : 'stop',
        resourcetype: 'workspace',
        resourcename: name,
        workspaceversion: dw.metadata?.annotations?.workspaceVersion ?? '',
        editorname: dw.metadata?.annotations?.editorDisplayName ?? '',
        duration: started ? '0 seconds' : durationSince(runningSince(dw), this.clock.now()),
        ...resources,
      },
      () => this.kube.setWorkspaceStarted(namespace, name, started),
    );
    this.logger.success(started ? 'workspace.started' : 'workspace.stopped', {
      soeid,
      namespace,
      workspace: name,
    });
    return toWorkspace({ ...dw, ...patched, metadata: { ...dw.metadata, ...patched.metadata } });
  }

  async delete(soeid: string, id: string): Promise<void> {
    const dw = await this.find(soeid, id);
    const namespace = dw.metadata?.namespace ?? '';
    const name = dw.metadata?.name ?? '';
    const resources = summarizeComponents(dw.spec?.template?.components);

    await this.recorder.track(
      {
        soeid,
        csiid: toWorkspace(dw).csiid,
        eventtype: 'delete',
        resourcetype: 'workspace',
        resourcename: name,
        workspaceversion: dw.metadata?.annotations?.workspaceVersion ?? '',
        editorname: dw.metadata?.annotations?.editorDisplayName ?? '',
        duration: durationSince(dw.metadata?.creationTimestamp, this.clock.now()),
        ...resources,
      },
      async () => {
        await this.kube.delete('DevWorkspace', namespace, name);
        // Workspaces created by the legacy flow reference a separate DevWorkspaceTemplate.
        for (const contribution of dw.spec?.contributions ?? []) {
          if (contribution.kubernetes?.name) {
            await this.kube.delete('DevWorkspaceTemplate', namespace, contribution.kubernetes.name);
          }
        }
      },
    );
    this.logger.success('workspace.deleted', { soeid, namespace, workspace: name });
  }

  private editorId(editor: EditorKey): string {
    return this.options.editors[editor];
  }
}
