/src/utils/errors.ts
export class AppError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public readonly status: number,
    public readonly code: string,
    options?: { cause?: unknown },
  ) {
    super(message, options);
    this.name = new.target.name;
  }
}

export class BadRequestError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, code = 'BAD_REQUEST') {
    super(message, 400, code);
  }
}

export class UnauthorizedError extends AppError {
  constructor(message = 'Authentication required') {
    super(message, 401, 'UNAUTHORIZED');
  }
}

export class ForbiddenError extends AppError {
  constructor(message = 'Forbidden', options?: { cause?: unknown }) {
    super(message, 403, 'FORBIDDEN', options);
  }
}

export class NotFoundError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, code = 'NOT_FOUND', options?: { cause?: unknown }) {
    super(message, 404, code, options);
  }
}

export class ConflictError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, code = 'CONFLICT', options?: { cause?: unknown }) {
    super(message, 409, code, options);
  }
}

export class UpstreamError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, options?: { cause?: unknown }) {
    super(message, 502, 'UPSTREAM_ERROR', options);
  }
}

export class TimeoutError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message, 504, 'TIMEOUT');
  }
}

export class WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError extends NotFoundError {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message, 'WORKSPACE_CONFIG_NOT_FOUND');
  }
}

export class WorkspaceCountExceededError extends ConflictError {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message, 'WORKSPACE_COUNT_EXCEEDED');
  }
}

export class WorkspaceRunCountExceededError extends ConflictError {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message, 'WORKSPACE_RUN_COUNT_EXCEEDED');
  }
}

export function errorMessage(error: unknown): string {
  return error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
}

/src/utils/k8s-errors.ts
import axios from 'axios';
import { AppError, ConflictError, ForbiddenError, NotFoundError, UpstreamError } from './errors.js';

interface KubeErrorLike {
  code?: unknown;
  statusCode?: unknown;
  body?: unknown;
  response?: { status?: unknown };
  message?: unknown;
}

function asNumber(value: unknown): number | undefined {
  return typeof value === 'number' ? value : undefined;
}

function parseBody(body: unknown): { code?: number; message?: string } {
  if (typeof body === 'string') {
    try {
      return parseBody(JSON.parse(body));
    } catch {
      return { message: body };
    }
  }
  if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
    const b = body as { code?: unknown; message?: unknown };
    return { code: asNumber(b.code), message: typeof b.message === 'string' ? b.message : undefined };
  }
  return {};
}

/** HTTP status carried by a Kubernetes client / axios error, if any. */
export function k8sStatus(error: unknown): number | undefined {
  if (!error || typeof error !== 'object') return undefined;
  const e = error as KubeErrorLike;
  return asNumber(e.code) ?? asNumber(e.statusCode) ?? asNumber(e.response?.status) ?? parseBody(e.body).code;
}

export const isK8sStatus = (error: unknown, status: number): boolean => k8sStatus(error) === status;

/** Human readable message for an unknown failure. Never includes upstream response bodies verbatim for 5xx. */
export function describeError(context: string, error: unknown): string {
  const status = k8sStatus(error);
  const kubeMessage = parseBody((error as KubeErrorLike | undefined)?.body).message;

  if (status === 404) return `${context}: Resource not found (404)`;
  if (status !== undefined && !axios.isAxiosError(error)) {
    return `${context}: Kubernetes API error (${status}) ${kubeMessage ?? (error as Error)?.message ?? ''}`.trim();
  }
  if (axios.isAxiosError(error)) {
    if (error.response) return `${context}: HTTP ${error.response.status} ${error.message}`;
    if (error.request) return `${context}: No response received from server`;
    return `${context}: Axios error ${error.message}`;
  }
  if (error instanceof Error) {
    if (error.message.includes('certificate') || error.message.includes('SSL')) {
      return `${context}: TLS/SSL certificate error`;
    }
    return `${context}: ${error.message}`;
  }
  return `${context}: ${String(error)}`;
}

/** Translate whatever a Kubernetes call threw into an AppError. */
export function toAppError(context: string, error: unknown): AppError {
  if (error instanceof AppError) return error;
  const status = k8sStatus(error);
  const message = describeError(context, error);
  if (status === 404) return new NotFoundError(message, 'NOT_FOUND', { cause: error });
  if (status === 403) {
    return new ForbiddenError(`${context}: Permission denied, insufficient privileges`, { cause: error });
  }
  if (status === 409) return new ConflictError(message, 'CONFLICT', { cause: error });
  return new UpstreamError(message, { cause: error });
}

/src/utils/log-format.ts
/** Readable terminal logs with timestamps and source locations. */

export type LogKind = 'debug' | 'info' | 'success' | 'warn' | 'error';

const ESC = '\u001b[';
export const RESET = `${ESC}0m`;
export const COLORS: Record<LogKind, string> = {
  error: `${ESC}31m`, // red
  warn: `${ESC}38;5;208m`, // orange
  success: `${ESC}32m`, // green
  info: `${ESC}97m`, // white
  debug: `${ESC}97m`, // white
};

const BADGE: Record<LogKind, string> = {
  error: 'ERROR',
  warn: 'WARN ',
  success: 'OK   ',
  info: 'INFO ',
  debug: 'DEBUG',
};

const EVENT_WIDTH = 30;
const BLOCK_INDENT = '    ';
const MAX_INLINE_FIELDS = 6;
const MAX_INLINE_VALUE = 80;
const MAX_INLINE_TOTAL = 120;

export interface LogEntry {
  time: Date;
  kind: LogKind;
  event: string;
  fields: Record<string, unknown>;
}

const pad2 = (n: number): string => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

/** Local time `2026-10-24 03:12:45`. */
export function formatLocalTime(d: Date): string {
  return (
    `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())} ` +
    `${pad2(d.getHours())}:${pad2(d.getMinutes())}:${pad2(d.getSeconds())}`
  );
}

type Primitive = string | number | boolean | null | undefined;
const isPrimitive = (v: unknown): v is Primitive => v === null || v === undefined || typeof v !== 'object';
const isPlainObject = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> =>
  typeof v === 'object' && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v);

const oneLine = (s: string): string => s.replace(/\r?\n/g, '\\n');

/** Inline value: bare when simple, quoted when it contains spaces or `=`. */
function inlineValue(v: Primitive): string {
  if (typeof v !== 'string') return String(v);
  const s = oneLine(v);
  return /^[^\s"=]+$/.test(s) ? s : JSON.stringify(s);
}

/** Value on its own line: strings as they are, arrays as compact JSON. */
function blockValue(v: unknown): string {
  if (typeof v === 'string') return oneLine(v);
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return JSON.stringify(v);
  return String(v);
}

function renderBlock(obj: Record<string, unknown>, indent: string): string[] {
  const entries = Object.entries(obj).filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined);
  const width = Math.max(
    0,
    ...entries.filter(([, v]) => !isPlainObject(v) || Object.keys(v).length === 0).map(([k]) => k.length),
  );
  const lines: string[] = [];
  for (const [key, value] of entries) {
    if (isPlainObject(value) && Object.keys(value).length > 0) {
      lines.push(`${indent}${key}:`);
      lines.push(...renderBlock(value, `${indent}  `));
    } else {
      const text = isPlainObject(value) ? '{}' : blockValue(value);
      lines.push(`${indent}${key.padEnd(width)} : ${text}`);
    }
  }
  return lines;
}

function canInline(fields: [string, unknown][]): boolean {
  if (fields.length > MAX_INLINE_FIELDS) return false;
  let total = 0;
  for (const [k, v] of fields) {
    if (!isPrimitive(v)) return false;
    const text = inlineValue(v);
    if (text.length > MAX_INLINE_VALUE) return false;
    total += k.length + text.length + 3;
  }
  return total <= MAX_INLINE_TOTAL;
}

/** `GET    /api/v1/x -> 200 (12 ms)` for the per-request log line, plus the fields that are not part of it. */
function requestMessage(
  fields: Record<string, unknown>,
): { message: string; rest: Record<string, unknown> } | undefined {
  const { method, path, status, ms, ...rest } = fields;
  if (typeof method !== 'string' || typeof path !== 'string' || typeof status !== 'number') return undefined;
  const took = typeof ms === 'number' ? ` (${ms} ms)` : '';
  return { message: `${method.padEnd(6)} ${path} -> ${status}${took}`, rest };
}

/** One log entry as text. Multi-line entries end with a blank line so that entries stay visually separate. */
export function formatPretty(entry: LogEntry, color: boolean): string {
  const { file = 'unknown', function: functionName = 'anonymous', ...data } = entry.fields;
  const prefix = `[${formatLocalTime(entry.time)}] [File: ${oneLine(String(file))}] [Function: ${oneLine(String(functionName))}]`;
  const head = `${prefix} | ${BADGE[entry.kind]} | `;
  const request = entry.event === 'request' ? requestMessage(data) : undefined;
  const fields = Object.entries(request ? request.rest : data).filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined);
  const lines: string[] = [];

  if (fields.length === 0 && !request) {
    lines.push(`${head}${entry.event}`);
  } else if (canInline(fields)) {
    const inline = fields.map(([k, v]) => `${k}=${inlineValue(v as Primitive)}`).join('  ');
    const text = [request?.message, inline].filter(Boolean).join('  ');
    lines.push(`${head}${entry.event.padEnd(EVENT_WIDTH)} | ${text}`);
  } else {
    lines.push(
      request ? `${head}${entry.event.padEnd(EVENT_WIDTH)} | ${request.message}` : `${head}${entry.event}`,
    );
    lines.push(...renderBlock(Object.fromEntries(fields), BLOCK_INDENT).map((line) => `${prefix} ${line}`));
    lines.push(''); // blank line after a multi-line entry
  }

  const paint = (line: string): string =>
    color && line !== '' ? `${COLORS[entry.kind]}${line}${RESET}` : line;
  return lines.map(paint).join('\n');
}

/src/utils/logger.ts
import { formatPretty, formatLocalTime, type LogKind } from './log-format.js';
import { appendDailyLog } from '../config/daily-logs.js';

export type LogLevel = 'debug' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'error' | 'silent';
export type LogFormat = 'json' | 'pretty';

const ORDER: Record<LogLevel, number> = { debug: 10, info: 20, warn: 30, error: 40, silent: 100 };
const SENSITIVE = /token|password|authorization|secret|pat$|credential|cookie/i;

export type LogFields = Record<string, unknown>;

export interface Logger {
  debug(event: string, fields?: LogFields): void;
  info(event: string, fields?: LogFields): void;
  /** Something finished successfully (a call, a created resource): shown green in the terminal. */
  success(event: string, fields?: LogFields): void;
  warn(event: string, fields?: LogFields): void;
  error(event: string, fields?: LogFields): void;
  child(bindings: LogFields): Logger;
}

export type LogSink = (line: string) => void;

export interface LoggerOptions {
  source?: { file: string; function: string };
  dailyFiles?: boolean;
  /** `json` (one JSON object per line, for log collectors; the default here) or `pretty` (terminal). */
  format?: LogFormat;
  /** ANSI colours for the pretty format: error red, warn orange, success green, everything else white. */
  color?: boolean;
  /** Clock, replaceable in tests. */
  now?: () => Date;
}

function logSource(error?: Error): { file: string; function: string } {
  for (const frame of (error?.stack ?? new Error().stack ?? '').split('\n').slice(1)) {
    const match = /^\s*at (?:(.*?) \()?(.+?):\d+:\d+\)?$/.exec(frame);
    if (!match) continue;
    const path = match[2]?.replaceAll('\\', '/') ?? '';
    if (/\/(logger\.(?:ts|js))$/.test(path) || path.startsWith('node:')) continue;
    return { file: path.split('/').at(-1) || 'unknown', function: match[1] || 'anonymous' };
  }
  return { file: 'unknown', function: 'anonymous' };
}

export function redact(value: unknown, depth = 0): unknown {
  if (depth > 5 || value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return value;
  if (value instanceof Error) return { name: value.name, message: value.message };
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((v) => redact(v, depth + 1));
  return Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>).map(([k, v]) => [
      k,
      SENSITIVE.test(k) ? '[redacted]' : redact(v, depth + 1),
    ]),
  );
}

export function createLogger(
  level: LogLevel = 'info',
  sink: LogSink = (line) => process.stdout.write(`${line}\n`),
  bindings: LogFields = {},
  options: LoggerOptions = {},
): Logger {
  const format = options.format ?? 'json';
  const now = options.now ?? (() => new Date());

  const write = (kind: LogKind, event: string, fields?: LogFields): void => {
    // `success` is an info-level message that is shown in green
    const severity: Exclude<LogLevel, 'silent'> = kind === 'success' ? 'info' : kind;
    if (ORDER[severity] < ORDER[level]) return;
    const merged = {
      ...(redact(bindings) as LogFields),
      ...(redact(
        fields?.error instanceof Error ? { ...fields, error: fields.error.message } : (fields ?? {}),
      ) as LogFields),
      ...(options.source ?? logSource(fields?.error instanceof Error ? fields.error : undefined)),
    };
    const time = now();
    const timestamp = formatLocalTime(time);
    if (options.dailyFiles)
      appendDailyLog(
        JSON.stringify({ ...merged, time: time.toISOString(), timestamp, level: severity, event }),
        time,
      );
    if (format === 'pretty') {
      sink(formatPretty({ time, kind, event, fields: merged }, options.color === true));
      return;
    }
    sink(
      JSON.stringify({
        time: time.toISOString(),
        timestamp,
        level: severity,
        ...(kind === 'success' ? { outcome: 'success' } : {}),
        event,
        ...merged,
      }),
    );
  };

  return {
    debug: (e, f) => write('debug', e, f),
    info: (e, f) => write('info', e, f),
    success: (e, f) => write('success', e, f),
    warn: (e, f) => write('warn', e, f),
    error: (e, f) => write('error', e, f),
    child: (b) => createLogger(level, sink, { ...bindings, ...b }, options),
  };
}

export const silentLogger: Logger = createLogger('silent');

/src/utils/names.ts
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { BadRequestError } from './errors.js';

const SOEID_RE = /^[a-z0-9]{3,20}$/;
const CSIID_RE = /^\d{1,10}$/;
const DNS_LABEL_RE = /^[a-z0-9]([-a-z0-9]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?$/;
const TOKEN_NAME_RE = /^[a-z0-9]([-a-z0-9]{0,40}[a-z0-9])?$/;

/** Reserve API path names to avoid ambiguous user URLs. */
const RESERVED_SOEIDS = new Set(['api', 'login', 'catalog', 'healthz', 'readyz']);

/** Validate and normalise a user id. User ids end up in namespace names, so they are strictly allow-listed. */
export function parseSoeid(value: string): string {
  const soeid = value.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!SOEID_RE.test(soeid) || RESERVED_SOEIDS.has(soeid)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid soeid', 'INVALID_SOEID');
  }
  return soeid;
}

export function parseCsiid(value: string): string {
  const csiid = value.trim();
  if (!CSIID_RE.test(csiid)) throw new BadRequestError('csiid must be numeric', 'INVALID_CSIID');
  return csiid;
}

export function parseResourceName(value: string, what = 'resource name'): string {
  const name = value.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!DNS_LABEL_RE.test(name)) throw new BadRequestError(`Invalid ${what}`, 'INVALID_NAME');
  return name;
}

export function parseTokenName(value: string): string {
  const name = value.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!TOKEN_NAME_RE.test(name)) throw new BadRequestError('Invalid token name', 'INVALID_TOKEN_NAME');
  return name;
}

export const workspaceNamespace = (soeid: string, csiid: string): string => `${soeid}-devspaces-${csiid}`;
export const deploymentNamespace = (soeid: string, csiid: string): string =>
  `${soeid}-devspaces-deploy-${csiid}`;

export function csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace(namespace: string): string | undefined {
  return /-devspaces-(\d+)$/.exec(namespace)?.[1];
}

export function csiidFromDeploymentNamespace(namespace: string): string | undefined {
  return /-devspaces-deploy-(\d+)$/.exec(namespace)?.[1];
}

/** `https://api.<cluster>.ecs.dyn…:6443` → `<cluster>`. */
export function extractClusterName(url: string | undefined): string {
  const match = /(?:api\.|apps\.)([^.]+)\.ecs/.exec(url ?? '');
  return match?.[1] ?? 'unknown-cluster';
}

export const randomSuffix = (): string => randomBytes(2).toString('hex');

/** DNS-1123 workspace name `<stack>-<suffix>`, at most 63 characters. */
export function cleanWorkspaceName(base: string, suffix: string = randomSuffix()): string {
  const cleaned =
    base
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9-]+/g, '-')
      .replace(/-{2,}/g, '-')
      .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
      .slice(0, 63 - suffix.length - 1)
      .replace(/-+$/g, '') || 'workspace';
  return `${cleaned}-${suffix}`;
}

/src/utils/resources.ts
export interface ResourceSummary {
  limitcpu: number;
  limitmemory: number;
  requestcpu: number;
  requestmemory: number;
  storage: number;
}

export const EMPTY_RESOURCES: ResourceSummary = {
  limitcpu: 0,
  limitmemory: 0,
  requestcpu: 0,
  requestmemory: 0,
  storage: 0,
};

const round3 = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000;

/** CPU quantity to cores: `500m` → 0.5, `2` → 2. Unknown or empty → 0. */
export function parseCpu(quantity: string | number | undefined | null): number {
  if (quantity === undefined || quantity === null || quantity === '') return 0;
  if (typeof quantity === 'number') return Number.isFinite(quantity) ? round3(quantity) : 0;
  const q = quantity.trim();
  const value = parseFloat(q);
  if (Number.isNaN(value)) return 0;
  return round3(q.endsWith('m') ? value / 1000 : value);
}

const BYTES: Record<string, number> = {
  Ki: 1024,
  Mi: 1024 ** 2,
  Gi: 1024 ** 3,
  Ti: 1024 ** 4,
  K: 1e3,
  M: 1e6,
  G: 1e9,
  T: 1e12,
};

/** Memory/storage quantity to GiB: `512Mi` → 0.5, `2Gi` → 2, plain numbers are bytes. */
export function parseGi(quantity: string | number | undefined | null): number {
  if (quantity === undefined || quantity === null || quantity === '') return 0;
  const q = String(quantity).trim();
  const match = /^([0-9]*\.?[0-9]+)\s*([A-Za-z]*)$/.exec(q);
  if (!match) return 0;
  const value = parseFloat(match[1] ?? '0');
  const unit = match[2] ?? '';
  if (unit === '') return round3(value / 1024 ** 3);
  const factor = BYTES[unit];
  return factor === undefined ? 0 : round3((value * factor) / 1024 ** 3);
}

interface ContainerLike {
  name?: string;
  container?: {
    cpuLimit?: string;
    cpuRequest?: string;
    memoryLimit?: string;
    memoryRequest?: string;
  };
  volume?: { size?: string };
}

/**
 * Total resources a workspace asks for. The editor component (`checode`) is excluded because it is
 * injected by Dev Spaces and not chosen by the user's stack.
 */
export function summarizeComponents(
  components: readonly ContainerLike[] | undefined,
  excludedNames: readonly string[] = ['checode'],
): ResourceSummary {
  const total = { ...EMPTY_RESOURCES };
  for (const component of components ?? []) {
    if (component.name && excludedNames.includes(component.name)) continue;
    if (component.container) {
      total.limitcpu += parseCpu(component.container.cpuLimit);
      total.requestcpu += parseCpu(component.container.cpuRequest);
      total.limitmemory += parseGi(component.container.memoryLimit);
      total.requestmemory += parseGi(component.container.memoryRequest);
    }
    if (component.volume) total.storage += parseGi(component.volume.size);
  }
  return {
    limitcpu: round3(total.limitcpu),
    limitmemory: round3(total.limitmemory),
    requestcpu: round3(total.requestcpu),
    requestmemory: round3(total.requestmemory),
    storage: round3(total.storage),
  };
}

/src/utils/timestamp.ts
const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
  month: '2-digit',
  day: '2-digit',
  year: '2-digit',
  hour: '2-digit',
  minute: '2-digit',
  second: '2-digit',
  hourCycle: 'h23',
  timeZone: 'America/Chicago',
});

/** `MM-DD-YY HH:mm:ss CST` in the America/Chicago zone (for logs and emails only). */
export function getFormattedTimestampCST(date: Date = new Date()): string {
  const parts = Object.fromEntries(formatter.formatToParts(date).map((p) => [p.type, p.value]));
  return `${parts.month}-${parts.day}-${parts.year} ${parts.hour}:${parts.minute}:${parts.second} CST`;
}

const plural = (n: number, unit: string): string => `${n} ${unit}${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;

/** `45 seconds`, `3 minutes 5 seconds`, `2 hours 10 minutes`. */
export function formatDuration(totalSeconds: number): string {
  const seconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor(totalSeconds));
  if (seconds < 60) return plural(seconds, 'second');
  if (seconds < 3600) {
    const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    const rest = seconds % 60;
    return rest > 0 ? `${plural(minutes, 'minute')} ${plural(rest, 'second')}` : plural(minutes, 'minute');
  }
  const hours = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const minutes = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
  return minutes > 0 ? `${plural(hours, 'hour')} ${plural(minutes, 'minute')}` : plural(hours, 'hour');
}

export function durationSince(from: Date | string | undefined, now: Date = new Date()): string {
  if (!from) return '0 seconds';
  const start = typeof from === 'string' ? new Date(from) : from;
  if (Number.isNaN(start.getTime())) return '0 seconds';
  return formatDuration((now.getTime() - start.getTime()) / 1000);
}

/tests/e2e/journeys.test.ts
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import { afterAll, afterEach, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  caller,
  startControllerSimulation,
  startE2E,
  waitFor,
  type Caller,
  type E2E,
} from '../helpers/e2e.js';
import { SAMPLE_PAT } from '../helpers/sample-devfile.js';
import { TEST_SECRET } from '../helpers/testkit.js';

let e2e: E2E;
let call: Caller;
let stopController: () => void;

beforeAll(async () => {
  e2e = await startE2E();
  ({ call } = await e2e.login());
});
afterAll(() => e2e.stop());
beforeEach(async () => {
  e2e.fakes.kube.namespaces.clear();
  e2e.fakes.kube.objects.clear();
  e2e.fakes.metrics.records.length = 0;
  e2e.fakes.notifier.created.length = 0;
  e2e.fakes.notifier.existing.length = 0;
  e2e.fakes.notifier.deleted.length = 0;
  e2e.registry.failing = false;
  await e2e.sql('DELETE FROM metrics."devspaces-metrics"');
  stopController = startControllerSimulation(e2e.fakes.kube);
});
afterEach(() => stopController());

const events = async () =>
  (await e2e.sql('SELECT eventtype, resourcetype FROM metrics."devspaces-metrics" ORDER BY id')).map(
    (r) => `${r.eventtype}:${r.resourcetype}`,
  );

describe('journey: a developer’s day', () => {
  it('logs in, picks a stack, works, stops, resumes and cleans up', async () => {
    // Orion shows the stacks
    const catalog = await call('GET', '/api/v1/catalog');
    expect(catalog.status).toBe(200);
    const stack = catalog.body[0].name as string;
    expect(stack).toBe('java-maven');

    // Create (the project does not exist yet, so it is created too)
    const created = await call('POST', '/api/v1/pp58386/workspaces', {
      devworkspaceValue: stack,
      csiid: '177398',
    });
    expect(created.status).toBe(200);
    const id = created.body.id as string;
    expect(created.body.namespace).toBe('pp58386-devspaces-177398');

    // The controller brings it up
    const running = await waitFor('workspace Running', async () => {
      const ws = await call('GET', `/api/v1/pp58386/workspaces/${id}`);
      return ws.body.phase === 'Running' ? ws.body : undefined;
    });
    expect(running.mainUrl).toBe(`https://${id}.apps.test/`);

    // It appears in the list and in the project list
    expect((await call('GET', '/api/v1/pp58386/workspaces')).body).toHaveLength(1);
    expect((await call('GET', '/api/v1/pp58386/projects')).body[0]).toMatchObject({ csiid: '177398' });

    // Stop after an hour of work
    e2e.fakes.clock.advance(60 * 60 * 1000);
    expect((await call('POST', `/api/v1/pp58386/workspaces/${id}/stop`)).status).toBe(200);
    await waitFor(
      'workspace Stopped',
      async () => (await call('GET', `/api/v1/pp58386/workspaces/${id}`)).body.phase === 'Stopped',
    );

    // Resume
    expect((await call('POST', `/api/v1/pp58386/workspaces/${id}/start`)).status).toBe(200);
    await waitFor(
      'workspace Running again',
      async () => (await call('GET', `/api/v1/pp58386/workspaces/${id}`)).body.phase === 'Running',
    );

    // Delete
    expect((await call('DELETE', `/api/v1/pp58386/workspaces/${id}`)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await call('GET', '/api/v1/pp58386/workspaces')).body).toEqual([]);
    expect(e2e.fakes.kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(0);

    // Everything was recorded for the dashboard, in order
    expect(await events()).toEqual([
      'create:project',
      'create:workspace',
      'stop:workspace',
      'start:workspace',
      'delete:workspace',
    ]);
    const stop = (
      await e2e.sql(`SELECT duration FROM metrics."devspaces-metrics" WHERE eventtype = 'stop'`)
    )[0];
    expect(stop?.duration).toBe('1 hour');
  });

  it('keeps a developer’s tooling in sync: git config, PAT, deployment project', async () => {
    await call('POST', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces', { devworkspaceValue: 'python', csiid: '5' });

    expect(
      (await call('PUT', '/api/v1/pp1/gitconfig', { csiid: '5', content: '[user]\n  name = Pat\n' })).status,
    ).toBe(200);
    expect((await call('POST', '/api/v1/pp1/pats/main', { csiid: '5', token: SAMPLE_PAT })).status).toBe(201);
    const pats = await call('GET', '/api/v1/pp1/pats');
    expect(pats.body).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ name: 'main', namespace: 'pp1-devspaces-5' })]);

    const deploy = await call('POST', '/api/v1/pp1/deployment-projects', { csiid: '5' });
    expect(deploy.status).toBe(201);
    expect(e2e.fakes.notifier.created).toHaveLength(1);
    expect((await call('GET', '/api/v1/pp1/deployment-projects')).body).toEqual(['pp1-devspaces-deploy-5']);

    expect((await call('DELETE', '/api/v1/pp1/deployment-projects')).body.deleted).toEqual([
      'pp1-devspaces-deploy-5',
    ]);
    expect(e2e.fakes.notifier.deleted).toHaveLength(1);

    expect(await events()).toEqual([
      'create:project',
      'create:workspace',
      'create:gitconfig',
      'create:pat',
      'create:deployment_namespace',
      'delete:deployment_namespace',
    ]);
    // and no secret ever reached the metrics table
    expect(JSON.stringify(await e2e.sql('SELECT * FROM metrics."devspaces-metrics"'))).not.toContain(
      SAMPLE_PAT,
    );
  });
});

describe('journey: several users share the platform', () => {
  it('isolates users from each other', async () => {
    const a = await call('POST', '/api/v1/alice1/workspaces', {
      devworkspaceValue: 'java-maven',
      csiid: '1',
    });
    const b = await call('POST', '/api/v1/bob22/workspaces', { devworkspaceValue: 'python', csiid: '2' });
    expect((await call('GET', '/api/v1/alice1/workspaces')).body.map((w: { id: string }) => w.id)).toEqual([
      a.body.id,
    ]);
    expect((await call('GET', '/api/v1/bob22/workspaces')).body.map((w: { id: string }) => w.id)).toEqual([
      b.body.id,
    ]);
    expect((await call('DELETE', `/api/v1/bob22/workspaces/${a.body.id}`)).status).toBe(404);
    expect(e2e.fakes.kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('a user token reaches only its own resources', async () => {
    const userToken = jwt.sign({ role: 'user' }, TEST_SECRET, { subject: 'alice1' });
    const asAlice = caller(e2e.baseUrl, userToken);
    expect((await asAlice('GET', '/api/v1/alice1/workspaces')).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await asAlice('GET', '/api/v1/bob22/workspaces')).status).toBe(403);
    expect(
      (await asAlice('POST', '/api/v1/bob22/workspaces', { devworkspaceValue: 'python', csiid: '2' })).status,
    ).toBe(403);
    expect(e2e.fakes.kube.namespaces.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it('enforces the per-user limits while other users are unaffected', async () => {
    const make = (user: string, csiid: string) =>
      call('POST', `/api/v1/${user}/workspaces`, { devworkspaceValue: 'python', csiid });
    stopController(); // keep workspaces "active" so the running limit bites
    expect((await make('alice1', '1')).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await make('alice1', '1')).status).toBe(200);
    const blocked = await make('alice1', '1');
    expect(blocked.status).toBe(409);
    expect(blocked.body.code).toBe('WORKSPACE_RUN_COUNT_EXCEEDED');
    expect((await make('bob22', '2')).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it('survives two simultaneous first requests for a brand-new project', async () => {
    stopController();
    const [x, y] = await Promise.all([
      call('POST', '/api/v1/carol3/workspaces', { devworkspaceValue: 'python', csiid: '3' }),
      call('POST', '/api/v1/carol3/workspaces', { devworkspaceValue: 'python', csiid: '3' }),
    ]);
    expect([x.status, y.status]).toEqual([200, 200]);
    expect(e2e.fakes.kube.workspaces('carol3-devspaces-3')).toHaveLength(2);
    expect([...e2e.fakes.kube.namespaces.keys()]).toEqual(['carol3-devspaces-3']);
  });
});

describe('journey: things go wrong', () => {
  it('unauthenticated callers get nothing', async () => {
    for (const path of [
      '/api/v1/catalog',
      '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces',
      '/api/v1/pp1/pats',
      '/api/v1/pp1/deployment-projects',
    ]) {
      expect((await e2e.anonymous('GET', path)).status).toBe(401);
    }
    expect((await e2e.anonymous('POST', '/api/v1/login', { username: 'x', password: 'y' })).status).toBe(401);
  });

  it('a registry outage is a clean 502 that creates nothing and is recorded', async () => {
    e2e.registry.failing = true;
    const res = await call('POST', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces', { devworkspaceValue: 'java-maven', csiid: '1' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(502);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'UPSTREAM_ERROR' });
    expect(e2e.fakes.kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(0);
    // recovery without restarting anything
    e2e.registry.failing = false;
    expect(
      (await call('POST', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces', { devworkspaceValue: 'java-maven', csiid: '1' })).status,
    ).toBe(200);
  });

  it('a cluster failure while creating a workspace is recorded as a failed event', async () => {
    e2e.fakes.kube.failOnce(
      'create:DevWorkspace',
      Object.assign(new Error('admission webhook denied the request'), { code: 500 }),
    );
    const res = await call('POST', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces', { devworkspaceValue: 'java-maven', csiid: '1' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(500);
    const [row] = await e2e.sql(
      `SELECT status FROM metrics."devspaces-metrics" WHERE resourcetype = 'workspace'`,
    );
    expect(row?.status).toMatchObject({ failed: true });
    expect(JSON.stringify(row?.status)).toContain('admission webhook denied');
  });

  it('a metrics database outage never blocks the user', async () => {
    // The harness uses pg-mem, so simulate the outage by breaking the recorder's store.
    const original = e2e.container.metrics.insert.bind(e2e.container.metrics);
    e2e.container.metrics.insert = async () => {
      throw new Error('connection to metrics database lost');
    };
    try {
      const res = await call('POST', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces', { devworkspaceValue: 'python', csiid: '1' });
      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      expect((await call('GET', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces')).body).toHaveLength(1);
      expect((await e2e.anonymous('GET', '/api/v1/healthz')).status).toBe(200);
    } finally {
      e2e.container.metrics.insert = original;
    }
  });

  it('readiness reflects the cluster, liveness does not', async () => {
    expect((await e2e.anonymous('GET', '/api/v1/readyz')).status).toBe(200);
    e2e.fakes.kube.failOnce('ping', new Error('api server down'));
    const down = await e2e.anonymous('GET', '/api/v1/readyz');
    expect(down.status).toBe(503);
    expect(down.body.checks.kubernetes).toBe('unavailable');
    expect((await e2e.anonymous('GET', '/api/v1/healthz')).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it('answers malformed requests with 400s, not crashes', async () => {
    const bad = await fetch(`${e2e.baseUrl}/api/v1/pp1/workspaces`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', authorization: `Bearer ${(await e2e.login()).token}` },
      body: '{"devworkspaceValue":',
    });
    expect(bad.status).toBe(400);
    expect(((await bad.json()) as { code: string }).code).toBe('INVALID_JSON');
    expect(
      (await call('POST', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces', { devworkspaceValue: '../../etc/passwd', csiid: '1' }))
        .status,
    ).toBe(400);
    expect((await call('GET', '/api/v1/..%2F..%2Fetc/workspaces')).status).toBe(400);
  });
});

describe('journey: versioned API only', () => {
  it('rejects old paths and provisions through v1', async () => {
    expect((await e2e.anonymous('POST', '/login', {})).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await call('GET', '/pp1/workspaces')).status).toBe(404);
    const project = await call('POST', '/api/v1/pp1/projects', { csiid: '7' });
    expect(project.status).toBe(201);
    expect(
      (await call('PUT', '/api/v1/support/resources', { soeid: 'pp1', csiid: '7', target: 'workspace' }))
        .status,
    ).toBe(200);
    const settings = await call('PATCH', '/api/v1/configuration', { MAX_RUNNING_WORKSPACES: 1 });
    expect(settings.status).toBe(200);
    expect((await call('GET', '/api/v1/logs?days=1')).status).toBe(200);
  });
});

/tests/helpers/InMemoryKube.ts
import type { DevWorkspaceResource, KubeObject } from '../../src/types.js';
import type { KubeGateway, NamespacedKind } from '../../src/infra/ports.js';
import { ConflictError, NotFoundError } from '../../src/utils/errors.js';

function matches(labels: Record<string, string> | undefined, selector?: string): boolean {
  if (!selector) return true;
  return selector.split(',').every((term) => {
    const [key, value] = term.split('=');
    return key !== undefined && labels?.[key] === value;
  });
}

/** A tiny stand-in for a cluster: same contract as KubeClient, no network. */
export class InMemoryKube implements KubeGateway {
  readonly namespaces = new Map<string, KubeObject>();
  readonly objects = new Map<string, KubeObject>();
  readonly calls: string[] = [];
  private readonly failures = new Map<string, Error>();
  private version = 1;

  constructor(private readonly now: () => Date = () => new Date()) {}

  /** Make the next call of `operation` (e.g. `create:DevWorkspace`, `createNamespace`) throw. */
  failOnce(operation: string, error: Error): void {
    this.failures.set(operation, error);
  }

  private enter(operation: string): void {
    this.calls.push(operation);
    const failure = this.failures.get(operation);
    if (failure) {
      this.failures.delete(operation);
      throw failure;
    }
  }

  private stamp<T extends KubeObject>(body: T, namespace?: string): T {
    const copy = structuredClone(body);
    copy.metadata = {
      ...copy.metadata,
      ...(namespace ? { namespace } : {}),
      creationTimestamp: copy.metadata?.creationTimestamp ?? this.now().toISOString(),
      resourceVersion: String(this.version++),
      uid: copy.metadata?.uid ?? `uid-${this.version}`,
    };
    return copy;
  }

  private key(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string): string {
    return `${kind}/${namespace}/${name}`;
  }

  // ---- namespaces --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  async listNamespaces(labelSelector?: string): Promise<KubeObject[]> {
    this.enter('listNamespaces');
    return [...this.namespaces.values()]
      .filter((ns) => matches(ns.metadata?.labels, labelSelector))
      .map((ns) => structuredClone(ns));
  }

  async getNamespace(name: string): Promise<KubeObject | null> {
    this.enter('getNamespace');
    const ns = this.namespaces.get(name);
    return ns ? structuredClone(ns) : null;
  }

  async createNamespace(body: KubeObject): Promise<KubeObject> {
    this.enter('createNamespace');
    const name = body.metadata?.name ?? '';
    if (this.namespaces.has(name)) throw new ConflictError(`namespace ${name} already exists`);
    const created = this.stamp({ ...body, apiVersion: 'v1', kind: 'Namespace' });
    this.namespaces.set(name, created);
    return structuredClone(created);
  }

  async deleteNamespace(name: string): Promise<boolean> {
    this.enter('deleteNamespace');
    const existed = this.namespaces.delete(name);
    for (const key of [...this.objects.keys()]) if (key.split('/')[1] === name) this.objects.delete(key);
    return existed;
  }

  async createProject(body: KubeObject): Promise<KubeObject> {
    this.enter('createProject');
    const name = body.metadata?.name ?? '';
    if (this.namespaces.has(name)) throw new ConflictError(`project ${name} already exists`);
    const created = this.stamp({ ...body, apiVersion: 'project.openshift.io/v1', kind: 'Project' });
    this.namespaces.set(name, created);
    return structuredClone(created);
  }

  // ---- namespaced objects ------------------------------------------------------------------
  async get<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string): Promise<T | null> {
    this.enter(`get:${kind}`);
    const found = this.objects.get(this.key(kind, namespace, name));
    return found ? (structuredClone(found) as T) : null;
  }

  async list<T extends KubeObject>(
    kind: NamespacedKind,
    namespace: string,
    labelSelector?: string,
  ): Promise<T[]> {
    this.enter(`list:${kind}`);
    return [...this.objects.entries()]
      .filter(([key]) => key.startsWith(`${kind}/${namespace}/`))
      .map(([, obj]) => obj)
      .filter((obj) => matches(obj.metadata?.labels, labelSelector))
      .map((obj) => structuredClone(obj) as T);
  }

  async create<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, body: T): Promise<T> {
    this.enter(`create:${kind}`);
    if (!this.namespaces.has(namespace)) throw new NotFoundError(`namespace ${namespace} not found`);
    const name = body.metadata?.name ?? '';
    const key = this.key(kind, namespace, name);
    if (this.objects.has(key)) throw new ConflictError(`${kind} ${name} already exists`);
    const created = this.stamp({ ...body, kind }, namespace);
    this.objects.set(key, created);
    return structuredClone(created) as T;
  }

  async apply<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, body: T): Promise<T> {
    this.enter(`apply:${kind}`);
    if (!this.namespaces.has(namespace)) throw new NotFoundError(`namespace ${namespace} not found`);
    const name = body.metadata?.name ?? '';
    const key = this.key(kind, namespace, name);
    const previous = this.objects.get(key);
    const applied = this.stamp(
      {
        ...body,
        kind,
        metadata: { ...body.metadata, creationTimestamp: previous?.metadata?.creationTimestamp },
      },
      namespace,
    );
    this.objects.set(key, applied);
    return structuredClone(applied) as T;
  }

  async delete(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string): Promise<boolean> {
    this.enter(`delete:${kind}`);
    return this.objects.delete(this.key(kind, namespace, name));
  }

  async setWorkspaceStarted(
    namespace: string,
    name: string,
    started: boolean,
  ): Promise<DevWorkspaceResource> {
    this.enter('setWorkspaceStarted');
    const key = this.key('DevWorkspace', namespace, name);
    const dw = this.objects.get(key) as DevWorkspaceResource | undefined;
    if (!dw) throw new NotFoundError(`DevWorkspace ${name} not found`);
    dw.spec = { ...dw.spec, started };
    dw.status = { ...dw.status, phase: started ? 'Starting' : 'Stopping' };
    return structuredClone(dw);
  }

  async ping(): Promise<void> {
    this.enter('ping');
  }

  // ---- test helpers ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  /** Simulate the DevWorkspace controller moving a workspace to a phase. */
  setPhase(namespace: string, name: string, phase: string, mainUrl?: string): void {
    const dw = this.objects.get(this.key('DevWorkspace', namespace, name)) as
      DevWorkspaceResource | undefined;
    if (!dw) throw new Error(`no such DevWorkspace ${namespace}/${name}`);
    dw.status = {
      ...dw.status,
      phase,
      ...(mainUrl ? { mainUrl } : {}),
      conditions: [
        {
          type: 'Started',
          status: phase === 'Running' ? 'True' : 'False',
          lastTransitionTime: this.now().toISOString(),
        },
      ],
    };
  }

  workspaces(namespace?: string): DevWorkspaceResource[] {
    return [...this.objects.entries()]
      .filter(([key]) => key.startsWith('DevWorkspace/') && (!namespace || key.split('/')[1] === namespace))
      .map(([, obj]) => obj as DevWorkspaceResource);
  }

  seedNamespace(name: string, soeid: string, csiid = '1'): void {
    this.namespaces.set(name, {
      apiVersion: 'v1',
      kind: 'Namespace',
      metadata: {
        name,
        labels: { 'app.kubernetes.io/component': 'workspaces-namespace', 'devspaces.citi.com/csiid': csiid },
        annotations: { 'che.eclipse.org/username': soeid },
        creationTimestamp: this.now().toISOString(),
      },
    });
  }
}

/tests/helpers/e2e.ts
import { buildContainer, type Container } from '../../src/container.js';
import { DevfileRegistryClient, createRegistryHttp } from '../../src/infra/devreg/DevfileRegistryClient.js';
import { silentLogger } from '../../src/utils/logger.js';
import { startRegistryServer, type RegistryServer } from './registry-server.js';
import {
  ADMIN,
  listen,
  makeConfig,
  makeFakes,
  makePgMemRepository,
  type Fakes,
  type TestServer,
} from './testkit.js';
import type { InMemoryKube } from './InMemoryKube.js';

// Response bodies are inspected ad hoc by the journey tests, so they are deliberately loosely typed.
// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
export interface ApiResult<T = any> {
  status: number;
  body: T;
  headers: Headers;
}

export interface E2E {
  baseUrl: string;
  container: Container;
  fakes: Fakes;
  registry: RegistryServer;
  sql: (query: string) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>[]>;
  /** Log in over HTTP and return an API caller bound to the resulting token. */
  login(username?: string, password?: string): Promise<{ token: string; call: Caller }>;
  anonymous: Caller;
  stop(): Promise<void>;
}

export type Caller = (method: string, path: string, body?: unknown) => Promise<ApiResult>;

function caller(baseUrl: string, token?: string): Caller {
  return async (method, path, body) => {
    const res = await fetch(`${baseUrl}${path}`, {
      method,
      headers: {
        ...(body === undefined ? {} : { 'content-type': 'application/json' }),
        ...(token ? { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      },
      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
    });
    const text = await res.text();
    let parsed: unknown = text;
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(text);
    } catch {
      /* plain text (e.g. /api/v1/healthz) */
    }
    return { status: res.status, body: parsed, headers: res.headers };
  };
}

/** Plays the part of the Dev Spaces controller: Starting → Running, Stopping → Stopped. */
export function startControllerSimulation(kube: InMemoryKube, intervalMs = 5): () => void {
  const timer = setInterval(() => {
    for (const dw of kube.workspaces()) {
      const ns = dw.metadata?.namespace ?? '';
      const name = dw.metadata?.name ?? '';
      if (dw.status?.phase === 'Stopping') kube.setPhase(ns, name, 'Stopped');
      else if (!dw.status?.phase || dw.status.phase === 'Starting') {
        kube.setPhase(ns, name, 'Running', `https://${name}.apps.test/`);
      }
    }
  }, intervalMs);
  return () => clearInterval(timer);
}

export async function waitFor<T>(
  what: string,
  probe: () => Promise<T | undefined | false> | T | undefined | false,
  timeoutMs = 3000,
): Promise<T> {
  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
  for (;;) {
    const value = await probe();
    if (value) return value;
    if (Date.now() > deadline) throw new Error(`timed out waiting for ${what}`);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 10));
  }
}

export async function startE2E(env: Record<string, string> = {}): Promise<E2E> {
  const registry = await startRegistryServer();
  const config = makeConfig({ DEVREG_URL: registry.url, ...env });
  const fakes = makeFakes();
  const db = await makePgMemRepository();
  const container = buildContainer(config, silentLogger, {
    kube: fakes.kube,
    metrics: db.repo,
    registry: new DevfileRegistryClient(createRegistryHttp(registry.url)),
    notifier: fakes.notifier,
    clock: fakes.clock,
  });
  const server: TestServer = await listen(container);
  const anonymous = caller(server.baseUrl);
  return {
    baseUrl: server.baseUrl,
    container,
    fakes,
    registry,
    sql: db.query,
    anonymous,
    async login(username = ADMIN.username, password = ADMIN.password) {
      const res = await anonymous('POST', '/api/v1/login', { username, password });
      if (res.status !== 200) throw new Error(`login failed: ${res.status}`);
      return { token: res.body.token as string, call: caller(server.baseUrl, res.body.token as string) };
    },
    async stop() {
      await server.close();
      await registry.close();
    },
  };
}

export { caller };

/tests/helpers/fakes.ts
import type { CatalogEntry, MetricsRecord } from '../../src/types.js';
import type {
  Clock,
  DevfileRegistry,
  MetricsStore,
  NamespaceCreatedEmail,
  NamespaceExistsEmail,
  NamespacesDeletedEmail,
  Notifier,
} from '../../src/infra/ports.js';
import { WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError } from '../../src/utils/errors.js';
import { SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML } from './sample-devfile.js';

export class FakeMetricsStore implements MetricsStore {
  readonly records: MetricsRecord[] = [];
  failWith?: Error;
  delayMs = 0;
  closed = false;

  async insert(record: MetricsRecord): Promise<void> {
    if (this.delayMs) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, this.delayMs));
    if (this.failWith) throw this.failWith;
    this.records.push(record);
  }
  async ping(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.failWith) throw this.failWith;
  }
  async close(): Promise<void> {
    this.closed = true;
  }
}

export class FakeNotifier implements Notifier {
  readonly created: NamespaceCreatedEmail[] = [];
  readonly existing: NamespaceExistsEmail[] = [];
  readonly deleted: NamespacesDeletedEmail[] = [];
  async namespaceCreated(e: NamespaceCreatedEmail): Promise<void> {
    this.created.push(e);
  }
  async namespaceAlreadyExists(e: NamespaceExistsEmail): Promise<void> {
    this.existing.push(e);
  }
  async namespacesDeleted(e: NamespacesDeletedEmail): Promise<void> {
    this.deleted.push(e);
  }
}

export class FakeRegistry implements DevfileRegistry {
  catalog: CatalogEntry[] = [
    {
      name: 'java-maven',
      displayName: 'Java Maven',
      description: 'Java 17 with Maven',
      versions: [{ version: '1.0.0' }, { version: '2.0.0', default: true }],
    },
    { name: 'python', displayName: 'Python', versions: [{ version: '1.0.0', default: true }] },
  ];
  yaml = SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML;
  failCatalog?: Error;

  async getCatalog(): Promise<CatalogEntry[]> {
    if (this.failCatalog) throw this.failCatalog;
    return this.catalog;
  }

  async getDevfile(stack: string, version?: string): Promise<{ yaml: string; version: string }> {
    const entry = (await this.getCatalog()).find((e) => e.name === stack);
    if (!entry) throw new WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError(`No such workspace config: ${stack}`);
    const versions = entry.versions ?? [];
    const chosen = version
      ? versions.find((v) => v.version === version)
      : (versions.find((v) => v.default) ?? versions[0]);
    if (!chosen) throw new WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError(`No version '${version}' for ${stack}`);
    return { yaml: this.yaml, version: chosen.version };
  }
}

export class FakeClock implements Clock {
  constructor(private current: Date = new Date('2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z')) {}
  now(): Date {
    return new Date(this.current);
  }
  advance(ms: number): void {
    this.current = new Date(this.current.getTime() + ms);
  }
  async sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    this.advance(ms);
  }
}

/tests/helpers/integration.ts
import type { Express } from 'express';
import request from 'supertest';
import { buildContainer, type Container } from '../../src/container.js';
import { createApp } from '../../src/app.js';
import { signToken } from '../../src/http/auth.js';
import { DevfileRegistryClient, createRegistryHttp } from '../../src/infra/devreg/DevfileRegistryClient.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../../src/utils/logger.js';
import { makeConfig, makeFakes, makePgMemRepository, type Fakes } from './testkit.js';
import { startRegistryServer, type RegistryServer } from './registry-server.js';
import type { InMemoryKube } from './InMemoryKube.js';

export interface Integration {
  app: Express;
  container: Container;
  fakes: Fakes;
  registry: RegistryServer;
  token: string;
  /** Run SQL against the in-memory metrics database. */
  sql: (query: string) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>[]>;
  /** Rows of the metrics table, oldest first. */
  metricsRows: () => Promise<Record<string, unknown>[]>;
  api: (method: 'get' | 'post' | 'put' | 'delete', path: string) => request.Test;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

/**
 * Real routes, services, SQL (pg-mem) and HTTP devfile registry; only the cluster is in memory.
 */
export async function startIntegration(
  env: Record<string, string> = {},
  options: { deployKube?: InMemoryKube; logger?: Logger } = {},
): Promise<Integration> {
  const registry = await startRegistryServer();
  const config = makeConfig({ DEVREG_URL: registry.url, ...env });
  const fakes = makeFakes();
  const db = await makePgMemRepository();
  const container = buildContainer(config, options.logger ?? silentLogger, {
    kube: fakes.kube,
    deployKube: options.deployKube,
    metrics: db.repo,
    registry: new DevfileRegistryClient(createRegistryHttp(registry.url)),
    notifier: fakes.notifier,
    clock: fakes.clock,
  });
  const app = createApp(container);
  const token = signToken(config.auth, { soeid: config.auth.adminUsername, role: 'admin' });
  return {
    app,
    container,
    fakes,
    registry,
    token,
    sql: db.query,
    metricsRows: () => db.query('SELECT * FROM metrics."devspaces-metrics" ORDER BY id'),
    api: (method, path) => request(app)[method](path).set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`),
    close: () => registry.close(),
  };
}

/tests/helpers/metrics-table.sql
-- TEST FIXTURE ONLY. The real metrics table already exists in the Orion Postgres database and is owned by
-- its DBA: workspace-mgr never creates or alters tables. This file only gives the in-memory test database
-- (pg-mem) a table of the same shape. Not shipped, not run by the application.
-- Metrics table written by workspace-mgr (one row per action, success or failure).
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS metrics;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS metrics."devspaces-metrics" (
  id               BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  "timestamp"      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  csiid            VARCHAR(32),
  soeid            VARCHAR(64),
  eventtype        VARCHAR(32)  NOT NULL,   -- create | delete | start | stop | update | view
  resourcename     VARCHAR(255),
  resourcetype     VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,   -- workspace | project | deployment_namespace | pat | gitconfig | configmap ...
  status           JSONB        NOT NULL DEFAULT '{"failed": false, "error": "", "message": ""}',
  limitcpu         NUMERIC(10, 3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  limitmemory      NUMERIC(12, 3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  requestcpu       NUMERIC(10, 3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  requestmemory    NUMERIC(12, 3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  duration         VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL DEFAULT '0 seconds',
  storage          NUMERIC(12, 3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  useraction       VARCHAR(32)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'user',  -- user | system
  workspaceversion VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  editorname       VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  errordetail      TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS devspaces_metrics_ts_idx   ON metrics."devspaces-metrics" ("timestamp");
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS devspaces_metrics_soeid_idx ON metrics."devspaces-metrics" (soeid);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS devspaces_metrics_event_idx ON metrics."devspaces-metrics" (eventtype, resourcetype);

/tests/helpers/registry-server.ts
import http from 'node:http';
import type { AddressInfo } from 'node:net';
import { SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML } from './sample-devfile.js';

export interface RegistryServer {
  url: string;
  requests: string[];
  failing: boolean;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

const CATALOG = [
  {
    name: 'java-maven',
    displayName: 'Java Maven',
    description: 'Java 17 with Maven',
    versions: [{ version: '1.0.0' }, { version: '2.0.0', default: true }],
  },
  { name: 'python', displayName: 'Python', versions: [{ version: '1.0.0', default: true }] },
];

/** A real HTTP server speaking the two devfile-registry endpoints workspace-mgr uses. */
export async function startRegistryServer(devfileYaml = SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML): Promise<RegistryServer> {
  const state: RegistryServer = { url: '', requests: [], failing: false, close: async () => undefined };
  const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
    state.requests.push(`${req.method} ${req.url}`);
    if (state.failing) {
      res.writeHead(503).end('registry unavailable');
      return;
    }
    if (req.url === '/v2index/all') {
      res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' }).end(JSON.stringify(CATALOG));
      return;
    }
    const match = /^\/devfiles\/([^/]+)\/([^/]+)$/.exec(req.url ?? '');
    const entry = match && CATALOG.find((c) => c.name === decodeURIComponent(match[1] ?? ''));
    if (entry && entry.versions.some((v) => v.version === decodeURIComponent(match?.[2] ?? ''))) {
      res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'text/plain' }).end(devfileYaml);
      return;
    }
    res.writeHead(404).end('not found');
  });
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve));
  state.url = `http://127.0.0.1:${(server.address() as AddressInfo).port}`;
  state.close = () => new Promise((resolve, reject) => server.close((e) => (e ? reject(e) : resolve())));
  return state;
}

/** A stack shaped like the ones in the devfile registry: tools container, editor mount, venv and home volumes. */
export const SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML = `schemaVersion: 2.2.0

  name: java-maven

  description: Java 17 with Maven

    exec:
      component: tools
      commandLine: mvn package
events:
  postStart:
    - build
components:
  - name: tools
    container:
      image: registry.example.net/udi:latest
      memoryLimit: 4Gi
      memoryRequest: 1Gi
      cpuLimit: 2000m
      cpuRequest: 500m
      env:
        - name: FOO
          value: bar
      endpoints:
        - name: http
          targetPort: 8080
      volumeMounts:
        - name: venv
          path: /home/user/.venv
        - name: checode
          path: /checode
  - name: venv
    volume:
      size: 1Gi
  - name: checode
    volume: {}
  - name: home
    volume:
      size: 2Gi
`;

/tests/helpers/testkit.ts
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import type { Server } from 'node:http';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import type { AddressInfo } from 'node:net';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { newDb } from 'pg-mem';
import { loadConfig, type AppConfig } from '../../src/config/env.js';
import { buildContainer, type Container, type Dependencies } from '../../src/container.js';
import { createApp } from '../../src/app.js';
import { MetricsRepository } from '../../src/infra/db/MetricsRepository.js';
import { silentLogger } from '../../src/utils/logger.js';
import { FakeClock, FakeMetricsStore, FakeNotifier, FakeRegistry } from './fakes.js';
import { InMemoryKube } from './InMemoryKube.js';

export const TEST_SECRET = 'test-secret-key-0123456789abcdef';
export const ADMIN = { username: 'orion-admin', password: 'correct horse battery staple' };

export function makeConfig(overrides: Record<string, string> = {}): AppConfig {
  return loadConfig({
    SECRET_KEY: TEST_SECRET,
    ADMIN_USERNAME: ADMIN.username,
    ADMIN_PASSWORD: ADMIN.password,
    DEVREG_URL: 'http://registry.test',
    CLUSTER_API_URL: 'https://api.testcluster.ecs.dyn.example.net:6443',
    DEV_SPACES_SERVICE_FQDN: 'https://devspaces.test',
    POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'false',
    MAX_RUNNING_WORKSPACES: '2',
    MAX_TOTAL_WORKSPACES: '3',
    EMAIL_DOMAIN: 'example.com',
    ...overrides,
  });
}

/** A throwaway kubeconfig pointing at nothing; enough to construct the real KubeClient. */
export function writeTestKubeconfig(): string {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'wsm-kube-'));
  const file = join(dir, 'config');
  writeFileSync(
    file,
    [
      'apiVersion: v1',
      'kind: Config',
      'clusters:',
      '  - name: test',
      '    cluster: { server: "https://127.0.0.1:1", insecure-skip-tls-verify: true }',
      'users:',
      '  - name: test',
      '    user: { token: test-token }',
      'contexts:',
      '  - name: test',
      '    context: { cluster: test, user: test }',
      'current-context: test',
      '',
    ].join('\n'),
  );
  return file;
}

export interface Fakes {
  kube: InMemoryKube;
  metrics: FakeMetricsStore;
  notifier: FakeNotifier;
  registry: FakeRegistry;
  clock: FakeClock;
}

export function makeFakes(): Fakes {
  const clock = new FakeClock();
  return {
    kube: new InMemoryKube(() => clock.now()),
    metrics: new FakeMetricsStore(),
    notifier: new FakeNotifier(),
    registry: new FakeRegistry(),
    clock,
  };
}

export function makeContainer(
  fakes: Fakes = makeFakes(),
  config: AppConfig = makeConfig(),
  overrides: Partial<Dependencies> = {},
): { container: Container; fakes: Fakes } {
  const container = buildContainer(config, silentLogger, {
    kube: fakes.kube,
    metrics: fakes.metrics,
    registry: fakes.registry,
    notifier: fakes.notifier,
    clock: fakes.clock,
    ...overrides,
  });
  return { container, fakes };
}

/** A real Postgres-compatible database in memory, created from the shipped migration. */
export async function makePgMemRepository(options: { sql?: string; table?: string } = {}): Promise<{
  repo: MetricsRepository;
  query: (sql: string) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>[]>;
}> {
  const db = newDb();
  // Test-only stand-in for the table that already exists in the real database.
  const schema =
    options.sql ?? readFileSync(fileURLToPath(new URL('./metrics-table.sql', import.meta.url)), 'utf8');
  db.public.none(schema);
  const { Pool } = db.adapters.createPg();
  const pool = new Pool();
  return {
    repo: new MetricsRepository(pool, options.table),
    query: async (sql) => (await pool.query(sql)).rows as Record<string, unknown>[],
  };
}

export interface TestServer {
  baseUrl: string;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

export async function listen(container: Container): Promise<TestServer> {
  const server: Server = await new Promise((resolve) => {
    const s = createApp(container).listen(0, '127.0.0.1', () => resolve(s));
  });
  const { port } = server.address() as AddressInfo;
  return {
    baseUrl: `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`,
    close: () => new Promise((resolve, reject) => server.close((e) => (e ? reject(e) : resolve()))),
  };
}

/tests/integration/administration.test.ts
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { startIntegration, type Integration } from '../helpers/integration.js';
import { InMemoryKube } from '../helpers/InMemoryKube.js';
import { signToken } from '../../src/http/auth.js';

let t: Integration;
let deploy: InMemoryKube;
beforeEach(async () => {
  deploy = new InMemoryKube();
  t = await startIntegration({}, { deployKube: deploy });
});
afterEach(async () => {
  await t.close();
});

describe('versioned provisioning and discovery', () => {
  it('creates workspace projects idempotently and filters by CSI id', async () => {
    expect((await t.api('post', '/api/v1/pp1/projects').send({ csiid: '1001' })).status).toBe(201);
    expect((await t.api('post', '/api/v1/pp1/projects').send({ csiid: '1001' })).status).toBe(200);
    await t.api('post', '/api/v1/pp1/projects').send({ csiid: '1002' });
    const projects = await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/projects?csiid=1002');
    expect(projects.body).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(projects.body[0].csiid).toBe('1002');
    expect((await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/projects?csiid=bad')).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/stacks')).body).toEqual(
      (await t.api('get', '/api/v1/catalog')).body,
    );
    expect((await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces?csiid=1001')).body).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('filters deployment projects on the correct cluster', async () => {
    await t.api('post', '/api/v1/pp1/deployment-projects').send({ csiid: '1001' });
    await t.api('post', '/api/v1/pp1/deployment-projects').send({ csiid: '1002' });
    expect((await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/deployment-projects?csiid=1001')).body).toEqual([
      'pp1-devspaces-deploy-1001',
    ]);
    expect(await t.fakes.kube.getNamespace('pp1-devspaces-deploy-1001')).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('support resources', () => {
  it.each(['workspace', 'deployment'])('repairs defaults and applies custom quotas on %s', async (target) => {
    await t
      .api('post', `/api/v1/pp1/${target === 'workspace' ? 'projects' : 'deployment-projects'}`)
      .send({ csiid: '1001' });
    const body = { soeid: 'pp1', csiid: '1001', target };
    expect((await t.api('put', '/api/v1/support/resources').send(body)).status).toBe(200);
    const response = await t
      .api('put', '/api/v1/support/resources')
      .send({
        ...body,
        resources: [
          { kind: 'ResourceQuota', metadata: { name: 'custom-quota' }, spec: { hard: { pods: '4' } } },
        ],
      });
    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
    const kube = target === 'workspace' ? t.fakes.kube : deploy;
    expect(await kube.get('ResourceQuota', response.body.namespace, 'custom-quota')).toMatchObject({
      spec: { hard: { pods: '4' } },
    });
  });
  it('rejects missing projects and namespace ownership mismatches', async () => {
    const body = { soeid: 'pp1', csiid: '1001', target: 'deployment' };
    expect((await t.api('put', '/api/v1/support/resources').send(body)).status).toBe(404);
    deploy.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-deploy-1001', 'pp2', '1001');
    expect((await t.api('put', '/api/v1/support/resources').send(body)).status).toBe(409);
  });
  it('routes cluster roles to the selected cluster', async () => {
    deploy.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-deploy-1001', 'pp1', '1001');
    const apply = vi.fn(async (_kind, body) => body);
    t.container.deployKube.applyCluster = apply;
    const resource = { kind: 'ClusterRole', metadata: { name: 'support-reader' }, rules: [] };
    expect(
      (
        await t
          .api('put', '/api/v1/support/resources')
          .send({ soeid: 'pp1', csiid: '1001', target: 'deployment', resources: [resource] })
      ).status,
    ).toBe(200);
    expect(apply).toHaveBeenCalledWith('ClusterRole', resource);
  });
  it('rejects invalid resource kinds', async () => {
    expect(
      (
        await t
          .api('put', '/api/v1/support/resources')
          .send({
            soeid: 'pp1',
            csiid: '1001',
            target: 'workspace',
            resources: [{ kind: 'Secret', metadata: { name: 'token' } }],
          })
      ).status,
    ).toBe(400);
  });
});

describe('runtime configuration', () => {
  const patch = (body: object) =>
    request(t.app).patch('/api/v1/configuration').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${t.token}`).send(body);
  it('validates updates atomically, redacts secrets and keeps repeated changes active', async () => {
    expect(
      (await patch({ MAX_RUNNING_WORKSPACES: 1, NOTIFICATION_EMAIL_PASSWORD: 'private-value' })).status,
    ).toBe(200);
    expect((await patch({ MAX_TOTAL_WORKSPACES: 8 })).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await patch({ MAX_RUNNING_WORKSPACES: 9 })).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await patch({ SECRET_KEY: 'overwrite' })).status).toBe(400);
    const response = await t.api('get', '/api/v1/configuration');
    expect(response.body.settings).toMatchObject({
      MAX_RUNNING_WORKSPACES: 1,
      MAX_TOTAL_WORKSPACES: 8,
      NOTIFICATION_EMAIL_PASSWORD: '[redacted]',
    });
    expect(JSON.stringify(response.body)).not.toContain('private-value');
    expect(t.container.config.limits.maxRunning).toBe(1);
  });
  it('requires new credentials when a cluster URL changes', async () => {
    expect((await patch({ CLUSTER_API_URL: 'https://other.test' })).status).toBe(400);
    expect(
      (await patch({ CLUSTER_API_URL: 'https://api.other.test', CLUSTER_TOKEN: 'new-token' })).status,
    ).toBe(200);
    expect(t.container.config.cluster.deployApiUrl).toBe('https://api.other.test');
  });
  it('changes notification, registry, deployment cluster and limits together', async () => {
    const response = await patch({
      DEVREG_URL: 'https://registry.other.test',
      NOTIFICATION_MAIL_URL: 'https://mail.test',
      NOTIFICATION_EMAIL_USERNAME: 'mail-user',
      DEPLOY_CLUSTER_API_URL: 'https://deploy.test',
      DEPLOY_CLUSTER_TOKEN: 'deploy-token',
      MAX_TOTAL_WORKSPACES: 9,
    });
    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
    expect(t.container.config.notification.username).toBe('mail-user');
    expect(t.container.config.devreg.url).toBe('https://registry.other.test');
  });
});

describe('administrative access', () => {
  it.each(['/configuration', '/logs', '/support/resources'])(
    'rejects anonymous and regular users at %s',
    async (path) => {
      const token = signToken(t.container.config.auth, { soeid: 'pp1', role: 'user' });
      expect((await request(t.app).get(`/api/v1${path}`)).status).toBe(401);
      expect(
        (await request(t.app).get(`/api/v1${path}`).set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`)).status,
      ).toBe(403);
    },
  );
  it('serves bounded log results in JSON and readable text', async () => {
    expect((await t.api('get', '/api/v1/logs?days=2&format=text')).text).toContain('Application logs |');
    expect((await t.api('get', '/api/v1/logs?days=32')).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await t.api('get', '/api/v1/logs?days=1&limit=1')).body.entries.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
  });
});

/tests/integration/auth-and-ops.test.ts
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ADMIN, TEST_SECRET } from '../helpers/testkit.js';
import { startIntegration, type Integration } from '../helpers/integration.js';

let it$: Integration;
beforeAll(async () => {
  it$ = await startIntegration();
});
afterAll(() => it$.close());

describe('versioned operational endpoints', () => {
  it('/api/v1/healthz is OK and never touches dependencies', async () => {
    it$.fakes.kube.failOnce('ping', new Error('would fail'));
    const res = await request(it$.app).get('/api/v1/healthz');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.text).toBe('OK');
    expect(it$.fakes.kube.calls).not.toContain('ping');
    await expect(it$.fakes.kube.ping()).rejects.toThrow('would fail'); // consume the armed failure
  });

  it('/api/v1/readyz reports ready when kubernetes and postgres answer', async () => {
    const res = await request(it$.app).get('/api/v1/readyz');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body).toEqual({ status: 'ready', checks: { kubernetes: 'ok', postgres: 'ok' } });
  });

  it('/api/v1/readyz reports 503 when the cluster is unreachable', async () => {
    it$.fakes.kube.failOnce('ping', new Error('connection refused'));
    const res = await request(it$.app).get('/api/v1/readyz');
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(res.body).toEqual({ status: 'not-ready', checks: { kubernetes: 'unavailable', postgres: 'ok' } });
  });

  it('unversioned probes are removed', async () => {
    expect((await request(it$.app).get('/healthz')).status).toBe(404);
  });
});

describe('POST /api/v1/login', () => {
  it('issues a verifiable admin token for the right credentials', async () => {
    const res = await request(it$.app).post('/api/v1/login').send(ADMIN);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const claims = jwt.verify(res.body.token, TEST_SECRET) as jwt.JwtPayload;
    expect(claims).toMatchObject({ sub: ADMIN.username, role: 'admin' });
  });

  it.each([
    ['wrong password', { username: ADMIN.username, password: 'nope' }],
    ['wrong username', { username: 'nope', password: ADMIN.password }],
  ])('rejects %s with 401 and no token', async (_l, body) => {
    const res = await request(it$.app).post('/api/v1/login').send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'UNAUTHORIZED' });
    expect(res.body.token).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('rejects a malformed body with 400', async () => {
    const res = await request(it$.app).post('/api/v1/login').send({ username: 'x' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('VALIDATION_ERROR');
  });

  it('rejects invalid JSON with 400', async () => {
    const res = await request(it$.app)
      .post('/api/v1/login')
      .set('Content-Type', 'application/json')
      .send('{oops');
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('INVALID_JSON');
  });

  it('rejects an oversized body with 413', async () => {
    const res = await request(it$.app)
      .post('/api/v1/login')
      .send({ username: 'x'.repeat(400_000), password: 'y' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(413);
  });
});

describe('authentication on protected routes', () => {
  it.each(['/api/v1/catalog', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces', '/api/v1/pp1/projects'])(
    '%s needs a token',
    async (path) => {
      const res = await request(it$.app).get(path);
      expect(res.status).toBe(401);
      expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'UNAUTHORIZED' });
    },
  );

  it('rejects a token signed with another secret', async () => {
    const forged = jwt.sign({ role: 'admin' }, 'some-other-secret-some-other-secret', {
      subject: 'attacker',
    });
    const res = await request(it$.app).get('/api/v1/pp1/workspaces').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${forged}`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  it('lets a non-admin user in only for their own soeid', async () => {
    const own = jwt.sign({ role: 'user' }, TEST_SECRET, { subject: 'pp1' });
    const ok = await request(it$.app).get('/api/v1/pp1/workspaces').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${own}`);
    expect(ok.status).toBe(200);
    const other = await request(it$.app).get('/api/v1/pp2/workspaces').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${own}`);
    expect(other.status).toBe(403);
    expect(other.body.code).toBe('FORBIDDEN');
  });

  it.each(['..%2Fetc', 'a%20b', 'x'])('rejects a malformed or too-short soeid (%s)', async (soeid) => {
    const res = await it$.api('get', `/api/v1/${soeid}/workspaces`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('INVALID_SOEID');
  });
});

describe('versioning', () => {
  it('serves v1 without deprecation headers', async () => {
    const res = await it$.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.headers.deprecation).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('rejects unversioned user paths', async () => {
    expect((await it$.api('get', '/pp1/workspaces')).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it('rejects unversioned login', async () => {
    expect((await request(it$.app).post('/login').send(ADMIN)).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it('an unknown API version is a 404, not a user lookup', async () => {
    const res = await it$.api('get', '/api/v2/pp1/workspaces');
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('ROUTE_NOT_FOUND');
  });

  it('unknown routes under v1 are 404', async () => {
    const res = await it$.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/nothing-here');
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
  });
});

describe('request ids', () => {
  it('echoes a safe incoming x-request-id and generates one otherwise', async () => {
    const given = await request(it$.app).get('/api/v1/healthz').set('x-request-id', 'abc-12345678');
    expect(given.headers['x-request-id']).toBe('abc-12345678');
    const generated = await request(it$.app).get('/api/v1/healthz').set('x-request-id', 'bad id with spaces');
    expect(generated.headers['x-request-id']).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/);
  });

  it('does not advertise the framework', async () => {
    expect((await request(it$.app).get('/api/v1/healthz')).headers['x-powered-by']).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

/tests/integration/deploy-cluster.test.ts
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { InMemoryKube } from '../helpers/InMemoryKube.js';
import { startIntegration, type Integration } from '../helpers/integration.js';

/** Dev Spaces cluster (001d) and a separate build/deploy cluster (004d), as in the original design. */
let t: Integration;
let deploy: InMemoryKube;

beforeAll(async () => {
  deploy = new InMemoryKube();
  t = await startIntegration({}, { deployKube: deploy });
});
afterAll(() => t.close());

describe('workspaces stay on the Dev Spaces cluster, deployment projects go to the deploy cluster', () => {
  it('creates the deployment project (and its quotas, roles, bindings) only on the deploy cluster', async () => {
    const res = await t.api('post', '/api/v1/pp1/deployment-projects').send({ csiid: '1001' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(201);

    expect(deploy.namespaces.has('pp1-devspaces-deploy-1001')).toBe(true);
    expect([...deploy.objects.keys()].some((k) => k.startsWith('Role/pp1-devspaces-deploy-1001/'))).toBe(
      true,
    );
    expect(t.fakes.kube.namespaces.size).toBe(0);
    expect(t.fakes.kube.objects.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it('lists and deletes them on the deploy cluster', async () => {
    expect((await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/deployment-projects')).body).toEqual([
      'pp1-devspaces-deploy-1001',
    ]);
    const res = await t.api('delete', '/api/v1/pp1/deployment-projects');
    expect(res.body.deleted).toEqual(['pp1-devspaces-deploy-1001']);
    expect(deploy.namespaces.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it('workspaces and projects are created on the Dev Spaces cluster, never on the deploy cluster', async () => {
    const res = await t
      .api('post', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces')
      .send({ devworkspaceValue: 'java-maven', csiid: '1001' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces('pp1-devspaces-1001')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(deploy.namespaces.size).toBe(0);
    expect(deploy.objects.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it('personal access tokens and git config also belong to the Dev Spaces cluster', async () => {
    const pat = `ghp_${'a1B2c3D4e5F6g7H8i9J0k1L2m3N4o5P6q7R8'}`;
    expect((await t.api('post', '/api/v1/pp1/pats/main').send({ csiid: '1001', token: pat })).status).toBe(
      201,
    );
    expect(
      (await t.api('put', '/api/v1/pp1/gitconfig').send({ csiid: '1001', content: '[user]\n  name = P\n' }))
        .status,
    ).toBe(200);
    expect(deploy.objects.size).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('readiness checks both clusters when they are different', () => {
  it('logs authentication failures without exposing upstream messages or credentials', async () => {
    const log = vi.spyOn(t.container.logger, 'error');
    try {
      t.fakes.kube.failOnce('ping', Object.assign(new Error('Bearer private-token'), { statusCode: 401 }));
      expect((await request(t.app).get('/api/v1/readyz')).status).toBe(503);
      expect(log).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'readiness.check_failed',
        expect.objectContaining({
          dependency: 'kubernetes',
          reason: 'authentication_failed',
          httpStatus: 401,
        }),
      );
      expect(JSON.stringify(log.mock.calls)).not.toContain('private-token');
    } finally {
      log.mockRestore();
    }
  });
  it('reports each cluster', async () => {
    const res = await request(t.app).get('/api/v1/readyz');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.checks).toEqual({ kubernetes: 'ok', deployCluster: 'ok', postgres: 'ok' });
  });

  it('is not ready when only the deploy cluster is down', async () => {
    deploy.failOnce('ping', new Error('deploy cluster unreachable'));
    const res = await request(t.app).get('/api/v1/readyz');
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(res.body.checks).toEqual({ kubernetes: 'ok', deployCluster: 'unavailable', postgres: 'ok' });
  });

  it('is not ready when only the Dev Spaces cluster is down', async () => {
    t.fakes.kube.failOnce('ping', new Error('dev spaces cluster unreachable'));
    const res = await request(t.app).get('/api/v1/readyz');
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(res.body.checks).toEqual({ kubernetes: 'unavailable', deployCluster: 'ok', postgres: 'ok' });
  });
});

describe('with a single cluster (the default, as in the team’s current environment)', () => {
  it('uses the same cluster for deployment projects and reports one cluster check', async () => {
    const single = await startIntegration();
    try {
      expect(single.container.deployKube).toBe(single.container.kube);
      expect((await single.api('post', '/api/v1/pp1/deployment-projects').send({ csiid: '5' })).status).toBe(
        201,
      );
      expect(single.fakes.kube.namespaces.has('pp1-devspaces-deploy-5')).toBe(true);
      const ready = await request(single.app).get('/api/v1/readyz');
      expect(ready.body.checks).toEqual({ kubernetes: 'ok', postgres: 'ok' });
    } finally {
      await single.close();
    }
  });
});

/tests/integration/hand-over-sh.test.ts
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { afterAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

const repo = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../', import.meta.url));
const hasBuilder = existsSync(join(repo, 'tools', 'build-all-code.sh'));
const SLOW = { timeout: 180_000 };

/** Prefer Git Bash on Windows (a bare `bash` may be WSL, which resolves paths differently). */
function findBash(): string | undefined {
  const candidates = [
    process.env.GIT_BASH,
    'C:\\Program Files\\Git\\bin\\bash.exe',
    'C:\\Program Files\\Git\\usr\\bin\\bash.exe',
    'bash',
  ].filter((c): c is string => Boolean(c));
  for (const candidate of candidates) {
    const r = spawnSync(candidate, ['-c', 'echo $BASH_VERSION && command -v awk sha256sum base64 cmp comm'], {
      encoding: 'utf8',
    });
    if (r.status === 0 && r.stdout.trim().split('\n').length >= 6) return candidate;
  }
  return undefined;
}

const bash = findBash();
const sandboxes: string[] = [];
const tmp = (label: string) => {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), `wsm-${label}-`));
  sandboxes.push(dir);
  return dir;
};
afterAll(() => sandboxes.forEach((d) => rmSync(d, { recursive: true, force: true })));
