function sh(cwd: string, script: string, args: string[] = []) {
  const r = spawnSync(bash as string, [script, ...args], { cwd, encoding: 'utf8' });
  return { code: r.status, out: r.stdout, err: r.stderr, all: `${r.stdout}${r.stderr}` };
}

const bytes = (c: string | Buffer) => (Buffer.isBuffer(c) ? c : Buffer.from(c));

function put(root: string, rel: string, content: string | Buffer) {
  const full = join(root, ...rel.split('/'));
  mkdirSync(dirname(full), { recursive: true });
  writeFileSync(full, content);
}
const read = (root: string, rel: string) => readFileSync(join(root, ...rel.split('/')));
const has = (root: string, rel: string) => existsSync(join(root, ...rel.split('/')));
const sha = (b: Buffer) => createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');

const RECREATE = readFileSync(join(repo, 'recreate-app.sh'));

/** Writes a hand-over text file in the documented format (what build-all-code.sh produces), with checksums. */
function handOver(opts: {
  plain?: Record<string, string>;
  removed?: string[];
  binary?: Record<string, Buffer>;
  checksums?: boolean;
}): string {
  const plain = opts.plain ?? {};
  const binary = opts.binary ?? {};
  let out = '# test hand-over\n\n';
  for (const [path, content] of Object.entries(plain)) out += `/${path}\n${content}\n`;
  if (opts.removed?.length) out += `/_REMOVED.txt\n${opts.removed.map((r) => `/${r}`).join('\n')}\n\n`;
  const names = Object.keys(binary);
  if (names.length) {
    out += '@@BINARY-SECTION\n';
    for (const n of names)
      out += `@@FILE /${n}\n${bytes(binary[n] as Buffer)
        .toString('base64')
        .replace(/(.{76})/g, '$1\n')}\n@@END\n`;
  }
  if (opts.checksums !== false) {
    out += '@@CHECKSUMS\n';
    for (const [p, c] of Object.entries(plain)) out += `${sha(Buffer.from(c))}  /${p}\n`;
    for (const [p, b] of Object.entries(binary)) out += `${sha(b)}  /${p}\n`;
  }
  return out;
}

let dir: string;
beforeEach(() => {
  dir = tmp('app');
  put(dir, 'recreate-app.sh', RECREATE);
});

const apply = (text: string, args: string[] = ['-f'], name = 'hand.txt') => {
  writeFileSync(join(dir, name), text);
  return sh(dir, 'recreate-app.sh', [...args, name]);
};

describe('the shipped recreate script', () => {
  it('uses LF line endings only (CRLF would break bash)', () => {
    expect(RECREATE.includes(13)).toBe(false);
    if (hasBuilder) expect(readFileSync(join(repo, 'tools', 'build-all-code.sh')).includes(13)).toBe(false);
    expect(readFileSync(join(repo, '.gitattributes'), 'utf8')).toContain('*.sh text eol=lf');
  });
});

describe.skipIf(!bash)('recreate-app.sh', SLOW, () => {
  const PLAIN = {
    'src/a.ts': "export const a = 'é — 日本語 🚀';\n",
    'src/deep/er/b.ts': 'line1\n\n\nline4 after two blank lines\n',
    'package.json': '{ "name": "demo" }\n',
    'docs/empty.md': '',
  };
  const PNG = Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a, 0x00, 0x00, 0xff, 0xfe, 0x80]);

  it('writes plain and base64 files exactly, creating folders, and verifies checksums', () => {
    const r = apply(
      handOver({
        plain: PLAIN,
        binary: { 'img/logo.png': PNG, 'with space/crlf file.yaml': Buffer.from('a: 1\r\nb: 2') },
      }),
    );
    expect(r.code, r.all).toBe(0);
    expect(r.out).toContain('6 written (6 new, 0 overwritten)');
    for (const [p, c] of Object.entries(PLAIN)) expect(read(dir, p).equals(bytes(c)), p).toBe(true);
    expect(read(dir, 'img/logo.png').equals(PNG)).toBe(true);
    expect(read(dir, 'with space/crlf file.yaml').toString()).toBe('a: 1\r\nb: 2');
  });

  it('keeps existing files that differ unless -f is given, and leaves identical ones alone', () => {
    apply(handOver({ plain: PLAIN }));
    put(dir, 'package.json', '{ "name": "edited by hand" }\n');

    const safe = apply(handOver({ plain: PLAIN }), []);
    expect(safe.code).toBe(0);
    expect(safe.out).toContain('skipped (exists and differs): package.json');
    expect(safe.out).toContain('3 already identical, 1 skipped');
    expect(read(dir, 'package.json').toString()).toContain('edited by hand');

    const forced = apply(handOver({ plain: PLAIN }));
    expect(forced.out).toContain('1 written (0 new, 1 overwritten)');
    expect(read(dir, 'package.json').toString()).toBe('{ "name": "demo" }\n');
  });

  it('--dry-run writes nothing; --list lists', () => {
    const text = handOver({ plain: PLAIN, removed: ['old.ts'] });
    const dry = apply(text, ['-n', '-f']);
    expect(dry.out).toContain('Dry run: 4 written');
    expect(has(dir, 'src/a.ts')).toBe(false);
    const list = apply(text, ['-l']);
    expect(list.out).toContain('src/deep/er/b.ts');
    expect(list.out).toContain('4 file(s), 1 to remove');
    expect(has(dir, 'src/a.ts')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('refuses a damaged file and writes nothing: tampered, truncated, or without checksums', () => {
    const good = handOver({ plain: PLAIN });
    const tampered = apply(good.replace('"demo"', '"DEMO"'));
    expect(tampered.code).toBe(1);
    expect(tampered.err).toMatch(/checksum mismatch for \/package\.json/);

    const truncated = apply(good.slice(0, good.indexOf('@@CHECKSUMS') - 20));
    expect(truncated.code).toBe(1);
    expect(truncated.err).toContain('no @@CHECKSUMS section');

    const handwritten = apply(handOver({ plain: PLAIN, checksums: false }));
    expect(handwritten.code).toBe(1);

    expect(has(dir, 'src/a.ts')).toBe(false);
    expect(has(dir, 'package.json')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('--no-verify applies a hand-edited file anyway', () => {
    const edited = apply(handOver({ plain: PLAIN }).replace('"demo"', '"DEMO"'), ['-f', '--no-verify']);
    expect(edited.code, edited.all).toBe(0);
    expect(edited.err).toContain('did not match their checksum');
    expect(read(dir, 'package.json').toString()).toBe('{ "name": "DEMO" }\n');
  });

  it('detects a file added to the text without a checksum', () => {
    const extra = handOver({ plain: { ...PLAIN } }).replace(
      '/package.json\n',
      '/sneaky.ts\nevil();\n\n/package.json\n',
    );
    const r = apply(extra);
    expect(r.code).toBe(1);
    expect(r.err).toContain('sneaky.ts');
    expect(has(dir, 'sneaky.ts')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('is immune to line-ending changes of the text file (editor, e-mail, git autocrlf)', () => {
    const text = handOver({ plain: PLAIN, binary: { 'img/logo.png': PNG } });
    for (const converted of [text.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n'), text.replace(/(?<!\r)\n/g, '\r\n')]) {
      const target = tmp('conv');
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'conv.txt'), converted);
      const r = sh(dir, 'recreate-app.sh', ['-f', 'conv.txt', target]);
      expect(r.code, r.all).toBe(0);
      for (const [p, c] of Object.entries(PLAIN)) expect(read(target, p).equals(bytes(c)), p).toBe(true);
      expect(read(target, 'img/logo.png').equals(PNG)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('rejects paths that could escape the folder', () => {
    const out = join(dir, 'out');
    for (const bad of ['../escape.bin', '//abs.bin', 'a/../../b.bin', 'a\\b.bin']) {
      const text = `@@BINARY-SECTION\n@@FILE ${bad.startsWith('/') ? '' : '/'}${bad}\naGk=\n@@END\n@@CHECKSUMS\n`;
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'evil.txt'), text);
      const r = sh(dir, 'recreate-app.sh', ['-f', 'evil.txt', out]);
      expect(r.code, bad).toBe(1);
      expect(r.err, bad).toMatch(/unsafe path/);
    }
    // a path line that tries to climb out is never treated as a file at all
    const sneaky = apply('# x\n\n/a.ts\nok();\n\n/../escape.ts\nbad();\n\n/b/../../c.ts\nbad();\n', [
      '-f',
      '--no-verify',
    ]);
    expect(sneaky.code, sneaky.all).toBe(0);
    expect(has(dir, 'a.ts')).toBe(true);
    expect(existsSync(join(dir, '..', 'escape.ts'))).toBe(false);
    expect(existsSync(join(dir, '..', 'c.ts'))).toBe(false);
    expect(read(dir, 'a.ts').toString()).toContain('/../escape.ts'); // it is just text inside a.ts
  });

  it('lists removed files, and deletes only existing ones inside the folder with --prune', () => {
    put(dir, 'old/gone.ts', 'x');
    put(dir, 'keep.ts', 'y');
    const text = handOver({ plain: { 'new.ts': 'n();\n' }, removed: ['old/gone.ts', 'never-existed.ts'] });

    const noPrune = apply(text);
    expect(noPrune.out).toContain('delete    old/gone.ts');
    expect(noPrune.out).toContain('--prune');
    expect(has(dir, 'old/gone.ts')).toBe(true);

    const prune = apply(text, ['-f', '--prune']);
    expect(prune.out).toContain('deleted   old/gone.ts');
    expect(prune.out).not.toContain('never-existed');
    expect(has(dir, 'old/gone.ts')).toBe(false);
    expect(has(dir, 'keep.ts')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('never replaces the running script: a newer version is saved as recreate-app.sh.new', () => {
    const newer = `${RECREATE.toString()}\n# newer version\n`;
    const r = apply(handOver({ plain: { 'recreate-app.sh': newer, 'src/x.ts': 'x();\n' } }));
    expect(r.code, r.all).toBe(0);
    expect(r.out).toContain('recreate-app.sh.new');
    expect(read(dir, 'recreate-app.sh').equals(RECREATE)).toBe(true);
    expect(read(dir, 'recreate-app.sh.new').toString()).toBe(newer);
    expect(has(dir, 'src/x.ts')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('says what to run next, only when relevant', () => {
    const code = apply(
      handOver({
        plain: { 'package.json': '{}\n', 'src/index.ts': 'run();\n', 'tests/a.test.ts': 't();\n' },
      }),
    );
    expect(code.out).toMatch(
      /Next:[\s\S]*npm install[\s\S]*npm run build[\s\S]*npm test[\s\S]*restart the app/,
    );
    const docs = apply(handOver({ plain: { 'docs/NOTES.md': 'hello\n' } }), ['-f'], 'docs.txt');
    expect(docs.out).not.toContain('Next:');
  });

  it('reports usage problems clearly', () => {
    expect(sh(dir, 'recreate-app.sh', ['-f', 'missing.txt']).code).toBe(1);
    expect(sh(dir, 'recreate-app.sh', ['--bogus']).err).toContain('unknown option');
    const help = sh(dir, 'recreate-app.sh', ['--help']);
    expect(help.code).toBe(0);
    expect(help.out).toContain('recreate-app.sh -f');
  });

  it('defaults to ./all-app-code.txt', () => {
    writeFileSync(join(dir, 'all-app-code.txt'), handOver({ plain: PLAIN }));
    const r = sh(dir, 'recreate-app.sh', ['-f']);
    expect(r.code, r.all).toBe(0);
    expect(has(dir, 'src/a.ts')).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe.skipIf(!bash || !hasBuilder)('tools/build-all-code.sh → recreate-app.sh', SLOW, () => {
  const PNG = Buffer.from([
    0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a, 0x00, 0x00, 0xff, 0xfe, 0x80, 10, 13,
  ]);
  const APP: Record<string, string | Buffer> = {
    'package.json': '{ "name": "demo" }\n',
    'README.md': '# demo\n',
    '.gitignore': 'node_modules\n',
    'src/a.ts': "export const a = 'é — 日本語 🚀';\n",
    'src/blank-inside.ts': 'a();\n\n\nb();\n',
    'src/crlf.yaml': 'a: 1\r\nb: 2\r\n',
    'src/no-newline.txt': 'abc',
    'src/ends-blank.txt': 'text\n\n',
    'src/empty.txt': '',
    'src/with space/file name.txt': 'spaces in the path\n',
    'src/lookalike.md': '# notes\n\n/docs/guide.md\n@@END\n@@CHECKSUMS\nx\n',
    'src/logo.bin': PNG,
    'tests/x.test.ts': "it('x', () => {});\n",
  };
  const ALL = [...Object.keys(APP), 'recreate-app.sh'].sort();

  let proj: string;
  beforeEach(() => {
    proj = tmp('proj');
    put(proj, 'recreate-app.sh', RECREATE);
    put(proj, 'tools/build-all-code.sh', readFileSync(join(repo, 'tools', 'build-all-code.sh')));
    for (const [p, c] of Object.entries(APP)) put(proj, p, c);
    put(proj, '.env', 'SECRET=hunter2\n');
    put(proj, 'node_modules/dep/index.js', 'module.exports = 1');
    put(proj, 'dist/out.js', 'built');
    put(proj, 'legacy/old.ts', 'old');
    put(proj, 'coverage/lcov.info', 'x');
  });

  const build = (args: string[] = []) => sh(proj, 'tools/build-all-code.sh', args);
  const listed = (file = 'all-app-code.txt') =>
    sh(proj, 'recreate-app.sh', ['-l', file])
      .out.split('\n')
      .filter((l) => l && !l.includes('file(s)'))
      .map((l) => l.replace(/^\//, ''))
      .sort();
  const sameAsProject = (copy: string, files = ALL) => {
    for (const p of files) {
      expect(has(copy, p), p).toBe(true);
      expect(read(copy, p).equals(read(proj, p)), p).toBe(true);
    }
  };

  it('the first hand-over is the ENTIRE app and recreates every file exactly', () => {
    const r = build();
    expect(r.code, r.all).toBe(0);
    expect(r.out).toContain(`Entire app: ${ALL.length} file(s)`);
    expect(listed()).toEqual(ALL);

    const copy = tmp('copy');
    writeFileSync(join(copy, 'all-app-code.txt'), readFileSync(join(proj, 'all-app-code.txt')));
    put(copy, 'recreate-app.sh', RECREATE);
    const rc = sh(copy, 'recreate-app.sh', ['-f']);
    expect(rc.code, rc.all).toBe(0);
    for (const [p, c] of Object.entries(APP)) expect(read(copy, p).equals(bytes(c)), p).toBe(true);
    for (const absent of ['.env', 'node_modules', 'dist', 'legacy', 'coverage', 'tools', '.handover-state']) {
      expect(has(copy, absent), absent).toBe(false);
    }
  });

  it('shows ordinary files as readable plain text and stores the exceptions as base64', () => {
    const r = build();
    const text = readFileSync(join(proj, 'all-app-code.txt'), 'utf8');
    expect(text).toContain("/src/a.ts\nexport const a = 'é — 日本語 🚀';\n");
    expect(text).toMatch(/\n@@BINARY-SECTION\n/);
    expect(text).toMatch(/\n@@CHECKSUMS\n/);
    expect(text).not.toContain('hunter2');
    for (const odd of [
      'src/crlf.yaml',
      'src/no-newline.txt',
      'src/ends-blank.txt',
      'src/with space/file name.txt',
      'src/lookalike.md',
      'src/logo.bin',
    ]) {
      expect(r.out, odd).toContain(`  ${odd}`);
      expect(text, odd).toContain(`@@FILE /${odd}\n`);
    }
    for (const plain of ['src/a.ts', 'src/blank-inside.ts', 'package.json', 'README.md', 'src/empty.txt']) {
      expect(text, plain).toContain(`\n/${plain}\n`);
      expect(text, plain).not.toContain(`@@FILE /${plain}\n`);
    }
  });

  it('records what was handed over, and says so when nothing changed', () => {
    build();
    expect(existsSync(join(proj, '.handover-state'))).toBe(true);
    const before = readFileSync(join(proj, 'all-app-code.txt'));
    const again = build();
    expect(again.code).toBe(0);
    expect(again.out).toContain('Nothing changed since the last hand-over');
    expect(readFileSync(join(proj, 'all-app-code.txt')).equals(before)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('later hand-overs hold ONLY new and changed files plus the removal list', () => {
    build();
    const copy = tmp('copy');
    sh(proj, 'recreate-app.sh', ['-f', 'all-app-code.txt', copy]);

    put(proj, 'src/a.ts', "export const a = 'changed';\n");
    put(proj, 'src/new.ts', 'export const n = 1;\n');
    put(proj, 'src/new-crlf.yaml', 'k: v\r\n');
    rmSync(join(proj, 'src', 'empty.txt'));
    rmSync(join(proj, 'tests', 'x.test.ts'));

    const r = build();
    expect(r.code, r.all).toBe(0);
    expect(r.out).toContain('Updated files only: 3 new or changed, 2 removed');
    expect(listed()).toEqual(['src/a.ts', 'src/new-crlf.yaml', 'src/new.ts']);
    expect(readFileSync(join(proj, 'all-app-code.txt'), 'utf8')).toContain(
      '/_REMOVED.txt\n/src/empty.txt\n/tests/x.test.ts\n',
    );

    const apply1 = sh(copy, 'recreate-app.sh', ['-f', join(proj, 'all-app-code.txt')]);
    expect(apply1.code, apply1.all).toBe(0);
    expect(apply1.out).toContain('3 written (2 new, 1 overwritten)');
    expect(apply1.out).toContain('delete    src/empty.txt');
    expect(has(copy, 'src/empty.txt')).toBe(true); // not deleted without --prune

    const apply2 = sh(copy, 'recreate-app.sh', ['-f', '--prune', join(proj, 'all-app-code.txt')]);
    expect(apply2.out).toContain('deleted   src/empty.txt');
    expect(has(copy, 'src/empty.txt')).toBe(false);
    expect(has(copy, 'tests/x.test.ts')).toBe(false);
    sameAsProject(copy, listedAll());
  });

  const listedAll = () =>
    sh(proj, 'tools/build-all-code.sh', ['--full', '--list', '--keep-state'])
      .out.split('\n')
      .filter((l) => l.startsWith('  '))
      .map((l) => l.trim());

  it('--full gives the entire app again, --list and --keep-state record nothing, --redo repeats the previous one', () => {
    build();
    put(proj, 'src/one.ts', 'one();\n');
    expect(build().out).toContain('Updated files only: 1 new or changed');
    put(proj, 'src/two.ts', 'two();\n');

    const state = readFileSync(join(proj, '.handover-state'));
    const peek = build(['--list']);
    expect(peek.out).toContain('1 file(s) to include');
    expect(readFileSync(join(proj, '.handover-state')).equals(state)).toBe(true);

    const kept = build(['--keep-state']);
    expect(kept.out).toContain('(not recorded as handed over)');
    expect(readFileSync(join(proj, '.handover-state')).equals(state)).toBe(true);
    expect(listed()).toEqual(['src/two.ts']);

    const redo = build(['--redo']);
    expect(redo.out).toContain('Updated files only: 2 new or changed'); // one.ts (never confirmed as received) + two.ts
    expect(listed()).toEqual(['src/one.ts', 'src/two.ts']);

    const full = build(['--full']);
    expect(full.out).toContain('Entire app:');
    expect(listed().length).toBe(ALL.length + 2);
  });

  it('includes removals in a full snapshot when updating an existing installation', () => {
    expect(build().code).toBe(0);
    const target = tmp('full-update');
    expect(sh(proj, 'recreate-app.sh', ['all-app-code.txt', target]).code).toBe(0);
    rmSync(join(proj, 'src/a.ts'));
    expect(build(['--full']).code).toBe(0);
    expect(readFileSync(join(proj, 'all-app-code.txt'), 'utf8')).toContain('/_REMOVED.txt\n/src/a.ts');
    expect(sh(proj, 'recreate-app.sh', ['-f', '--prune', 'all-app-code.txt', target]).code).toBe(0);
    expect(existsSync(join(target, 'src/a.ts'))).toBe(false);
    for (const path of ALL.filter(path => path !== 'src/a.ts')) {
      expect(readFileSync(join(target, path)).equals(readFileSync(join(proj, path)))).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('refuses to pack a private key and never packs secrets', () => {
    put(proj, 'certs/dev.pem', '-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----\nMIIB\n-----END CERTIFICATE-----\n');
    expect(build().code).toBe(0);
    expect(listed()).toContain('certs/dev.pem');

    // built from pieces so that THIS file does not itself contain a private-key marker
    const key = ['-----BEGIN RSA ', 'PRIVATE KEY-----\nMIIE\n-----END RSA ', 'PRIVATE KEY-----\n'].join('');
    put(proj, 'certs/server.pem', key);
    const r = build(['--full']);
    expect(r.code).toBe(1);
    expect(r.err).toContain('private key');
    expect(readFileSync(join(proj, 'all-app-code.txt'), 'utf8')).not.toContain('MIIE');
  });

  it('a built hand-over survives a line-ending conversion of the text file', () => {
    build();
    const text = readFileSync(join(proj, 'all-app-code.txt'), 'utf8');
    for (const converted of [text.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n'), text.replace(/(?<!\r)\n/g, '\r\n')]) {
      const copy = tmp('conv');
      writeFileSync(join(proj, 'conv.txt'), converted);
      const r = sh(proj, 'recreate-app.sh', ['-f', 'conv.txt', copy]);
      expect(r.code, r.all).toBe(0);
      sameAsProject(copy);
    }
  });

  it('builds quietly: nothing on stderr for a normal build', () => {
    const r = build();
    expect(r.code).toBe(0);
    expect(r.err).toBe('');
  });
});

/tests/integration/metrics-db-and-wiring.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createProductionDependencies, buildContainer } from '../../src/container.js';
import { NoopMetricsStore } from '../../src/infra/db/MetricsRepository.js';
import { KubeClient } from '../../src/infra/kube/KubeClient.js';
import { EmailNotifier, NoopNotifier } from '../../src/infra/email/EmailNotifier.js';
import { MetricsRecorder } from '../../src/services/MetricsRecorder.js';
import { silentLogger } from '../../src/utils/logger.js';
import { FakeClock } from '../helpers/fakes.js';
import { makeConfig, makePgMemRepository, writeTestKubeconfig } from '../helpers/testkit.js';

const kubeconfig = writeTestKubeconfig();

/**
 * The table as the ORIGINAL service wrote it: no workspaceversion / editorname, but an errordetail column.
 * It lives in `public` here because pg-mem reports every table under `public` in information_schema (real Postgres
 * keeps the `metrics` schema; the exact catalog SQL for that is pinned in tests/unit/infra/MetricsRepository.test.ts).
 */
const LEGACY_TABLE_SQL = `
  CREATE TABLE public."devspaces-metrics-dev" (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    "timestamp" TIMESTAMP,
    csiid VARCHAR(32),
    soeid VARCHAR(64),
    eventtype VARCHAR(32),
    resourcename VARCHAR(255),
    resourcetype VARCHAR(64),
    status JSONB,
    limitcpu NUMERIC,
    limitmemory NUMERIC,
    requestcpu NUMERIC,
    requestmemory NUMERIC,
    duration INTERVAL,
    useraction VARCHAR(32),
    storage NUMERIC,
    errordetail TEXT
  );`;

describe('writing into the EXISTING table of the Orion database', () => {
  const base = {
    timestamp: new Date('2026-03-04T05:06:07.089Z'),
    csiid: '177398',
    soeid: 'pp58386',
    eventtype: 'create',
    resourcename: 'java-maven-ab12',
    resourcetype: 'workspace',
    status: { failed: false, error: '', message: '' },
    useraction: 'user',
    limitcpu: 2,
    limitmemory: 4,
    requestcpu: 0.5,
    requestmemory: 1,
    duration: '2 hours 5 minutes',
    storage: 3,
    workspaceversion: '2.0.0',
    editorname: 'vscode',
  } as const;

  it('works with a table shaped like the original one, using the configured dev table name', async () => {
    const { repo, query } = await makePgMemRepository({
      sql: LEGACY_TABLE_SQL,
      table: 'public."devspaces-metrics-dev"',
    });
    await repo.insert(base);
    await repo.insert({
      ...base,
      eventtype: 'delete',
      status: { failed: true, error: 'namespace is terminating', message: '' },
    });

    const rows = await query('SELECT * FROM public."devspaces-metrics-dev" ORDER BY id');
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(rows[0]).toMatchObject({
      soeid: 'pp58386',
      csiid: '177398',
      eventtype: 'create',
      errordetail: null,
    });
    expect(rows[1]).toMatchObject({ eventtype: 'delete', errordetail: 'namespace is terminating' });
    expect(Number(rows[0]?.limitcpu)).toBe(2);
    expect(rows[0]).not.toHaveProperty('workspaceversion');
    // the timestamp is stored as the UTC wall-clock time, exactly as the original service did
    expect(new Date(rows[0]?.timestamp as Date).toISOString()).toBe('2026-03-04T05:06:07.089Z');
  });

  it('creates nothing: the database has exactly the objects it started with', async () => {
    const { repo, query } = await makePgMemRepository({
      sql: LEGACY_TABLE_SQL,
      table: 'public."devspaces-metrics-dev"',
    });
    const snapshot = () =>
      query(
        `SELECT table_schema, table_name FROM information_schema.tables
          WHERE table_schema NOT IN ('information_schema', 'pg_catalog') ORDER BY 1, 2`,
      );
    const before = await snapshot();
    await repo.insert(base);
    await repo.ping();
    expect(await snapshot()).toEqual(before);
    expect(before).toEqual([{ table_schema: 'public', table_name: 'devspaces-metrics-dev' }]);
  });

  it('reports a missing table instead of creating it', async () => {
    const { repo, query } = await makePgMemRepository({
      sql: 'CREATE SCHEMA metrics;',
      table: 'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"',
    });
    await expect(repo.insert(base)).rejects.toThrow();
    expect(await query(`SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables`)).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('metrics table (test fixture + repository on a real SQL engine)', () => {
  it('stores every column of a record', async () => {
    const { repo, query } = await makePgMemRepository();
    await repo.insert({
      timestamp: new Date('2026-03-04T05:06:07Z'),
      csiid: '1001',
      soeid: 'pp1',
      eventtype: 'create',
      resourcename: 'java-maven-ab12',
      resourcetype: 'workspace',
      status: { failed: false, error: '', message: 'ok' },
      useraction: 'user',
      limitcpu: 2.5,
      limitmemory: 4,
      requestcpu: 0.5,
      requestmemory: 1,
      duration: '1 minute',
      storage: 3,
      workspaceversion: '2.0.0',
      editorname: 'vscode',
    });
    const [row] = await query('SELECT * FROM metrics."devspaces-metrics"');
    expect(row).toMatchObject({
      csiid: '1001',
      soeid: 'pp1',
      eventtype: 'create',
      resourcename: 'java-maven-ab12',
      resourcetype: 'workspace',
      duration: '1 minute',
      useraction: 'user',
      workspaceversion: '2.0.0',
      editorname: 'vscode',
      status: { failed: false, error: '', message: 'ok' },
    });
    expect(Number(row?.limitcpu)).toBe(2.5);
    expect(Number(row?.storage)).toBe(3);
    expect(new Date(String(row?.timestamp)).toISOString()).toBe('2026-03-04T05:06:07.000Z');
    expect(row?.id).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('supports the dashboard questions: counts per stack and failures', async () => {
    const { repo, query } = await makePgMemRepository();
    const recorder = new MetricsRecorder(repo, new FakeClock(), silentLogger);
    const base = { soeid: 'pp1', eventtype: 'create', resourcetype: 'workspace' } as const;
    await recorder.record({ ...base, resourcename: 'a', workspaceversion: '1' });
    await recorder.record({ ...base, soeid: 'pp2', resourcename: 'b', workspaceversion: '1' });
    await recorder.record({
      ...base,
      resourcename: 'c',
      status: { failed: true, error: 'quota', message: '' },
    });

    const users = await query('SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT soeid) AS n FROM metrics."devspaces-metrics"');
    expect(Number(users[0]?.n)).toBe(2);
    const failed = await query(
      `SELECT resourcename FROM metrics."devspaces-metrics" WHERE status->>'failed' = 'true'`,
    );
    expect(failed).toEqual([{ resourcename: 'c' }]);
  });

  it('pings and the recorder survives a broken table', async () => {
    const { repo } = await makePgMemRepository();
    await expect(repo.ping()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    const broken = new (class extends Object {
      async insert(): Promise<void> {
        throw new Error('relation "metrics.devspaces-metrics" does not exist');
      }
      async ping(): Promise<void> {}
      async close(): Promise<void> {}
    })();
    await expect(
      new MetricsRecorder(broken, new FakeClock(), silentLogger).record({
        soeid: 'pp1',
        eventtype: 'create',
        resourcetype: 'workspace',
      }),
    ).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('production wiring', () => {
  it('fails fast at startup when there is no cluster configuration', () => {
    const config = makeConfig({ KUBECONFIG_PATH: '/no/such/file' });
    expect(() => createProductionDependencies(config, silentLogger)).toThrow();
  });

  it('uses no-op adapters when Postgres and mail are disabled', () => {
    const config = makeConfig({ KUBECONFIG_PATH: kubeconfig });
    const deps = createProductionDependencies(config, silentLogger);
    expect(deps.metrics).toBeInstanceOf(NoopMetricsStore);
    expect(deps.notifier).toBeInstanceOf(NoopNotifier);
    expect(deps.registry).toBeDefined();
    expect(deps.kube).toBeDefined();
  });

  describe('with the team’s token-based environment (no kubeconfig file at all)', () => {
    const team = {
      CLUSTER_API_URL: 'https://api.namctigtd001d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443',
      devSpacesClusterToken001d: 'fake-devspaces-token',
      devOpsToken004d: 'fake-devops-token',
      NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED: '0',
    };

    it('connects with the cluster URL and token, and uses one cluster for everything', () => {
      const deps = createProductionDependencies(makeConfig(team), silentLogger);
      expect(deps.kube).toBeInstanceOf(KubeClient);
      expect(deps.deployKube).toBeUndefined(); // DEPLOY_CLUSTER_API_URL is not set
      const container = buildContainer(makeConfig(team), silentLogger, deps);
      expect(container.deployKube).toBe(container.kube);
    });

    it('builds a second client for a different deploy cluster, authenticated with devOpsToken004d', () => {
      const config = makeConfig({
        ...team,
        DEPLOY_CLUSTER_API_URL: 'https://api.namctiswd004d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443',
      });
      const deps = createProductionDependencies(config, silentLogger);
      expect(deps.kube).toBeInstanceOf(KubeClient);
      expect(deps.deployKube).toBeInstanceOf(KubeClient);
      expect(deps.deployKube).not.toBe(deps.kube);
      expect(config.cluster.deployToken).toBe('fake-devops-token');
      expect(config.cluster.token).toBe('fake-devspaces-token');
    });
  });

  it('uses the email notifier and a user directory when configured', () => {
    const config = makeConfig({
      KUBECONFIG_PATH: kubeconfig,
      NOTIFICATION_MAIL_URL: 'http://mail.test',
      USER_DIRECTORY_GRAPHQL_URL: 'http://dir.test/graphql',
    });
    expect(createProductionDependencies(config, silentLogger).notifier).toBeInstanceOf(EmailNotifier);
  });

  it('builds a container exposing every service', () => {
    const config = makeConfig({ KUBECONFIG_PATH: kubeconfig });
    const container = buildContainer(
      config,
      silentLogger,
      createProductionDependencies(config, silentLogger),
    );
    expect(Object.keys(container).sort()).toEqual(
      [
        'config',
        'deployKube',
        'deployments',
        'gitConfig',
        'kube',
        'logger',
        'metrics',
        'namespaces',
        'pats',
        'recorder',
        'reconfigure',
        'support',
        'registry',
        'workspaces',
      ].sort(),
    );
  });
});

/tests/integration/pats-gitconfig-deployments.test.ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { startIntegration, type Integration } from '../helpers/integration.js';
import { SAMPLE_PAT } from '../helpers/sample-devfile.js';

let t: Integration;
beforeAll(async () => {
  t = await startIntegration();
});
afterAll(() => t.close());
beforeEach(async () => {
  t.fakes.kube.namespaces.clear();
  t.fakes.kube.objects.clear();
  t.fakes.notifier.created.length = 0;
  t.fakes.notifier.existing.length = 0;
  t.fakes.notifier.deleted.length = 0;
  await t.sql('DELETE FROM metrics."devspaces-metrics"');
  t.fakes.kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-1001', 'pp1', '1001');
});

describe('personal access tokens', () => {
  const url = (name = 'work') => `/api/v1/pp1/pats/${name}`;

  it('runs the full create → list → view → update → delete lifecycle', async () => {
    const created = await t.api('post', url()).send({ csiid: '1001', token: SAMPLE_PAT });
    expect(created.status).toBe(201);
    expect(created.body).toMatchObject({ name: 'work', namespace: 'pp1-devspaces-1001' });

    expect((await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/pats')).body).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ name: 'work' }),
    ]);

    const view = await t.api('get', `${url()}?csiid=1001`);
    expect(view.status).toBe(200);
    expect(view.body.token).toBe(`gh…${SAMPLE_PAT.slice(-3)}`);
    expect(JSON.stringify(view.body)).not.toContain(SAMPLE_PAT);

    const next = `ghp_${'Q'.repeat(36)}`;
    expect((await t.api('put', url()).send({ csiid: '1001', token: next })).status).toBe(200);

    expect((await t.api('delete', `${url()}?csiid=1001`)).body.message).toBe("PAT 'work' deleted.");
    expect((await t.api('delete', `${url()}?csiid=1001`)).body.message).toMatch(/not found/);
  });

  it('records PAT events without the token', async () => {
    await t.api('post', url()).send({ csiid: '1001', token: SAMPLE_PAT });
    await t.api('put', url()).send({ csiid: '1001', token: SAMPLE_PAT });
    await t.api('delete', `${url()}?csiid=1001`);
    const rows = await t.metricsRows();
    expect(rows.map((r) => `${r.eventtype}:${r.resourcetype}`)).toEqual([
      'create:pat',
      'update:pat',
      'delete:pat',
    ]);
    expect(JSON.stringify(rows)).not.toContain(SAMPLE_PAT);
  });

  it('409 on duplicate, 404 on unknown, 400 on bad token or csiid', async () => {
    await t.api('post', url()).send({ csiid: '1001', token: SAMPLE_PAT });
    expect((await t.api('post', url()).send({ csiid: '1001', token: SAMPLE_PAT })).status).toBe(409);
    expect((await t.api('get', `${url('ghost')}?csiid=1001`)).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await t.api('put', url('ghost')).send({ csiid: '1001', token: SAMPLE_PAT })).status).toBe(404);
    const bad = await t.api('post', url('other')).send({ csiid: '1001', token: 'nope' });
    expect(bad.status).toBe(400);
    expect(bad.body.code).toBe('INVALID_PAT');
    expect((await t.api('get', `${url()}`)).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await t.api('post', url('Bad_Name!')).send({ csiid: '1001', token: SAMPLE_PAT })).status).toBe(
      400,
    );
  });

  it('404s for a project that does not exist (and does not create it)', async () => {
    const res = await t.api('post', url()).send({ csiid: '9999', token: SAMPLE_PAT });
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('PROJECT_NOT_FOUND');
    expect(t.fakes.kube.namespaces.has('pp1-devspaces-9999')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('git config', () => {
  const url = '/api/v1/pp1/gitconfig';
  const content = '[user]\n  name = Pat\n';

  it('supports put → get → delete', async () => {
    expect((await t.api('get', `${url}?csiid=1001`)).status).toBe(404);
    const put = await t.api('put', url).send({ csiid: '1001', content });
    expect(put.status).toBe(200);
    expect(put.body).toEqual({
      name: 'workspace-userdata-gitconfig-configmap',
      namespace: 'pp1-devspaces-1001',
      content,
    });
    expect((await t.api('get', `${url}?csiid=1001`)).body.content).toBe(content);
    expect((await t.api('delete', `${url}?csiid=1001`)).body.message).toBe('Git config deleted.');
    expect((await t.api('delete', `${url}?csiid=1001`)).body.message).toMatch(/nothing to delete/);
  });

  it('records create then update', async () => {
    await t.api('put', url).send({ csiid: '1001', content });
    await t.api('put', url).send({ csiid: '1001', content: `${content}\n` });
    expect((await t.metricsRows()).map((r) => r.eventtype)).toEqual(['create', 'update']);
  });

  it('validates input', async () => {
    expect((await t.api('put', url).send({ csiid: '1001', content: '' })).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await t.api('get', url)).status).toBe(400);
  });
});

describe('deployment projects', () => {
  const url = '/api/v1/pp1/deployment-projects';
  const name = 'pp1-devspaces-deploy-1001';

  it('creates once (201), then reports the existing one (200)', async () => {
    const first = await t.api('post', url).send({ csiid: '1001' });
    expect(first.status).toBe(201);
    expect(first.body).toEqual({ exists: false, name });
    expect(t.fakes.notifier.created).toEqual([{ soeid: 'pp1', namespace: name }]);

    const second = await t.api('post', url).send({ csiid: '1001' });
    expect(second.status).toBe(200);
    expect(second.body).toEqual({ exists: true, name });
    expect(t.fakes.notifier.existing).toHaveLength(1);

    const rows = await t.metricsRows();
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(rows[0]).toMatchObject({
      eventtype: 'create',
      resourcetype: 'deployment_namespace',
      csiid: '1001',
    });
  });

  it('lists and deletes the user’s deployment projects', async () => {
    await t.api('post', url).send({ csiid: '1001' });
    await t.api('post', url).send({ csiid: '1002' });
    expect((await t.api('get', url)).body.sort()).toEqual([name, 'pp1-devspaces-deploy-1002']);

    const res = await t.api('delete', url);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.deleted).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(res.body.failed).toEqual([]);
    expect((await t.api('get', url)).body).toEqual([]);
    expect(t.fakes.notifier.deleted).toHaveLength(1);
    expect((await t.metricsRows()).filter((r) => r.eventtype === 'delete')).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('deleting when there are none is a harmless 200', async () => {
    const res = await t.api('delete', url);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ exists: false, deleted: [] });
  });

  it('validates csiid', async () => {
    expect((await t.api('post', url).send({})).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await t.api('post', url).send({ csiid: 'x' })).status).toBe(400);
  });

  it('never touches another user’s deployment projects', async () => {
    t.fakes.kube.seedNamespace('pp2-devspaces-deploy-1', 'pp2');
    await t.api('delete', url);
    expect(t.fakes.kube.namespaces.has('pp2-devspaces-deploy-1')).toBe(true);
  });
});

/tests/integration/request-logging.test.ts
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { COLORS, RESET } from '../../src/utils/log-format.js';
import { createLogger } from '../../src/utils/logger.js';
import { startIntegration, type Integration } from '../helpers/integration.js';

/** Terminal output exactly as an operator sees it: pretty format with colours. */
const raw: string[] = [];
let t: Integration;

beforeAll(async () => {
  const logger = createLogger('debug', (line) => raw.push(line), {}, { format: 'pretty', color: true });
  t = await startIntegration({}, { logger });
});
afterAll(() => t.close());
beforeEach(() => {
  raw.length = 0;
});

const findRequest = (path: string) => raw.find((l) => l.includes('| request ') && l.includes(path));
/** The line is written when the response has been sent, which can be a moment after the client has it. */
async function requestLine(path: string): Promise<string | undefined> {
  for (let i = 0; i < 50 && !findRequest(path); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 10));
  return findRequest(path);
}
const strip = (line: string) =>
  line
    .replaceAll(COLORS.error, '')
    .replaceAll(COLORS.warn, '')
    .replaceAll(COLORS.success, '')
    .replaceAll(COLORS.info, '')
    .replaceAll(RESET, '');

describe('what the terminal shows for each API call', () => {
  it('a successful call is green, with method, path, status and duration', async () => {
    await request(t.app).get('/api/v1/healthz');
    const line = await requestLine('/api/v1/healthz');
    expect(line?.startsWith(COLORS.success)).toBe(true);
    expect(strip(line as string)).toMatch(
      /\| OK {4}\| request +\| GET +\/api\/v1\/healthz -> 200 \(\d+ ms\) {2}requestId=[0-9a-f-]{36}$/,
    );
  });

  it('shows the FULL path, also for successful calls handled inside nested routers', async () => {
    t.fakes.kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-1001', 'pp1', '1001');
    await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/projects?csiid=1001');
    await request(t.app)
      .post('/api/v1/login')
      .send({ username: 'orion-admin', password: 'correct horse battery staple' });
    expect(strip((await requestLine('/api/v1/pp1/projects')) as string)).toContain(
      'GET    /api/v1/pp1/projects -> 200',
    );
    expect(strip((await requestLine('/api/v1/login')) as string)).toContain('POST   /api/v1/login -> 200');
    // the query string is not part of the logged path
    expect(raw.join('\n')).not.toContain('csiid=1001 ->');
    expect(raw.join('\n')).not.toContain('?csiid');
  });

  it('a call rejected for the caller (401, 404, 409 ...) is orange', async () => {
    await request(t.app).get('/api/v1/pp1/workspaces'); // no token
    const line = await requestLine('/api/v1/pp1/workspaces');
    expect(line?.startsWith(COLORS.warn)).toBe(true);
    expect(strip(line as string)).toContain('| WARN  | request');
    expect(strip(line as string)).toContain('-> 401');
  });

  it('a call that fails on our side (5xx) is red, and the cause is logged in red too', async () => {
    t.fakes.kube.failOnce('listNamespaces', new Error('boom: the cluster exploded'));
    const res = await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces');
    expect(res.status).toBe(500);

    const line = await requestLine('/api/v1/pp1/workspaces');
    expect(line?.startsWith(COLORS.error)).toBe(true);
    expect(strip(line as string)).toContain('| ERROR | request');
    expect(strip(line as string)).toContain('-> 500');

    const cause = raw.find((l) => l.includes('request.crashed'));
    expect(cause?.startsWith(COLORS.error)).toBe(true);
    expect(strip(cause as string)).toContain('error="boom: the cluster exploded"');
  });

  it('a user action that worked (create) is green: the call and the workspace event', async () => {
    const res = await t
      .api('post', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces')
      .send({ devworkspaceValue: 'java-maven', csiid: '1001' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect((await requestLine('/api/v1/pp1/workspaces'))?.startsWith(COLORS.success)).toBe(true);
    const created = raw.find((l) => l.includes('workspace.created'));
    expect(created?.startsWith(COLORS.success)).toBe(true);
    expect(strip(created as string)).toMatch(
      /\| OK {4}\| workspace\.created +\| soeid=pp1 {2}namespace=pp1-devspaces-1001 {2}workspace=java-maven-[0-9a-f]{4}$/,
    );
  });

  it('start and stop are green successes too', async () => {
    const created = await t
      .api('post', '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces')
      .send({ devworkspaceValue: 'python', csiid: '1001' });
    const id = created.body.id as string;
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase('pp1-devspaces-1001', id, 'Running');
    raw.length = 0;
    await t.api('post', `/api/v1/pp1/workspaces/${id}/stop`);
    expect(raw.find((l) => l.includes('workspace.stopped'))?.startsWith(COLORS.success)).toBe(true);
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase('pp1-devspaces-1001', id, 'Stopped');
    await t.api('post', `/api/v1/pp1/workspaces/${id}/start`);
    expect(raw.find((l) => l.includes('workspace.started'))?.startsWith(COLORS.success)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('never prints a secret, and every entry stays readable text', async () => {
    const pat = `ghp_${'a1B2c3D4e5F6g7H8i9J0k1L2m3N4o5P6q7R8'}`;
    t.fakes.kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-1001', 'pp1', '1001');
    await t.api('post', '/api/v1/pp1/pats/main').send({ csiid: '1001', token: pat });
    expect(raw.join('\n')).not.toContain(pat);
    for (const line of raw) expect(line).not.toMatch(/\{"time"|\[object Object\]|undefined/);
  });
});

/tests/integration/workspaces.test.ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { startIntegration, type Integration } from '../helpers/integration.js';

let t: Integration;
beforeAll(async () => {
  t = await startIntegration();
});
afterAll(() => t.close());
beforeEach(async () => {
  t.fakes.kube.namespaces.clear();
  t.fakes.kube.objects.clear();
  await t.sql('DELETE FROM metrics."devspaces-metrics"');
  t.registry.failing = false;
});

const base = '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces';
const body = { devworkspaceValue: 'java-maven', csiid: '1001' };

describe('GET /api/v1/catalog', () => {
  it('returns the registry catalog over real HTTP', async () => {
    const res = await t.api('get', '/api/v1/catalog');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.map((e: { name: string }) => e.name)).toEqual(['java-maven', 'python']);
  });

  it('answers 502 when the registry is down', async () => {
    t.registry.failing = true;
    const res = await t.api('get', '/api/v1/catalog');
    expect(res.status).toBe(502);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('UPSTREAM_ERROR');
  });
});

describe('POST /api/v1/:soeid/workspaces', () => {
  it('creates the project and the workspace, and records both', async () => {
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({
      namespace: 'pp1-devspaces-1001',
      csiid: '1001',
      stack: 'java-maven',
      version: '2.0.0',
      displayName: 'Java Maven',
      editor: 'Visual Studio Code',
      limitcpu: 2,
      limitmemory: 4,
      storage: 3,
    });

    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces('pp1-devspaces-1001')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(registryPaths()).toEqual(['GET /v2index/all', 'GET /devfiles/java-maven/2.0.0']);

    const rows = await t.metricsRows();
    expect(rows.map((r) => `${r.eventtype}:${r.resourcetype}`)).toEqual([
      'create:project',
      'create:workspace',
    ]);
    expect(rows[1]).toMatchObject({
      soeid: 'pp1',
      csiid: '1001',
      workspaceversion: '2.0.0',
      editorname: 'vscode',
    });
    expect(Number(rows[1]?.limitcpu)).toBe(2);
    expect(rows[1]?.status).toEqual({ failed: false, error: '', message: '' });
  });

  it('does not recreate the project for a second workspace', async () => {
    await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    const rows = await t.metricsRows();
    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.resourcetype === 'project')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.resourcetype === 'workspace')).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('honours editor, stack version and repository', async () => {
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send({
      ...body,
      editor: 'intellij',
      stackVersion: '1.0.0',
      repositoryUrl: 'https://github.com/acme/app.git',
      repositoryBranch: 'main',
    });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.version).toBe('1.0.0');
    const dw = t.fakes.kube.workspaces()[0];
    expect(dw?.spec?.template?.projects).toEqual([
      {
        name: 'app',
        git: { remotes: { origin: 'https://github.com/acme/app.git' }, checkoutFrom: { revision: 'main' } },
      },
    ]);
    expect(dw?.spec?.template?.components?.some((c) => c.name === 'che-idea-server')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('answers 404 for an unknown stack and creates nothing', async () => {
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send({ ...body, devworkspaceValue: 'cobol' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'WORKSPACE_CONFIG_NOT_FOUND' });
    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('answers 502 when the registry fails mid-create', async () => {
    t.registry.failing = true;
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(502);
    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it.each([
    ['missing stack', { csiid: '1' }],
    ['bad csiid', { ...body, csiid: 'x' }],
    ['unknown field', { ...body, sneaky: true }],
    ['http repository', { ...body, repositoryUrl: 'http://github.com/a/b' }],
  ])('answers 400 for %s', async (_l, payload) => {
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(payload);
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('VALIDATION_ERROR');
  });

  it('answers 409 once the running limit (2) is reached', async () => {
    await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'WORKSPACE_RUN_COUNT_EXCEEDED' });
    expect(res.body.message).toMatch(/limit is 2/);
  });

  it('answers 409 once the total limit (3) is reached', async () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      t.fakes.kube.setPhase('pp1-devspaces-1001', res.body.id, 'Stopped');
    }
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('WORKSPACE_COUNT_EXCEEDED');
  });

  it('records a failed metric and answers 502 when the cluster rejects the workspace', async () => {
    t.fakes.kube.failOnce('create:DevWorkspace', Object.assign(new Error('denied'), { code: 500 }));
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(500);
    const failed = (await t.metricsRows()).find((r) => r.resourcetype === 'workspace');
    expect(failed?.status).toMatchObject({ failed: true });
  });

  it('refuses to use a namespace owned by someone else', async () => {
    t.fakes.kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-1001', 'intruder', '1001');
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('NAMESPACE_OWNERSHIP_MISMATCH');
  });
});

describe('workspace lifecycle', () => {
  async function created() {
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    return res.body as { id: string; namespace: string };
  }

  it('lists and gets only the caller’s workspaces', async () => {
    const ws = await created();
    expect((await t.api('get', base)).body.map((w: { id: string }) => w.id)).toEqual([ws.id]);
    expect((await t.api('get', `${base}/${ws.id}`)).body.id).toBe(ws.id);
    expect((await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp2/workspaces')).body).toEqual([]);
    expect((await t.api('get', `/api/v1/pp2/workspaces/${ws.id}`)).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it('reflects the cluster’s phase and URL', async () => {
    const ws = await created();
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(ws.namespace, ws.id, 'Running', 'https://ws.example/ide');
    const res = await t.api('get', `${base}/${ws.id}`);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ phase: 'Running', mainUrl: 'https://ws.example/ide' });
  });

  it('stops then starts, with metrics for each', async () => {
    const ws = await created();
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(ws.namespace, ws.id, 'Running');
    t.fakes.clock.advance(3 * 60 * 1000);

    const stop = await t.api('post', `${base}/${ws.id}/stop`);
    expect(stop.status).toBe(200);
    expect(stop.body.message).toBe(`Workspace with id ${ws.id} is stopping.`);
    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces()[0]?.spec?.started).toBe(false);

    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(ws.namespace, ws.id, 'Stopped');
    const start = await t.api('post', `${base}/${ws.id}/start`);
    expect(start.status).toBe(200);
    expect(start.body.message).toBe(`Workspace with id ${ws.id} is starting.`);
    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces()[0]?.spec?.started).toBe(true);

    const rows = await t.metricsRows();
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.eventtype)).toEqual(['create', 'create', 'stop', 'start']);
    expect(rows[2]?.duration).toBe('3 minutes');
  });

  it('refuses to start a third running workspace', async () => {
    const a = await created();
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(a.namespace, a.id, 'Stopped');
    const b = await created();
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(b.namespace, b.id, 'Stopped');
    const c = await created();
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(c.namespace, c.id, 'Stopped');
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(a.namespace, a.id, 'Running');
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(b.namespace, b.id, 'Running');
    const res = await t.api('post', `${base}/${c.id}/start`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
  });

  it('deletes a workspace and records its lifetime', async () => {
    const ws = await created();
    t.fakes.clock.advance(2 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const res = await t.api('delete', `${base}/${ws.id}`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.message).toBe(`Workspace with id ${ws.id} is terminating.`);
    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(0);
    const last = (await t.metricsRows()).at(-1);
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ eventtype: 'delete', duration: '2 hours' });
  });

  it.each([
    ['get', ''],
    ['delete', ''],
    ['post', '/start'],
    ['post', '/stop'],
  ] as const)('answers 404 for %s %s on an unknown workspace', async (method, suffix) => {
    const res = await t.api(method, `${base}/ghost${suffix}`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND');
  });

  it('GET /projects lists the user’s projects', async () => {
    await created();
    const res = await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/projects');
    expect(res.body).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ name: 'pp1-devspaces-1001', csiid: '1001', soeid: 'pp1' }),
    ]);
  });
});

function registryPaths(): string[] {
  return t.registry.requests.slice(-2);
}

/tests/unit/config/env.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  clusterNameOf,
  deriveDevSpacesFqdn,
  findClusterToken,
  loadConfig,
  normalizeEditorId,
  startupSummary,
  unquote,
} from '../../../src/config/env.js';

/** The variables the team really exports (secret values replaced by fakes; quotes as they appear in the notes file). */
const TEAM_ENV = {
  POSTGRES_DATABASE: 'orion_dev_metadata',
  POSTGRES_HOSTNAME: 'NAORI01DPS_INSTANCE1.pgaas.dyn.nsroot.net',
  POSTGRES_PASSWORD: 'fake-db-password',
  POSTGRES_PORT: "'1524'",
  POSTGRES_USER: 'app_orion_pgsql_dev',
  POSTGRES_DATABASE_TABLENAME: `'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"'`,
  NODE_ENV: 'development',
  devOpsToken004d: 'fake-devops-token-004d',
  devSpacesClusterToken001d: 'fake-devspaces-token-001d',
  CLUSTER_API_URL: 'https://api.namctigtd001d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443',
  DEVREG_URL: 'https://devreg-service-cti-svcs-devfile-registry-177398.apps.namctigtd001d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net',
  PORT: '8080',
  EDITOR_VSCODE: 'che-incubator/che-code/latest',
  EDITOR_INTELLIJ: 'che-incubator/che-idea-server/latest',
  SECRET_KEY: 'fake-secret-key-0123456789abcdef',
  ADMIN_USERNAME: 'orion-devspaces',
  ADMIN_PASSWORD: 'fake-admin-password',
  NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED: "'0'",
};

const base = {
  SECRET_KEY: 'a-sufficiently-long-secret',
  ADMIN_USERNAME: 'admin',
  ADMIN_PASSWORD: 'pw',
  DEVREG_URL: 'http://registry',
  POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'false',
};

describe('loadConfig', () => {
  it('applies defaults', () => {
    const c = loadConfig(base);
    expect(c.port).toBe(3050);
    expect(c.limits).toEqual({ maxRunning: 3, maxTotal: 5 });
    expect(c.devreg.editors).toEqual({
      vscode: 'che-incubator/che-code/latest',
      intellij: 'che-incubator/che-idea/latest',
      goland: 'che-incubator/che-goland/latest',
    });
    expect(c.auth.expiresIn).toBe('60d');
    expect(c.postgres.enabled).toBe(false);
    expect(c.github).toEqual({ url: 'https://github.com', userSuffix: '' });
  });

  it('refuses to start without SECRET_KEY (no fallback secret)', () => {
    const { SECRET_KEY: _omit, ...rest } = base;
    expect(() => loadConfig(rest)).toThrow(/SECRET_KEY/);
  });

  it('rejects a short SECRET_KEY', () => {
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, SECRET_KEY: 'short' })).toThrow(/at least 16/);
  });

  it('lists every problem at once', () => {
    expect(() => loadConfig({})).toThrow(
      /SECRET_KEY[\s\S]*ADMIN_USERNAME[\s\S]*ADMIN_PASSWORD[\s\S]*DEVREG_URL/,
    );
  });

  it('treats empty variables as unset', () => {
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, PORT: '', KUBECONFIG_PATH: '' }).port).toBe(3050);
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, KUBECONFIG_PATH: '' }).cluster.kubeconfigPath).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('coerces numbers and rejects non-positive ones', () => {
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, PORT: '8080', MAX_RUNNING_WORKSPACES: '7' }).limits.maxRunning).toBe(7);
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, PORT: '0' })).toThrow(/PORT/);
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, PORT: 'abc' })).toThrow(/PORT/);
  });

  it('requires database coordinates only when Postgres is enabled', () => {
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'true' })).toThrow(
      /POSTGRES_DATABASE and POSTGRES_USER/,
    );
    const ok = loadConfig({ ...base, POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'true', POSTGRES_DATABASE: 'd', POSTGRES_USER: 'u' });
    expect(ok.postgres).toMatchObject({ enabled: true, database: 'd', user: 'u', port: 5432, ssl: true });
  });

  it('uses TLS to Postgres by default, as the original service always did', () => {
    const env = { ...base, POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'true', POSTGRES_DATABASE: 'd', POSTGRES_USER: 'u' };
    expect(loadConfig(env).postgres).toMatchObject({ ssl: true, env: 'development', caFile: undefined });
    expect(loadConfig({ ...env, POSTGRES_SSL: 'false' }).postgres.ssl).toBe(false);
    expect(loadConfig({ ...env, NODE_ENV: 'production' }).postgres.env).toBe('production');
  });

  it('accepts explicit CA overrides', () => {
    const env = { ...base, POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'true', POSTGRES_DATABASE: 'd', POSTGRES_USER: 'u' };
    const pg = loadConfig({ ...env, POSTGRES_CA_FILE: '/ca.pem', POSTGRES_CERT_DIR: '/certs' }).postgres;
    expect(pg).toMatchObject({ caFile: '/ca.pem', certDir: '/certs' });
  });

  describe('the existing Orion database environment', () => {
    // Exactly what the team exports (quotes included, as they appear when pasted from the notes file).
    const exported = {
      POSTGRES_DATABASE: 'orion_dev_metadata',
      POSTGRES_HOSTNAME: 'NAORI01DPS_INSTANCE1.pgaas.dyn.nsroot.net',
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: 'secret',
      POSTGRES_PORT: "'1524'",
      POSTGRES_USER: 'app_orion_pgsql_dev',
      POSTGRES_DATABASE_TABLENAME: `'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"'`,
      NODE_ENV: 'development',
    };

    it('is understood as-is, with or without the shell quotes', () => {
      for (const env of [
        exported,
        {
          ...exported,
          POSTGRES_PORT: '1524',
          POSTGRES_DATABASE_TABLENAME: 'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"',
        },
      ]) {
        expect(loadConfig({ ...base, POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'true', ...env }).postgres).toEqual({
          enabled: true,
          host: 'NAORI01DPS_INSTANCE1.pgaas.dyn.nsroot.net',
          port: 1524,
          database: 'orion_dev_metadata',
          user: 'app_orion_pgsql_dev',
          password: 'secret',
          ssl: true,
          caFile: undefined,
          certDir: undefined,
          env: 'development',
          table: 'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"',
        });
      }
    });

    it('defaults to Postgres being on, so these variables alone are enough', () => {
      const { POSTGRES_ENABLED: _off, ...withoutFlag } = base;
      expect(loadConfig({ ...withoutFlag, ...exported }).postgres.enabled).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  it('unquote only unwraps one matching pair', () => {
    expect(unquote("'a'")).toBe('a');
    expect(unquote('"a b"')).toBe('a b');
    expect(unquote(`'metrics."t-1"'`)).toBe('metrics."t-1"');
    for (const untouched of ["'a", 'a"', `'a"`, 'plain', "it's", '"', "''".slice(0, 1)]) {
      expect(unquote(untouched)).toBe(untouched);
    }
    expect(unquote("''")).toBe('');
  });

  it('strips a trailing slash from GITHUB_URL', () => {
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, GITHUB_URL: 'https://ghe.example.com/' }).github.url).toBe(
      'https://ghe.example.com',
    );
  });

  it('rejects an unknown LOG_LEVEL', () => {
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, LOG_LEVEL: 'loud' })).toThrow(/LOG_LEVEL/);
  });
});

describe("the team's real environment (every variable they export)", () => {
  const c = () => loadConfig(TEAM_ENV);

  it('is accepted as it is, and nothing is left unused', () => {
    const cfg = c();
    expect(cfg.port).toBe(8080);
    expect(cfg.nodeEnv).toBe('development');
    expect(cfg.auth).toMatchObject({ adminUsername: 'orion-devspaces', secretKey: TEAM_ENV.SECRET_KEY });
    expect(cfg.devreg.url).toBe(TEAM_ENV.DEVREG_URL);
    expect(cfg.postgres).toMatchObject({
      host: 'NAORI01DPS_INSTANCE1.pgaas.dyn.nsroot.net',
      port: 1524,
      database: 'orion_dev_metadata',
      user: 'app_orion_pgsql_dev',
      table: 'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"',
    });
  });

  it('authenticates to the Dev Spaces cluster with devSpacesClusterToken001d', () => {
    const { cluster } = c();
    expect(cluster.apiUrl).toBe('https://api.namctigtd001d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443');
    expect(cluster.token).toBe('fake-devspaces-token-001d');
    expect(cluster.tokenVar).toBe('devSpacesClusterToken001d');
  });

  it('does not send devOpsToken004d to cluster 001d: that token belongs to cluster 004d', () => {
    const { cluster } = c();
    expect(cluster.deployApiUrl).toBe(cluster.apiUrl); // DEPLOY_CLUSTER_API_URL is commented out
    expect(cluster.deployToken).toBeUndefined();
    expect(JSON.stringify(cluster)).not.toContain('fake-devops-token-004d');
  });

  it('uses devOpsToken004d for a separate deploy cluster 004d, and the right token for each', () => {
    const { cluster } = loadConfig({
      ...TEAM_ENV,
      DEPLOY_CLUSTER_API_URL: 'https://api.namctiswd004d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443',
    });
    expect(cluster.apiUrl).toContain('namctigtd001d');
    expect(cluster.token).toBe('fake-devspaces-token-001d');
    expect(cluster.deployApiUrl).toBe('https://api.namctiswd004d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443');
    expect(cluster.deployToken).toBe('fake-devops-token-004d');
    expect(cluster.deployTokenVar).toBe('devOpsToken004d');
  });

  it('uses the full editor ids exactly as exported (no double che-incubator/…/latest)', () => {
    expect(c().devreg.editors).toMatchObject({
      vscode: 'che-incubator/che-code/latest',
      intellij: 'che-incubator/che-idea-server/latest',
    });
  });

  it('survives the stray empty EDITOR_INTELLIJ="" line the notes file also contains', () => {
    // in a shell the later assignment wins; an empty value must never override a real one or break the default
    expect(loadConfig({ ...TEAM_ENV, EDITOR_INTELLIJ: '' }).devreg.editors.intellij).toBe(
      'che-incubator/che-idea/latest',
    );
  });

  it("honours NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED='0' for the clusters, and verifies by default", () => {
    expect(c().cluster.tlsRejectUnauthorized).toBe(false);
    const { NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED: _off, ...verified } = TEAM_ENV;
    expect(loadConfig(verified).cluster.tlsRejectUnauthorized).toBe(true);
    expect(loadConfig({ ...verified, NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED: '1' }).cluster.tlsRejectUnauthorized).toBe(
      true,
    );
  });

  it('works out the Dev Spaces dashboard address from the cluster URL when it is not set', () => {
    expect(c().cluster.devSpacesServiceFqdn).toBe('https://devspaces.apps.namctigtd001d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net');
    expect(
      loadConfig({ ...TEAM_ENV, DEV_SPACES_SERVICE_FQDN: 'https://ds.example' }).cluster.devSpacesServiceFqdn,
    ).toBe('https://ds.example');
  });

  it('the startup summary names what was understood but contains no secret value', () => {
    const summary = JSON.stringify(startupSummary(c()));
    expect(summary).toContain('bearer token from devSpacesClusterToken001d');
    expect(summary).toContain('NOT verified');
    expect(summary).toContain('orion_dev_metadata');
    for (const secret of [
      'fake-devspaces-token-001d',
      'fake-devops-token-004d',
      'fake-db-password',
      'fake-admin-password',
      TEAM_ENV.SECRET_KEY,
    ]) {
      expect(summary).not.toContain(secret);
    }
  });
});

describe('cluster tokens', () => {
  const URL001 = 'https://api.namctigtd001d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443';

  it('explicit CLUSTER_TOKEN / DEPLOY_CLUSTER_TOKEN win over the by-name lookup', () => {
    const { cluster } = loadConfig({
      ...TEAM_ENV,
      CLUSTER_TOKEN: 'explicit-main',
      DEPLOY_CLUSTER_API_URL: 'https://api.namctiswd004d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443',
      DEPLOY_CLUSTER_TOKEN: 'explicit-deploy',
    });
    expect([cluster.token, cluster.tokenVar]).toEqual(['explicit-main', 'CLUSTER_TOKEN']);
    expect([cluster.deployToken, cluster.deployTokenVar]).toEqual([
      'explicit-deploy',
      'DEPLOY_CLUSTER_TOKEN',
    ]);
  });

  it('refuses a separate deploy cluster for which no token exists', () => {
    const { devOpsToken004d: _no, ...noDevOps } = TEAM_ENV;
    expect(() =>
      loadConfig({
        ...noDevOps,
        DEPLOY_CLUSTER_API_URL: 'https://api.namctiswd004d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443',
      }),
    ).toThrow(/DEPLOY_CLUSTER_API_URL.*no token/s);
    // with a kubeconfig that is fine: it carries its own credentials
    expect(() =>
      loadConfig({
        ...noDevOps,
        KUBECONFIG_PATH: '/kube/config',
        DEPLOY_CLUSTER_API_URL: 'https://api.namctiswd004d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443',
      }),
    ).not.toThrow();
  });

  it('runs without any token when none is set (kubeconfig / service account)', () => {
    const { devSpacesClusterToken001d: _a, devOpsToken004d: _b, ...none } = TEAM_ENV;
    const { cluster } = loadConfig(none);
    expect(cluster.token).toBeUndefined();
    expect(startupSummary(loadConfig(none)).clusterAuth).toBe('default kubeconfig / pod service account');
  });

  it('findClusterToken matches the id against the END of the cluster name, case-insensitively', () => {
    const env = { devSpacesClusterToken001D: 'a', devSpacesClusterToken002d: 'b', devOpsToken001d: 'c' };
    expect(findClusterToken(env, 'devSpacesClusterToken', URL001)).toEqual({
      name: 'devSpacesClusterToken001D',
      value: 'a',
    });
    expect(findClusterToken(env, 'devOpsToken', URL001)?.value).toBe('c');
    expect(
      findClusterToken(env, 'devSpacesClusterToken', 'https://api.other999x.example:6443'),
    ).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('a variable without an id fits any cluster, but two that fit is an error', () => {
    expect(findClusterToken({ devOpsToken: 'x' }, 'devOpsToken', URL001)?.value).toBe('x');
    expect(findClusterToken({ devOpsToken: 'x' }, 'devOpsToken', 'http://localhost')?.value).toBe('x');
    expect(() => findClusterToken({ devOpsToken: 'x', devOpsToken001d: 'y' }, 'devOpsToken', URL001)).toThrow(
      /several/,
    );
  });

  it('ignores empty values and an id that cannot be checked against a name-less cluster URL', () => {
    expect(findClusterToken({ devOpsToken001d: '' }, 'devOpsToken', URL001)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(
      findClusterToken({ devOpsToken001d: 'y' }, 'devOpsToken', 'http://localhost:8001'),
    ).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('logging settings', () => {
  const base = { ...TEAM_ENV };

  it('defaults to the readable coloured format', () => {
    const c = loadConfig(base);
    expect(c.logFormat).toBe('pretty');
    expect(c.logColor).toBe(true);
  });

  it('LOG_FORMAT=json switches to one JSON object per line', () => {
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, LOG_FORMAT: 'json' }).logFormat).toBe('json');
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, LOG_FORMAT: 'xml' })).toThrow(/LOG_FORMAT/);
  });

  it('colours can be switched off with LOG_COLOR=false or the standard NO_COLOR variable', () => {
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, LOG_COLOR: 'false' }).logColor).toBe(false);
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, NO_COLOR: '1' }).logColor).toBe(false);
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, NO_COLOR: '' }).logColor).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('address helpers', () => {
  it('clusterNameOf', () => {
    expect(clusterNameOf('https://api.namctigtd001d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443')).toBe('namctigtd001d');
    expect(clusterNameOf('https://kubernetes.default.svc')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(clusterNameOf('not a url')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(clusterNameOf('')).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('deriveDevSpacesFqdn', () => {
    expect(deriveDevSpacesFqdn('https://api.prod1.ecs.example.net:6443')).toBe(
      'https://devspaces.apps.prod1.ecs.example.net',
    );
    expect(deriveDevSpacesFqdn('https://10.0.0.1:6443')).toBe('');
    expect(deriveDevSpacesFqdn('')).toBe('');
  });

  it('normalizeEditorId keeps full ids and expands bare names', () => {
    expect(normalizeEditorId('che-incubator/che-code/latest')).toBe('che-incubator/che-code/latest');
    expect(normalizeEditorId('acme/my-editor/1.2')).toBe('acme/my-editor/1.2');
    expect(normalizeEditorId('che-code')).toBe('che-incubator/che-code/latest');
    expect(normalizeEditorId(' che-idea ')).toBe('che-incubator/che-idea/latest');
  });
});

/tests/unit/factories/devfile-updater.test.ts
import { dump, load } from 'js-yaml';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Devfile } from '../../../src/types.js';
import { applyEditorProfile, parseDevfile } from '../../../src/factories/devfile-updater.js';
import { BadRequestError } from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';
import { SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML } from '../../helpers/sample-devfile.js';

const sample = (): Devfile => parseDevfile(SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML);
const byName = (d: Devfile, name: string) => d.components?.find((c) => c.name === name);

describe('parseDevfile', () => {
  it('parses a devfile', () => {
    expect(sample().metadata?.name).toBe('java-maven');
  });
  it.each([
    ['invalid yaml', 'a: [unclosed'],
    ['a list', '- 1\n- 2'],
    ['a scalar', 'hello'],
    ['no components', 'schemaVersion: 2.2.0'],
    ['empty', ''],
  ])('rejects %s', (_label, text) => {
    expect(() => parseDevfile(text)).toThrow(BadRequestError);
  });
});

describe('applyEditorProfile', () => {
  it('leaves the devfile alone for VS Code (but returns a copy)', () => {
    const original = sample();
    const result = applyEditorProfile(original, 'vscode');
    expect(result).toEqual(original);
    expect(result).not.toBe(original);
    result.components?.pop();
    expect(original.components).toHaveLength(4);
  });

  it('never mutates its input', () => {
    const original = sample();
    const snapshot = structuredClone(original);
    applyEditorProfile(original, 'intellij');
    expect(original).toEqual(snapshot);
  });

  describe.each([
    ['intellij', 'che-idea-server', 2222, 'che-idea-server.eclipse.org'],
    ['goland', 'che-goland-server', 3400, 'che-goland-server.eclipse.org'],
  ] as const)('%s', (editor, server, port, partOf) => {
    const result = applyEditorProfile(sample(), editor);
    const tools = byName(result, 'tools');

    it('drops commands and events', () => {
      expect(result.commands).toBeUndefined();
      expect(result.events).toBeUndefined();
    });
    it('exposes the editor server endpoint on the tools container', () => {
      expect(tools?.container?.endpoints).toEqual([
        expect.objectContaining({
          name: server,
          targetPort: port,
          exposure: 'public',
          protocol: 'https',
          secure: true,
        }),
      ]);
      expect(tools?.container?.env).toBeUndefined();
      expect(tools?.attributes?.['app.kubernetes.io/part-of']).toBe(partOf);
    });
    it('moves the editor volume mount and removes venv', () => {
      expect(tools?.container?.volumeMounts).toEqual([{ name: server, path: `/${server}` }]);
      expect(byName(result, 'venv')).toBeUndefined();
      expect(byName(result, 'checode')).toBeUndefined();
      expect(byName(result, server)?.volume).toEqual({});
    });
    it('resizes the home volume', () => {
      expect(byName(result, 'home')?.volume?.size).toBe('10Gi');
    });
  });

  it('accepts a custom home volume size', () => {
    expect(byName(applyEditorProfile(sample(), 'goland', '20Gi'), 'home')?.volume?.size).toBe('20Gi');
  });

  it('tolerates a stack without a tools component', () => {
    const d: Devfile = { components: [{ name: 'other', container: { image: 'x' } }] };
    expect(applyEditorProfile(d, 'intellij').components).toEqual(d.components);
  });

  it('tolerates a tools container without volume mounts', () => {
    const d: Devfile = { components: [{ name: 'tools', container: { image: 'x' } }] };
    const tools = byName(applyEditorProfile(d, 'intellij'), 'tools');
    expect(tools?.container?.endpoints).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe('editor profile YAML serialization', () => {
  it('round-trips through YAML', () => {
    const out = load(dump(applyEditorProfile(parseDevfile(SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML), 'goland'))) as Devfile;
    expect(byName(out, 'che-goland-server')).toBeDefined();
  });
});

/tests/unit/factories/workspace-factory.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Devfile } from '../../../src/types.js';
import { buildLogRecord } from '../../../src/factories/log-factory.js';
import { buildDevWorkspaceObject, WORKSPACE_LABELS } from '../../../src/factories/workspace-factory.js';
import { parseDevfile } from '../../../src/factories/devfile-updater.js';
import { SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML } from '../../helpers/sample-devfile.js';

const args = (over: Partial<Parameters<typeof buildDevWorkspaceObject>[0]> = {}) => ({
  name: 'java-maven-ab12',
  namespace: 'pp1-devspaces-1',
  csiid: '1',
  stack: 'java-maven',
  stackVersion: '2.0.0',
  devfile: parseDevfile(SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML),
  editor: 'vscode' as const,
  editorId: 'che-incubator/che-code/latest',
  devSpacesServiceFqdn: 'https://devspaces.test',
  ...over,
});

describe('buildDevWorkspaceObject', () => {
  it('builds a started DevWorkspace with the stack inlined and the editor contributed', () => {
    const dw = buildDevWorkspaceObject(args());
    expect(dw).toMatchObject({
      apiVersion: 'workspace.devfile.io/v1alpha2',
      kind: 'DevWorkspace',
      metadata: { name: 'java-maven-ab12', namespace: 'pp1-devspaces-1' },
      spec: { started: true, routingClass: 'che' },
    });
    expect(dw.spec?.template?.components).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(dw.spec?.template?.commands).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(dw.spec?.template?.events).toBeDefined();
    expect(dw.spec?.contributions).toEqual([
      {
        name: 'ide',
        uri: 'https://devspaces.test/dashboard/api/editors/devfile?che-editor=che-incubator/che-code/latest',
      },
    ]);
  });

  it('uses the editor id exactly as configured, e.g. the team’s che-idea-server value', () => {
    const dw = buildDevWorkspaceObject(
      args({ editor: 'intellij', editorId: 'che-incubator/che-idea-server/latest' }),
    );
    expect(dw.spec?.contributions?.[0]?.uri).toBe(
      'https://devspaces.test/dashboard/api/editors/devfile?che-editor=che-incubator/che-idea-server/latest',
    );
    expect(dw.spec?.contributions?.[0]?.uri).not.toMatch(/che-incubator.*che-incubator/);
  });

  it('records stack metadata in annotations and labels', () => {
    const dw = buildDevWorkspaceObject(
      args({ editor: 'goland', editorId: 'che-incubator/che-goland/latest' }),
    );
    expect(dw.metadata?.annotations).toMatchObject({
      dw: 'java-maven-ab12',
      workspaceVersion: '2.0.0',
      workspaceDisplayName: 'Java Maven',
      workspaceDescription: 'Java 17 with Maven',
      editorDisplayName: 'GoLand',
    });
    expect(dw.metadata?.labels).toEqual({
      [WORKSPACE_LABELS.csiid]: '1',
      [WORKSPACE_LABELS.stack]: 'java-maven',
      [WORKSPACE_LABELS.managedBy]: 'workspace-mgr',
    });
  });

  it('adds the requested repository as a project, with a branch when given', () => {
    const dw = buildDevWorkspaceObject(
      args({ repositoryUrl: 'https://github.com/acme/My_Repo.git', repositoryBranch: 'develop' }),
    );
    expect(dw.spec?.template?.projects).toEqual([
      {
        name: 'my-repo',
        git: {
          remotes: { origin: 'https://github.com/acme/My_Repo.git' },
          checkoutFrom: { revision: 'develop' },
        },
      },
    ]);
  });

  it('keeps stack projects and omits checkoutFrom without a branch', () => {
    const devfile: Devfile = { components: [], projects: [{ name: 'starter' }] };
    const dw = buildDevWorkspaceObject(args({ devfile, repositoryUrl: 'https://github.com/acme/app' }));
    expect(dw.spec?.template?.projects).toEqual([
      { name: 'starter' },
      { name: 'app', git: { remotes: { origin: 'https://github.com/acme/app' } } },
    ]);
  });

  it('omits projects, commands and events when the stack has none', () => {
    const dw = buildDevWorkspaceObject(args({ devfile: { components: [] } }));
    expect(dw.spec?.template).not.toHaveProperty('projects');
    expect(dw.spec?.template).not.toHaveProperty('commands');
    expect(dw.spec?.template).not.toHaveProperty('events');
  });

  it('falls back to the stack name for the display name', () => {
    const dw = buildDevWorkspaceObject(args({ devfile: { components: [] } }));
    expect(dw.metadata?.annotations?.workspaceDisplayName).toBe('java-maven');
  });
});

describe('buildLogRecord', () => {
  const now = new Date('2026-01-01T00:00:00Z');
  it('fills the legacy defaults', () => {
    expect(buildLogRecord({ soeid: 'pp1', eventtype: 'create', resourcetype: 'workspace' }, now)).toEqual({
      timestamp: now,
      csiid: '',
      soeid: 'pp1',
      eventtype: 'create',
      resourcename: '',
      resourcetype: 'workspace',
      status: { failed: false, error: '', message: '' },
      useraction: 'user',
      limitcpu: 0,
      limitmemory: 0,
      requestcpu: 0,
      requestmemory: 0,
      duration: '0 seconds',
      storage: 0,
      workspaceversion: '',
      editorname: '',
    });
  });
  it('keeps what is given', () => {
    const r = buildLogRecord(
      {
        soeid: 'pp1',
        eventtype: 'delete',
        resourcetype: 'workspace',
        limitcpu: 2,
        useraction: 'system',
        csiid: '9',
      },
      now,
    );
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ limitcpu: 2, useraction: 'system', csiid: '9' });
  });
});

/tests/unit/http/auth.test.ts
import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from 'express';
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  authenticate,
  authorizeSoeid,
  checkAdminCredentials,
  getPrincipal,
  safeEqual,
  signToken,
} from '../../../src/http/auth.js';
import { ForbiddenError, UnauthorizedError, BadRequestError } from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';

const auth = {
  secretKey: 'unit-test-secret-0123456789',
  adminUsername: 'admin',
  adminPassword: 'pw',
  expiresIn: '1h',
};

function run(handler: (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => void, req: Partial<Request>) {
  const res = { locals: {} } as Response;
  let error: unknown;
  let called = false;
  handler({ headers: {}, params: {}, ...req } as Request, res, ((e?: unknown) => {
    called = true;
    error = e;
  }) as NextFunction);
  return { res, error, called };
}

describe('safeEqual / checkAdminCredentials', () => {
  it('compares strings of any length', () => {
    expect(safeEqual('a', 'a')).toBe(true);
    expect(safeEqual('a', 'b')).toBe(false);
    expect(safeEqual('a', 'aa')).toBe(false);
    expect(safeEqual('', '')).toBe(true);
  });
  it('requires both username and password', () => {
    expect(checkAdminCredentials(auth, 'admin', 'pw')).toBe(true);
    expect(checkAdminCredentials(auth, 'admin', 'x')).toBe(false);
    expect(checkAdminCredentials(auth, 'x', 'pw')).toBe(false);
    expect(checkAdminCredentials(auth, '', '')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('signToken', () => {
  it('issues an HS256 token with subject and role', () => {
    const token = signToken(auth, { soeid: 'admin', role: 'admin' });
    const decoded = jwt.verify(token, auth.secretKey, { complete: true });
    expect(decoded.header.alg).toBe('HS256');
    expect(decoded.payload).toMatchObject({ sub: 'admin', role: 'admin' });
  });
});

describe('authenticate', () => {
  const mw = authenticate(auth);
  const bearer = (token: string) => ({ headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });

  it('accepts a valid token and exposes the principal', () => {
    const { res, error } = run(mw, bearer(signToken(auth, { soeid: 'admin', role: 'admin' })));
    expect(error).toBeUndefined();
    expect(getPrincipal(res)).toEqual({ soeid: 'admin', role: 'admin' });
  });

  it('treats anything but role=admin as a plain user', () => {
    const token = jwt.sign({ role: 'root' }, auth.secretKey, { subject: 'pp1' });
    expect(getPrincipal(run(mw, bearer(token)).res).role).toBe('user');
  });

  it.each([
    ['no header', {}],
    ['wrong scheme', { headers: { authorization: 'Basic abc' } }],
    ['no token', { headers: { authorization: 'Bearer' } }],
    ['garbage', bearer('not.a.jwt')],
    ['wrong secret', bearer(jwt.sign({}, 'another-secret-another-secret', { subject: 'x' }))],
    ['expired', bearer(jwt.sign({}, auth.secretKey, { subject: 'x', expiresIn: -10 }))],
    ['no subject', bearer(jwt.sign({ role: 'admin' }, auth.secretKey))],
    [
      'alg none',
      bearer(
        `${Buffer.from('{"alg":"none"}').toString('base64url')}.${Buffer.from('{"sub":"x"}').toString('base64url')}.`,
      ),
    ],
    ['other algorithm', bearer(jwt.sign({}, auth.secretKey, { subject: 'x', algorithm: 'HS512' }))],
  ] as const)('rejects %s with 401', (_label, req) => {
    const { error } = run(mw, req as Partial<Request>);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(UnauthorizedError);
  });
});

describe('authorizeSoeid', () => {
  const mw = authorizeSoeid();
  const withPrincipal = (principal: unknown, soeid: string) => {
