    expect(metrics.records.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ eventtype: 'stop', duration: '1 hour 30 minutes' });
  });

  it('404s for an unknown workspace', async () => {
    await expect(service.stop('pp1', 'nope')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
    await expect(service.start('pp1', 'nope')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
  });

  it('records a failed stop', async () => {
    const ws = await service.create(input());
    kube.failOnce('setWorkspaceStarted', new UpstreamError('patch failed'));
    await expect(service.stop('pp1', ws.id)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
    expect(metrics.records.at(-1)?.status).toMatchObject({ failed: true, error: 'patch failed' });
  });
});

describe('delete', () => {
  it('deletes the workspace and records its lifetime', async () => {
    const ws = await service.create(input());
    clock.advance(2 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    await service.delete('pp1', ws.id);
    expect(kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(metrics.records.at(-1)).toMatchObject({
      eventtype: 'delete',
      duration: '2 hours',
      limitcpu: 2,
      storage: 3,
    });
  });

  it('also removes the legacy DevWorkspaceTemplate a workspace contributes', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace(wsNamespace, 'pp1', '1001');
    await kube.create('DevWorkspaceTemplate', wsNamespace, { metadata: { name: 'legacy-template' } });
    await kube.create('DevWorkspace', wsNamespace, {
      metadata: { name: 'legacy' },
      spec: { contributions: [{ name: 'x', kubernetes: { name: 'legacy-template' } }] },
    });
    await service.delete('pp1', 'legacy');
    expect(kube.objects.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it('404s for an unknown workspace', async () => {
    await expect(service.delete('pp1', 'nope')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
  });
});

describe('helpers', () => {
  it('isActive', () => {
    expect(isActive({ status: {} })).toBe(true);
    expect(isActive({})).toBe(true);
    for (const phase of ['Running', 'Starting', 'Stopping'])
      expect(isActive({ status: { phase } })).toBe(true);
    for (const phase of ['Stopped', 'Failed']) expect(isActive({ status: { phase } })).toBe(false);
  });

  it('toWorkspace tolerates a sparse resource', () => {
    expect(toWorkspace({ metadata: { name: 'x', namespace: 'pp1-devspaces-5' } })).toMatchObject({
      id: 'x',
      csiid: '5',
      phase: 'Starting',
      mainUrl: '',
      created: '',
      limitcpu: 0,
    });
  });
});

/tests/unit/utils/daily-logs.test.ts
import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { queryLogs } from '../../../src/config/daily-logs.js';
import { createLogger } from '../../../src/utils/logger.js';

let directory: string;
beforeEach(async () => {
  directory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'workspace-logs-'));
  vi.stubEnv('LOG_DIRECTORY', directory);
});
afterEach(async () => {
  vi.unstubAllEnvs();
  await rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
});
const now = new Date('2026-10-24T12:00:00Z');
describe('daily JSON logs', () => {
  it('rotates by UTC date, redacts secrets, and searches across days', async () => {
    let time = new Date('2026-10-23T13:00:00Z');
    const logger = createLogger(
      'info',
      () => {},
      {},
      { format: 'pretty', dailyFiles: true, now: () => time },
    );
    logger.info('project.created', { token: 'never-return', soeid: 'pp1', requestId: 'request-1' });
    time = new Date('2026-10-24T11:00:00Z');
    logger.error('project.failed', { soeid: 'pp2' });
    const all = await queryLogs({ days: 2, limit: 10 }, now);
    expect(all.entries.map((e) => e.event)).toEqual(['project.failed', 'project.created']);
    expect(await readFile(join(directory, 'app-2026-10-23.log'), 'utf8')).not.toContain('never-return');
    expect(
      (
        await queryLogs(
          { days: 1, limit: 10, level: 'info', soeid: 'pp1', requestId: 'request-1', search: 'CREATED' },
          now,
        )
      ).entries,
    ).toHaveLength(1);
    expect((await queryLogs({ days: 1, limit: 1 }, now)).truncated).toBe(true);
    expect((await queryLogs({ days: 1, limit: 10, requestId: 'missing' }, now)).entries).toHaveLength(0);
    expect((await queryLogs({ days: 1, limit: 10, soeid: 'missing' }, now)).entries).toHaveLength(0);
    expect((await queryLogs({ days: 1, limit: 10, search: 'missing' }, now)).entries).toHaveLength(0);
  });
  it('ignores malformed, old and future entries and absent dates', async () => {
    await writeFile(
      join(directory, 'app-2026-10-24.log'),
      [
        'not-json',
        JSON.stringify({ time: 'invalid' }),
        JSON.stringify({ time: '2026-10-25T00:00:00Z' }),
        JSON.stringify({ time: '2026-10-20T00:00:00Z' }),
      ].join('\n'),
    );
    expect((await queryLogs({ days: 1, limit: 10 }, now)).entries).toEqual([]);
  });
});

/tests/unit/utils/k8s-errors.test.ts
import axios, { AxiosError } from 'axios';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  AppError,
  ConflictError,
  ForbiddenError,
  NotFoundError,
  UpstreamError,
  errorMessage,
} from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';
import { describeError, isK8sStatus, k8sStatus, toAppError } from '../../../src/utils/k8s-errors.js';

describe('k8sStatus', () => {
  it('reads code, statusCode, response.status and body.code', () => {
    expect(k8sStatus({ code: 404 })).toBe(404);
    expect(k8sStatus({ statusCode: 403 })).toBe(403);
    expect(k8sStatus({ response: { status: 409 } })).toBe(409);
    expect(k8sStatus({ body: { code: 500 } })).toBe(500);
    expect(k8sStatus({ body: JSON.stringify({ code: 422 }) })).toBe(422);
  });
  it('is undefined for non-http errors', () => {
    expect(k8sStatus(new Error('x'))).toBeUndefined();
    expect(k8sStatus(null)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(k8sStatus('boom')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(k8sStatus({ body: 'not json' })).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it('isK8sStatus compares', () => {
    expect(isK8sStatus({ code: 404 }, 404)).toBe(true);
    expect(isK8sStatus({ code: 404 }, 403)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('describeError', () => {
  it('describes kubernetes api errors with the server message', () => {
    expect(describeError('ctx', { code: 422, body: { message: 'invalid spec' } })).toBe(
      'ctx: Kubernetes API error (422) invalid spec',
    );
  });
  it('describes 404 plainly', () => {
    expect(describeError('ctx', { code: 404 })).toBe('ctx: Resource not found (404)');
  });
  it('describes axios errors', () => {
    const withResponse = new AxiosError('boom', 'ERR', undefined, {}, { status: 503, data: {} } as never);
    expect(describeError('ctx', withResponse)).toBe('ctx: HTTP 503 boom');
    const noResponse = new AxiosError('down', 'ECONNREFUSED', undefined, {});
    expect(describeError('ctx', noResponse)).toBe('ctx: No response received from server');
    const bare = new AxiosError('odd');
    expect(axios.isAxiosError(bare)).toBe(true);
    expect(describeError('ctx', bare)).toBe('ctx: Axios error odd');
  });
  it('flags TLS problems', () => {
    expect(describeError('ctx', new Error('self signed certificate in chain'))).toBe(
      'ctx: TLS/SSL certificate error',
    );
  });
  it('falls back to the message or string form', () => {
    expect(describeError('ctx', new Error('plain'))).toBe('ctx: plain');
    expect(describeError('ctx', 'text')).toBe('ctx: text');
  });
});

describe('toAppError', () => {
  it('maps statuses to typed errors and keeps the cause', () => {
    const cause = { code: 404 };
    const notFound = toAppError('ctx', cause);
    expect(notFound).toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
    expect((notFound as { cause?: unknown }).cause).toBe(cause);
    expect(toAppError('ctx', { code: 403 })).toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
    expect(toAppError('ctx', { code: 409 })).toBeInstanceOf(ConflictError);
    const upstream = toAppError('ctx', { code: 500 });
    expect(upstream).toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
    expect(upstream.status).toBe(502);
  });
  it('passes AppErrors through untouched', () => {
    const original = new ConflictError('x');
    expect(toAppError('ctx', original)).toBe(original);
  });
  it('wraps unknown failures as upstream errors', () => {
    expect(toAppError('ctx', new Error('weird'))).toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
  });
});

describe('errors', () => {
  it('carry status, code and class name', () => {
    const e = new NotFoundError('nope');
    expect(e).toBeInstanceOf(AppError);
    expect([e.status, e.code, e.name]).toEqual([404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'NotFoundError']);
  });
  it('errorMessage handles non-errors', () => {
    expect(errorMessage(new Error('a'))).toBe('a');
    expect(errorMessage(42)).toBe('42');
  });
});

/tests/unit/utils/log-format.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  COLORS,
  RESET,
  formatLocalTime,
  formatPretty,
  type LogEntry,
  type LogKind,
} from '../../../src/utils/log-format.js';

const time = new Date(2026, 0, 2, 3, 4, 5, 678); // local time, as the formatter prints it
const T = '[2026-01-02 03:04:05] [File: unknown] [Function: anonymous]';
const entry = (kind: LogKind, event: string, fields: Record<string, unknown> = {}): LogEntry => ({
  time,
  kind,
  event,
  fields,
});
const plain = (e: LogEntry) => formatPretty(e, false);

describe('colours: errors red, warnings orange, successes green, everything else white', () => {
  it('uses exactly these ANSI codes', () => {
    expect(COLORS.error).toBe('\u001b[31m'); // red
    expect(COLORS.warn).toBe('\u001b[38;5;208m'); // orange
    expect(COLORS.success).toBe('\u001b[32m'); // green
    expect(COLORS.info).toBe('\u001b[97m'); // bright white
    expect(COLORS.debug).toBe(COLORS.info); // white as well
  });

  it.each(['error', 'warn', 'success', 'info', 'debug'] as const)(
    '%s line is painted in its colour',
    (kind) => {
      const out = formatPretty(entry(kind, 'some.event', { a: 1 }), true);
      expect(out.startsWith(COLORS[kind])).toBe(true);
      expect(out.endsWith(RESET)).toBe(true);
    },
  );

  it('every line of a multi-line entry is painted on its own, the separating blank line is not', () => {
    const out = formatPretty(entry('error', 'boom', { a: { b: 1 }, c: 2 }), true);
    const lines = out.split('\n');
    expect(lines.length).toBeGreaterThan(3);
    expect(lines.at(-1)).toBe('');
    for (const line of lines.slice(0, -1)) {
      expect(line.startsWith(COLORS.error), line).toBe(true);
      expect(line.endsWith(RESET), line).toBe(true);
      expect(line.slice(COLORS.error.length, -RESET.length)).not.toContain('\u001b');
    }
  });

  it('prints no escape codes at all when colour is off', () => {
    for (const kind of ['error', 'warn', 'success', 'info', 'debug'] as const) {
      expect(plain(entry(kind, 'e', { a: { b: 1 } }))).not.toContain('\u001b');
    }
  });
});

describe('layout', () => {
  it('formats local time as YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss', () => {
    expect(formatLocalTime(time)).toBe('2026-01-02 03:04:05');
    expect(formatLocalTime(new Date(2026, 10, 9, 23, 59, 1, 5))).toBe('2026-11-09 23:59:01');
  });

  it('a bare event is one line: time | LEVEL | event', () => {
    expect(plain(entry('info', 'server.shutdown'))).toBe(`${T} | INFO  | server.shutdown`);
  });

  it('level badges all have the same width so the columns line up', () => {
    const widths = (['error', 'warn', 'success', 'info', 'debug'] as const).map((k) =>
      plain(entry(k, 'e')).indexOf(' | e'),
    );
    expect(new Set(widths).size).toBe(1);
    expect(plain(entry('error', 'e'))).toContain('| ERROR |');
    expect(plain(entry('warn', 'e'))).toContain('| WARN  |');
    expect(plain(entry('success', 'e'))).toContain('| OK    |');
    expect(plain(entry('debug', 'e'))).toContain('| DEBUG |');
  });

  it('a few short fields stay on the same line, as key=value separated by two spaces', () => {
    expect(plain(entry('warn', 'metrics.columns_missing', { table: 't1', missing: 'a,b' }))).toBe(
      `${T} | WARN  | ${'metrics.columns_missing'.padEnd(30)} | table=t1  missing=a,b`,
    );
  });

  it('quotes inline values that contain spaces or =, and escapes newlines', () => {
    const out = plain(
      entry('error', 'x', { error: 'cluster is down', eq: 'a=b', multi: 'l1\nl2', bare: 'ok-1' }),
    );
    expect(out).toContain('error="cluster is down"');
    expect(out).toContain('eq="a=b"');
    expect(out).toContain('multi=l1\\nl2'); // the newline becomes a visible \n, so the entry stays on one line
    expect(out).toContain('bare=ok-1');
    expect(out.split('\n')).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('shows the per-request line as: METHOD path -> status (ms) plus the ids', () => {
    const out = plain(
      entry('success', 'request', {
        method: 'GET',
        path: '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces',
        status: 200,
        ms: 12,
        requestId: 'r-1',
        soeid: 'pp1',
      }),
    );
    expect(out).toBe(
      `${T} | OK    | ${'request'.padEnd(30)} | GET    /api/v1/pp1/workspaces -> 200 (12 ms)  requestId=r-1  soeid=pp1`,
    );
  });

  it('leaves out fields that have no value (a request without a user)', () => {
    const out = plain(
      entry('success', 'request', {
        method: 'GET',
        path: '/api/v1/healthz',
        status: 200,
        ms: 1,
        soeid: undefined,
      }),
    );
    expect(out).not.toContain('soeid');
    expect(out).not.toContain('undefined');
  });

  it('puts nested and long data on indented lines with aligned colons, and ends with a blank line', () => {
    const out = plain(
      entry('success', 'server.listening', {
        port: 8080,
        cluster: 'https://x',
        editors: { vscode: 'a', intellij: 'b' },
        postgres: 'disabled',
        empty: {},
        list: [1, 2],
        gone: undefined,
      }),
    );
    expect(out).toBe(
      [
        `${T} | OK    | server.listening`,
        `${T}     port     : 8080`,
        `${T}     cluster  : https://x`,
        `${T}     editors:`,
        `${T}       vscode   : a`,
        `${T}       intellij : b`,
        `${T}     postgres : disabled`,
        `${T}     empty    : {}`,
        `${T}     list     : [1,2]`,
        '',
      ].join('\n'),
    );
  });

  it('switches to the indented layout for many fields, or a long value', () => {
    const many = plain(entry('info', 'e', { a: 1, b: 2, c: 3, d: 4, e: 5, f: 6, g: 7 }));
    expect(many.split('\n')[0]).toBe(`${T} | INFO  | e`);
    expect(many).toContain('    g : 7');

    const long = plain(entry('error', 'e', { error: 'x'.repeat(100) }));
    expect(long.split('\n')[0]).toBe(`${T} | ERROR | e`);
    expect(long).toContain(`    error : ${'x'.repeat(100)}`);
  });

  it('an error with a request id and a message gets its own block, easy to read', () => {
    const out = plain(
      entry('error', 'request.failed', {
        requestId: '7989eb27-2d84-4d15-aa0b-95003ff51f85',
        code: 'UPSTREAM_ERROR',
        error: 'Devfile registry catalog: No response received from server',
      }),
    );
    expect(out).toBe(
      [
        `${T} | ERROR | request.failed`,
        `${T}     requestId : 7989eb27-2d84-4d15-aa0b-95003ff51f85`,
        `${T}     code      : UPSTREAM_ERROR`,
        `${T}     error     : Devfile registry catalog: No response received from server`,
        '',
      ].join('\n'),
    );
  });

  it('treats a "request" event without method/path/status as an ordinary entry', () => {
    expect(plain(entry('info', 'request', { note: 'hi' }))).toBe(
      `${T} | INFO  | ${'request'.padEnd(30)} | note=hi`,
    );
  });
});

/tests/unit/utils/logger.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createLogger, redact } from '../../../src/utils/logger.js';

function capture(level: Parameters<typeof createLogger>[0] = 'info') {
  const lines: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
  return { lines, logger: createLogger(level, (l) => lines.push(JSON.parse(l))) };
}

describe('logger', () => {
  it('records the calling file and function with a seconds-resolution timestamp', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture();
    function recordEvent() {
      logger.info('source.test');
    }
    recordEvent();
    expect(lines[0]).toMatchObject({ file: 'logger.test.ts', function: 'recordEvent' });
    expect(lines[0]?.timestamp).toMatch(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2} \d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}$/);
  });
  it('uses an error stack to identify its original file and function', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture();
    const error = new Error('creation failed');
    error.stack =
      'Error: creation failed\n    at NamespaceService.ensure (C:\\app\\src\\services\\NamespaceService.ts:42:10)';
    logger.error('namespace.failed', { error });
    expect(lines[0]).toMatchObject({
      file: 'NamespaceService.ts',
      function: 'NamespaceService.ensure',
      error: 'creation failed',
    });
    expect(JSON.stringify(lines[0])).not.toContain('C:\\app');
  });
  it('writes one JSON line with level, event and fields', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture();
    logger.info('hello', { a: 1 });
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(lines[0]).toMatchObject({ level: 'info', event: 'hello', a: 1 });
    expect(typeof lines[0]?.time).toBe('string');
  });

  it('filters below the configured level', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture('warn');
    logger.debug('d');
    logger.info('i');
    logger.warn('w');
    logger.error('e');
    expect(lines.map((l) => l.event)).toEqual(['w', 'e']);
  });

  it('is silent when asked', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture('silent');
    logger.error('e');
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('child loggers carry bindings', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture();
    logger.child({ requestId: 'r1' }).info('x', { b: 2 });
    expect(lines[0]).toMatchObject({ requestId: 'r1', b: 2 });
  });

  it('redacts sensitive keys, nested and in arrays', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture();
    logger.info('x', {
      token: 'ghp_secret',
      nested: { password: 'p', ok: 1 },
      list: [{ authorization: 'Bearer x' }],
    });
    expect(lines[0]).toMatchObject({
      token: '[redacted]',
      nested: { password: '[redacted]', ok: 1 },
      list: [{ authorization: '[redacted]' }],
    });
    expect(JSON.stringify(lines[0])).not.toContain('ghp_secret');
  });

  it('success is an info-level line marked as a success, and hidden when only warnings are wanted', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture();
    logger.success('workspace.created', { workspace: 'w1' });
    expect(lines[0]).toMatchObject({
      level: 'info',
      outcome: 'success',
      event: 'workspace.created',
      workspace: 'w1',
    });
    const quiet = capture('warn');
    quiet.logger.success('workspace.created');
    expect(quiet.lines).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('plain info lines carry no outcome', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture();
    logger.info('x');
    expect(lines[0]).not.toHaveProperty('outcome');
  });

  describe('pretty format (terminal)', () => {
    const now = () => new Date(2026, 0, 2, 3, 4, 5, 678);
    const pretty = (level: Parameters<typeof createLogger>[0] = 'info', color = true) => {
      const out: string[] = [];
      return {
        out,
        logger: createLogger(
          level,
          (l) => out.push(l),
          {},
          { format: 'pretty', color, now, source: { file: 'logger.test.ts', function: 'testLog' } },
        ),
      };
    };

    it('paints each kind in its colour: error red, warn orange, success green, info and debug white', () => {
      const { out, logger } = pretty('debug');
      logger.error('e');
      logger.warn('w');
      logger.success('s');
      logger.info('i');
      logger.debug('d');
      expect(out).toEqual([
        '\u001b[31m[2026-01-02 03:04:05] [File: logger.test.ts] [Function: testLog] | ERROR | e\u001b[0m',
        '\u001b[38;5;208m[2026-01-02 03:04:05] [File: logger.test.ts] [Function: testLog] | WARN  | w\u001b[0m',
        '\u001b[32m[2026-01-02 03:04:05] [File: logger.test.ts] [Function: testLog] | OK    | s\u001b[0m',
        '\u001b[97m[2026-01-02 03:04:05] [File: logger.test.ts] [Function: testLog] | INFO  | i\u001b[0m',
        '\u001b[97m[2026-01-02 03:04:05] [File: logger.test.ts] [Function: testLog] | DEBUG | d\u001b[0m',
      ]);
    });

    it('writes one sink call per entry, plain when colour is off', () => {
      const { out, logger } = pretty('info', false);
      logger.info('a', { n: 1 });
      logger.warn('b');
      expect(out).toEqual([
        `[2026-01-02 03:04:05] [File: logger.test.ts] [Function: testLog] | INFO  | ${'a'.padEnd(30)} | n=1`,
        '[2026-01-02 03:04:05] [File: logger.test.ts] [Function: testLog] | WARN  | b',
      ]);
    });

    it('applies the level filter, bindings of child loggers, and redaction', () => {
      const { out, logger } = pretty('info', false);
      logger.debug('hidden');
      logger.child({ requestId: 'r1' }).info('x', { token: 'ghp_secret', ok: 1 });
      expect(out).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(out[0]).toContain('requestId=r1');
      expect(out[0]).toContain('token=[redacted]');
      expect(out[0]).not.toContain('ghp_secret');
    });

    it('child loggers keep the format and colour of their parent', () => {
      const { out, logger } = pretty('info', true);
      logger.child({ a: 1 }).error('boom');
      expect(out[0]?.startsWith('\u001b[31m')).toBe(true);
    });

    it('the default is still JSON, so existing log collectors keep working', () => {
      const lines: string[] = [];
      createLogger('info', (l) => lines.push(l)).info('x');
      expect(JSON.parse(lines[0] as string)).toMatchObject({ event: 'x', level: 'info' });
    });
  });

  it('serialises errors as name and message only', () => {
    expect(redact(new Error('bad'))).toEqual({ name: 'Error', message: 'bad' });
  });

  it('stops redacting past a sane depth', () => {
    const deep = { a: { b: { c: { d: { e: { f: { g: 1 } } } } } } };
    expect(redact(deep)).toEqual(deep);
  });
});

/tests/unit/utils/names.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  cleanWorkspaceName,
  csiidFromDeploymentNamespace,
  csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace,
  deploymentNamespace,
  extractClusterName,
  parseCsiid,
  parseResourceName,
  parseSoeid,
  parseTokenName,
  randomSuffix,
  workspaceNamespace,
} from '../../../src/utils/names.js';
import { BadRequestError } from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';

describe('parseSoeid', () => {
  it('lower-cases and trims', () => expect(parseSoeid(' PP58386 ')).toBe('pp58386'));
  it.each(['', 'ab', 'a b', 'a/b', '../etc', 'x'.repeat(21), 'user;rm', 'üser'])('rejects %j', (value) => {
    expect(() => parseSoeid(value)).toThrow(BadRequestError);
  });
});

describe('parseCsiid', () => {
  it('accepts digits', () => expect(parseCsiid('177398')).toBe('177398'));
  it.each(['', 'abc', '12a', '-1', '12345678901'])('rejects %j', (value) => {
    expect(() => parseCsiid(value)).toThrow(BadRequestError);
  });
});

describe('parseResourceName / parseTokenName', () => {
  it('accepts DNS-1123 labels', () => {
    expect(parseResourceName('Java-Maven-ab12')).toBe('java-maven-ab12');
    expect(parseTokenName('My-Token')).toBe('my-token');
  });
  it.each(['-bad', 'bad-', 'has space', 'UPPER_CASE', 'x'.repeat(64)])('rejects %j', (value) => {
    expect(() => parseResourceName(value)).toThrow(BadRequestError);
  });
  it('rejects bad token names', () => {
    expect(() => parseTokenName('a/b')).toThrow(BadRequestError);
    expect(() => parseTokenName('')).toThrow(BadRequestError);
  });
});

describe('namespace helpers', () => {
  it('builds and parses workspace namespaces', () => {
    expect(workspaceNamespace('pp58386', '177398')).toBe('pp58386-devspaces-177398');
    expect(csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace('pp58386-devspaces-177398')).toBe('177398');
    expect(csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace('pp58386-devspaces-deploy-177398')).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it('builds and parses deployment namespaces', () => {
    expect(deploymentNamespace('pp58386', '177398')).toBe('pp58386-devspaces-deploy-177398');
    expect(csiidFromDeploymentNamespace('pp58386-devspaces-deploy-177398')).toBe('177398');
    expect(csiidFromDeploymentNamespace('pp58386-devspaces-177398')).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('extractClusterName', () => {
  it('reads the cluster from api and apps URLs', () => {
    expect(extractClusterName('https://api.namctiswd004d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443')).toBe('namctiswd004d');
    expect(extractClusterName('https://console-openshift-console.apps.prod1.ecs.dyn.example.net/')).toBe(
      'prod1',
    );
  });
  it('falls back for anything else', () => {
    expect(extractClusterName('http://localhost')).toBe('unknown-cluster');
    expect(extractClusterName(undefined)).toBe('unknown-cluster');
  });
});

describe('cleanWorkspaceName', () => {
  it('appends the suffix', () => expect(cleanWorkspaceName('java-maven', 'ab12')).toBe('java-maven-ab12'));
  it('sanitises to DNS-1123', () => {
    expect(cleanWorkspaceName('Java_Maven 17!!', 'ab12')).toBe('java-maven-17-ab12');
    expect(cleanWorkspaceName('---', 'ab12')).toBe('workspace-ab12');
  });
  it('never exceeds 63 characters', () => {
    expect(cleanWorkspaceName('x'.repeat(200), 'ab12').length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(63);
  });
  it('generates 4-hex-char suffixes by default', () => {
    expect(cleanWorkspaceName('py')).toMatch(/^py-[0-9a-f]{4}$/);
    expect(randomSuffix()).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{4}$/);
  });
});

/tests/unit/utils/resources.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { parseCpu, parseGi, summarizeComponents } from '../../../src/utils/resources.js';

describe('parseCpu', () => {
  it.each([
    ['500m', 0.5],
    ['3500m', 3.5],
    ['2', 2],
    ['0.25', 0.25],
    [2, 2],
    ['', 0],
    [undefined, 0],
    [null, 0],
    ['abc', 0],
  ])('%j → %s cores', (input, expected) => {
    expect(parseCpu(input as string | number | undefined | null)).toBe(expected);
  });
});

describe('parseGi', () => {
  it.each([
    ['1Gi', 1],
    ['512Mi', 0.5],
    ['1024Ki', 0.001],
    ['1Ti', 1024],
    ['1G', 0.931],
    ['2147483648', 2],
    ['', 0],
    ['nonsense', 0],
    ['5Zz', 0],
    [undefined, 0],
  ])('%j → %s GiB', (input, expected) => {
    expect(parseGi(input as string | undefined)).toBeCloseTo(expected, 3);
  });
});

describe('summarizeComponents', () => {
  const components = [
    {
      name: 'tools',
      container: { cpuLimit: '2000m', cpuRequest: '500m', memoryLimit: '4Gi', memoryRequest: '1Gi' },
    },
    { name: 'db', container: { cpuLimit: '1', memoryLimit: '512Mi' } },
    { name: 'checode', container: { cpuLimit: '9', memoryLimit: '9Gi' }, volume: { size: '9Gi' } },
    { name: 'venv', volume: { size: '1Gi' } },
    { name: 'home', volume: { size: '2Gi' } },
  ];

  it('sums containers and volumes, excluding the editor component', () => {
    expect(summarizeComponents(components)).toEqual({
      limitcpu: 3,
      requestcpu: 0.5,
      limitmemory: 4.5,
      requestmemory: 1,
      storage: 3,
    });
  });

  it('honours a custom exclusion list', () => {
    expect(summarizeComponents(components, ['checode', 'db']).limitcpu).toBe(2);
  });

  it('returns zeros for nothing', () => {
    expect(summarizeComponents(undefined)).toEqual({
      limitcpu: 0,
      limitmemory: 0,
      requestcpu: 0,
      requestmemory: 0,
      storage: 0,
    });
  });
});

/tests/unit/utils/timestamp.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { durationSince, formatDuration, getFormattedTimestampCST } from '../../../src/utils/timestamp.js';

describe('getFormattedTimestampCST', () => {
  it('formats in America/Chicago as MM-DD-YY HH:mm:ss CST (winter, UTC-6)', () => {
    expect(getFormattedTimestampCST(new Date('2026-01-15T18:30:05Z'))).toBe('01-15-26 12:30:05 CST');
  });

  it('follows daylight saving time (summer, UTC-5)', () => {
    expect(getFormattedTimestampCST(new Date('2026-07-04T18:30:05Z'))).toBe('07-04-26 13:30:05 CST');
  });

  it('uses 00 rather than 24 for midnight', () => {
    expect(getFormattedTimestampCST(new Date('2026-01-15T06:00:00Z'))).toBe('01-15-26 00:00:00 CST');
  });
});

describe('formatDuration', () => {
  it.each([
    [0, '0 seconds'],
    [1, '1 second'],
    [59, '59 seconds'],
    [60, '1 minute'],
    [61, '1 minute 1 second'],
    [185, '3 minutes 5 seconds'],
    [3600, '1 hour'],
    [3660, '1 hour 1 minute'],
    [7500, '2 hours 5 minutes'],
    [-5, '0 seconds'],
    [59.9, '59 seconds'],
  ])('%s s → %s', (seconds, expected) => {
    expect(formatDuration(seconds)).toBe(expected);
  });
});

describe('durationSince', () => {
  const now = new Date('2026-01-15T12:00:00Z');
  it('measures from a date or an ISO string', () => {
    expect(durationSince(new Date('2026-01-15T11:58:00Z'), now)).toBe('2 minutes');
    expect(durationSince('2026-01-15T10:00:00Z', now)).toBe('2 hours');
  });
  it('returns 0 seconds when the start is missing or invalid', () => {
    expect(durationSince(undefined, now)).toBe('0 seconds');
    expect(durationSince('not a date', now)).toBe('0 seconds');
  });
});
